# 六爻 · 疾病 · 神祟

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Illness · Spirits and curses: prayer, vows and what to sacrifice*

64 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.ILL.BSZZ.37 何知章·怪与死：多梦寐（螣蛇带鬼持世）、出鬼怪（螣蛇白虎临门）、人投水（玄武入水煞临鬼）、有吊颈（螣蛇木鬼临世爻）、孝服来（白虎临鬼；大全作丧门吊客临鬼）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:怎么知道这家的人多做噩梦：螣蛇带着鬼来持世；出鬼怪：螣蛇、白虎临在门上；有人投水：玄武入水，煞神临在鬼上；有人上吊：螣蛇、木鬼落在世爻上；要有孝服（丧事）来：卜筮正宗说是白虎交重（发动）、排在鬼上，卜筮大全说是丧门、吊客两颗星排在鬼上。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** How to know that a household is troubled by dreams: the Coiling Snake carrying the ghost holds the Self position; that ghosts and strange things appear: the Coiling Snake and White Tiger sit at the door; that someone will throw himself into water: the Dark Tortoise enters Water and a killing spirit sits on the ghost; that someone will hang himself: the Coiling Snake and a Wood ghost sit on the Self line; that mourning dress will come: in the Bushi Zhengzong the White Tiger moves and is set upon the ghost, in the Bushi Daquan the stars Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence are set upon the ghost.

**《卜筮正宗·赋文三篇·何知章》原文** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3570 行 · *Bushi Zhengzong (The Orthodox Line of Divination), "Three rhapsodies: the "how to know" rhyme", base text; Bushi Zhengzong, line 3570*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何知人家多梦寐？ | 怎么知道这家的人多梦寐（做噩梦）？ | How can one know that a household is troubled by dreams? |
| 螣蛇带鬼来持世； | 螣蛇带着鬼来持世； | the Coiling Snake carrying the ghost holds the Self position; |
| 何知人家出鬼怪？ | 怎么知道这家出鬼怪？ | How can one know that ghosts and strange things appear in a household? |
| 螣蛇白虎临门在； | 螣蛇、白虎临在门上； | the Coiling Snake and the White Tiger sit at the door; |
| 何知人家人投水？ | 怎么知道这家有人投水？ | How can one know that someone in a household will throw himself into water? |
| 玄武入水煞临鬼； | 玄武入水，煞神临在鬼上； | the Dark Tortoise enters Water and a killing spirit sits on the ghost; |
| 何知人家有吊颈？ | 怎么知道这家有人上吊？ | How can one know that someone in a household will hang himself? |
| 螣蛇木鬼世爻临； | 螣蛇、木鬼落在世爻上； | the Coiling Snake and a Wood ghost sit on the Self line; |
| 何知人家孝服来？ | 怎么知道这家会有孝服（丧事）来？ | How can one know that mourning dress will come to a household? |
| 交重白虎临鬼排； | 白虎交重（发动），排在鬼上； | the White Tiger moving, set upon the ghost; |

**《卜筮正宗·赋文三篇·何知章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9845 行 · *Bushi Zhengzong (The Orthodox Line of Divination), "Three rhapsodies: the "how to know" rhyme", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9845*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何知人家多梦寐？ | 怎么知道这家的人多梦寐（做噩梦）？ | How can one know that a household is troubled by dreams? |
| 鰧蛇带鬼来持世。 | 螣蛇带着鬼来持世。 | The Coiling Snake carrying the ghost holds the Self position. |
| 何知人家出鬼怪？ | 怎么知道这家出鬼怪？ | How can one know that ghosts and strange things appear in a household? |
| 鰧蛇白虎临门在。 | 螣蛇、白虎临在门上。 | The Coiling Snake and the White Tiger sit at the door. |
| 何知人家人投水？ | 怎么知道这家有人投水？ | How can one know that someone in a household will throw himself into water? |
| 玄武入水杀临鬼。 | 玄武入水，煞神临在鬼上。 | The Dark Tortoise enters Water and a killing spirit sits on the ghost. |
| 何知人家有吊颈？ | 怎么知道这家有人上吊？ | How can one know that someone in a household will hang himself? |
| 鰧蛇木鬼世爻临。 | 螣蛇、木鬼落在世爻上。 | The Coiling Snake and a Wood ghost sit on the Self line. |
| 何知人家孝服来？ | 怎么知道这家会有孝服（丧事）来？ | How can one know that mourning dress will come to a household? |
| 丧门吊客临鬼排。 | 丧门、吊客两颗星排在鬼上。 | The stars Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence are set upon the ghost. |

> **打架**:孝服来（有丧事）看什么：卜筮正宗答「交重白虎临鬼排」，白虎发动排在鬼爻上；卜筮大全答「丧门吊客临鬼排」，丧门、吊客两颗神煞排在鬼爻上。一个用六神白虎，一个用神煞丧门吊客，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What tells that mourning is coming: the Bushi Zhengzong answers 'the White Tiger moving, set upon the ghost', the White Tiger of the six spirits moving on the Pressure line; the Bushi Daquan answers 'Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence set upon the ghost', two spirit-stars on the Pressure line. One uses the White Tiger among the six spirits and the other the spirit-stars Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence, and the books do not decide.

> **按**:「何知章」一问一答，一共问了三四十件事（父母子孙的病、有没有子孙、讼事、财富、田地、屋宇、坟墓、六畜、口舌、盗贼……），都是一家的事；这里只收与病、死、鬼怪有关的。卜筮大全里它是「十六、何知章」，卜筮正宗里它在《赋文三篇》中；两本字句略有出入。「梦寐」是睡着做梦，这里是不好的梦；「吊颈」是上吊；「孝服」是丧服，指家里有丧事。丧门、吊客是两颗神煞，书里没有再解释。
>
> **Note:** The 'how to know' rhyme asks and answers some thirty or forty things (illnesses of parents and children, whether there are children, lawsuits, wealth, land, houses, graves, livestock, quarrels, thieves and more), all affairs of a household; only those to do with illness, death and ghostly happenings are taken here. In the Bushi Daquan it is chapter 16, 'the how-to-know rhyme'; in the Bushi Zhengzong it is among the three rhapsodies; the two texts differ a little in wording. 'Mengmei' is dreaming in sleep, here bad dreams; 'diaojing' is hanging oneself; 'mourning dress' (xiaofu) means a death in the family. Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence are two spirit-stars that the book does not explain further.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 9845 行:何知人家多梦寐;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9845 行:鰧蛇带鬼来持世;同文 第 9845 行:何知人家出鬼怪;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9845 行:鰧蛇白虎临门在;同文 第 9845 行:何知人家人投水;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9845 行:玄武入水杀临鬼;同文 第 9845 行:何知人家有吊颈;异文(相似 0.86) 第 9845 行:鰧蛇木鬼世爻临;同文 第 9845 行:何知人家孝服来
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3570 行:何知人家多梦寐;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3570 行:螣蛇带鬼来持世;同文 第 3570 行:何知人家出鬼怪;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3570 行:螣蛇白虎临门在;同文 第 3570 行:何知人家人投水;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3570 行:玄武入水煞临鬼;同文 第 3570 行:何知人家有吊颈;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3570 行:螣蛇木鬼世爻临;同文 第 3570 行:何知人家孝服来

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.118、LY.ILL.BSZZ.34、LY.ILL.BSZZ.36

## LY.ILL.HJC.50 日辰带鬼急为祈祷：日辰带鬼生合世爻，看世爻带的是哪一亲就知道该用什么治（父母经箓符醮、子孙用药、兄弟戒怒节食、财静养、官鬼祈祷）；认哪路神鬼，旧注按日辰地支、王洪绪按生合的六神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:日辰带着鬼、来生合世爻，就该祈祷。看世爻带的是哪一亲，就知道该用什么办法治：带父母，要靠经、箓、符、醮来保扶；带子孙，要用药调理；带兄弟，要戒暴怒、节制饮食；带财爻，要好好休养；带官鬼，要祈祷鬼神。要知道该求哪一路神鬼，两家的办法不同，见分歧。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Day carries Pressure and generates or combines with the Self line, one should pray. What the Self line carries shows the cure: Resource, scriptures, registers, talismans and rites; Output, medicine; Peer, curbing temper and moderating food; Wealth, good rest; Pressure, prayer to the ghosts and spirits. Which spirit or ghost to seek is found differently by the two commentaries (see the dispute).

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6940 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6940*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰带鬼，急为祈祷保无虞。 | 日辰上带着鬼，赶紧祈祷，保证没有忧患。 | The Day carrying Pressure: hurry to pray, and there will be nothing to fear. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6941–6942 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6941–6942*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日辰带鬼，生合世爻，世爻看带何等神，便知得何者为治。 | 日辰带着鬼，来生、合世爻，看世爻带的是哪一类神，就知道该用什么办法治。 | When the Day carries Pressure and generates or combines with the Self line, look at what sort of spirit the Self line carries, and one knows what is the cure. |
| 如带父母，必须经箓符醮保扶则愈。 | 如果带父母，一定要靠经、箓、符、醮来保扶才好。 | If it carries Resource, it will heal only with scriptures, registers, talismans and jiao offerings to protect and sustain. |
| 如带子孙，必须用药调理。 | 如果带子孙，一定要用药调理。 | If it carries Output, medicine is needed to regulate it. |
| 如带兄弟，必须戒暴怒，节饮食。 | 如果带兄弟，一定要戒暴怒，节制饮食。 | If it carries Peer, he must give up violent anger and moderate his food and drink. |
| 如带财爻，必须好好将息。 | 如果带财爻，一定要好好休养。 | If it carries Wealth, he must take good rest. |
| 带官鬼，祈祷鬼神。 | 带官鬼，就祈祷鬼神。 | If it carries Pressure, he should pray to the ghosts and spirits. |
| 世持鬼，亦宜先祷。 | 世爻持着鬼，也宜先祈祷。 | When the Self line itself holds Pressure, one should also pray first. |
| 但世鬼是先曾许下，日辰鬼是未曾许下。 | 不过世爻上的鬼是以前许过愿的，日辰上的鬼是没有许过愿的。 | But Pressure on the Self line is a vow made before, and Pressure on the Day is a vow not yet made. |
| 要知求何神鬼，有福力，以日辰推之。 | 要知道该求哪一路神鬼才有福力，用日辰来推。 | To know which spirit or ghost to beg, so that there is blessing and power, reason it from the Day. |
| 如子为北斗或北阴； | 比如子是北斗，或者北阴； | For example, Zi is the Northern Dipper or the North Yin; |
| 亥或溺死鬼，察其为河伯，或划龙船。 | 亥是溺死鬼之类，要察看是河伯，还是划龙船的。 | Hai is a drowned ghost or the like: look into whether it is the River Earl or the rowing of dragon boats. |
| 盖亥水中有甲木，故也。 | 因为亥水里藏着甲木。 | For within the Hai Water there is Jia Wood. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2398 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2398*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如日辰带官鬼生合世爻或用爻者，当为祈祷，看其生合者是何等神。 | 如果日辰带着官鬼、来生合世爻或者用爻，就该去祈祷，看生合它的是哪一路神。 | If the Day carries Pressure and generates or combines with the Self or Subject line, one should pray, and look at what sort of spirit it is that generates or combines with it. |
| 如生合青龙父母，是花幡香愿，勾陈则土地城隍，朱雀则香灯口愿，螣蛇则百怪惊神，白虎则伤司五道，玄武则玄帝北阴。 | 如果生合它的是青龙、父母，是花幡香愿；勾陈是土地、城隍；朱雀是香灯口愿；螣蛇是百怪惊神；白虎是伤司、五道；玄武是玄帝、北阴。 | If what generates or combines with it is the Azure Dragon and Resource, it is a vow of flower banners and incense; Hook Earth means the Earth God and the City God; the Vermilion Bird a vow of incense and lamps spoken aloud; the Coiling Snake the hundred oddities and startled spirits; the White Tiger the Office of Injury and the Five Ways; the Dark Tortoise the Dark Emperor and the North Yin. |
| 阳象阳爻是神，阴象阴爻是鬼。 | 阳的象、阳的爻是神，阴的象、阴的爻是鬼。 | A yang image on a yang line is a god; a yin image on a yin line is a ghost. |
| 今陈大略，后有《鬼神章》尽细，阅之照断方是，切不可妄断！ | 现在只讲个大概，后面有《鬼神章》讲得详尽，读了照着断才对，千万不可乱断！ | Only the outline is set out now; the later Chapter on Ghosts and Spirits is fully detailed, and one should read it and judge by it; on no account judge at random! |

> **打架**:怎样认该求哪一路神鬼：旧注用日辰的地支推（子是北斗或北阴，亥是溺死鬼，要察看是河伯、还是划龙船的），再按世爻带的六亲定治法；王洪绪看生合世爻或用爻的那一爻带的六神（青龙、勾陈、朱雀、螣蛇、白虎、玄武各是一路），并且说阳象阳爻是神、阴象阴爻是鬼。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How to tell which spirit or ghost to seek: the old commentary reasons from the branch of the Day (Zi is the Northern Dipper or the North Yin, Hai a drowned ghost, to be looked into as the River Earl or dragon-boat rowing) and fixes the cure by the relation the Self line carries; Wang Hongxu looks at the six spirits carried by the line that generates or combines with the Self or Subject line (the Azure Dragon, Hook Earth, Vermilion Bird, Coiling Snake, White Tiger and Dark Tortoise each a different sort), and says that a yang image on a yang line is a god and a yin image on a yin line a ghost.

> **按**:「箓」是道士画的符箓，「醮」是道士设坛祭神的仪式，「保扶」是保护、扶助。「花幡香愿」「香灯口愿」是向神许过愿、还愿时献的花幡、香灯。「伤司」「五道」是主伤亡、管冥途的神，「玄帝」是玄天上帝，「北阴」是北方的阴神。亥里藏着壬水和甲木，所以旧注说除了河伯（水），还要看有没有划龙船的（木船）。王洪绪说的《鬼神章》是本书后面专讲鬼神的一章。
>
> **Note:** A 'lu' is a register or talisman drawn by a Daoist, a 'jiao' a rite in which a Daoist sets up an altar and offers to the spirits, and 'protect and sustain' is to protect and support. 'A vow of flower banners and incense' and 'a vow of incense and lamps spoken aloud' are vows made to a god and the flower banners, incense and lamps offered in fulfilling them. 'The Office of Injury' and 'the Five Ways' are gods over injury and death and the road to the underworld, the 'Dark Emperor' is the Supreme Emperor of the Dark Heaven, and the 'North Yin' is the yin spirit of the north. Hai holds Ren Water and Jia Wood, so the old commentary says that besides the River Earl (Water) one must see whether there is dragon-boat rowing (a wooden boat). The Chapter on Ghosts and Spirits that Wang Hongxu mentions is the chapter later in the book that treats ghosts and spirits alone.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 2398 行:日辰带鬼，亟为祈祷保无虞
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 1250 行:日辰带鬼，亟为祈祷保无虞

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.06、LY.ILL.HJC.38

## LY.ILL.HJC.85 福鬼俱空当不治而自愈、子官皆动宜内补而外修：子孙官鬼都空是吉兆（或俱衰静无冲无并）病自愈不用服药；两爻都动不是药不对病而是神祟作祸，须服汤药又祈祷鬼神才愈，俗谓外修内补

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占病，子孙、官鬼两爻都空，是吉兆；或者两爻都衰弱安静、没有冲也没有并，这病自己会好，不用吃药。如果两爻都动，不是药不对病，而是有神祟隔在中间（王洪绪：神祟在作祸），所以没有功效，必须又吃汤药、又祈祷鬼神才会痊愈，俗话叫「外修内补」。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining illness, when the Output and Pressure lines are both Void it is a lucky sign; or if both are weak and still, with neither clash nor joining, the illness heals of itself and no medicine need be taken. If both lines move, it is not that the medicine does not fit the illness but that spirits or a haunting stand between (Wang Hongxu: are working mischief), and so it does no good; he must take decoctions and also pray to the ghosts and spirits to be cured, which the common saying calls 'cultivate without and nourish within'.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7071 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7071*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 福鬼俱空，当不治而自愈； | 子孙和官鬼都落空，应当不治疗也会自己痊愈； | Output and Pressure both Void: it should heal by itself without treatment; |
| 子官皆动，宜内补而外修。 | 子孙和官鬼两爻都动，宜在里面补养、在外面修功德。 | Output and Pressure both moving: it is fitting to nourish within and to cultivate merit without. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7072–7073 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7072–7073*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病，子官二爻俱空，乃吉兆。 | 占病，子孙、官鬼两爻都空，是吉兆。 | In divining illness, when the Output and Pressure lines are both Void, it is a lucky sign. |
| 或俱衰静，无冲无并者，其病自愈，不用服药。 | 或者两爻都衰弱安静，没有冲也没有并的，这病自己会好，不用吃药。 | Or if both are weak and still, with neither clash nor joining, the illness heals of itself and no medicine need be taken. |
| 若二爻俱动，非药不对病，乃是神祟间隔，故无功； | 如果两爻都动，不是药不对病，而是有神祟隔在中间，所以没有功效； | If both lines move, it is not that the medicine does not fit the illness but that spirits or a haunting stand between, and so it does no good; |
| 必须服汤药，又祈祷鬼神得痊。 | 必须又吃汤药、又祈祷鬼神才会痊愈。 | he must take decoctions and also pray to the ghosts and spirits to be cured. |
| 俗谓外修内补也。 | 俗话叫做外修内补。 | The common saying is 'cultivate without and nourish within'. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2470 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2470*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病子官二爻俱空，乃吉兆也，或俱衰静无冲无并者，其病自愈，不用服药。 | 占病，子孙、官鬼两爻都空，是吉兆，或者两爻都衰弱安静、没有冲也没有并的，这病自己会好，不用吃药。 | In divining illness, when the Output and Pressure lines are both Void it is a lucky sign; or if both are weak and still, with neither clash nor joining, the illness heals of itself and no medicine need be taken. |
| 若二爻俱动，此非药不对病，乃是神祟作祸，故曰无功，必须祈祷服药方得病痊，俗所谓外修内补也。 | 如果两爻都动，这不是药不对病，而是神祟在作祸，所以说没有功效，必须又祈祷又吃药才能痊愈，就是俗话说的外修内补。 | If both lines move, this is not that the medicine does not fit the illness but that spirits or a haunting are working mischief, hence it is said to do no good; he must pray and take medicine to be cured, which is the common 'cultivate without and nourish within'. |

> **按**:「并」大概是日辰、月建或别的爻和它同支、并起来帮它。旧注说神祟「间隔」（隔在药和病之间），王洪绪说神祟「作祸」。「外修内补」是外面修功德祈祷、里面吃药调养。
>
> **Note:** 'Joining' (bing) is probably the Day, the Month or another line of the same branch joining up to help it. The old commentary says the haunting 'stands between' (between the medicine and the illness), Wang Hongxu that it 'works mischief'. 'Cultivate without and nourish within' is to cultivate merit and pray outside while taking medicine and recuperating inside.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2470 行:福鬼俱空，当不治而自愈;同文 第 2470 行:子官皆动，宜内补而外修
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1354 行:福鬼俱空，当不治而自愈;同文 第 1354 行:子官皆动，宜内补而外修

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.50

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.93 占鬼神先看卦内官爻：祈福祭祷不拿易爻核对就是白做，官鬼能为祸也能为福佑，看它便知鬼神的情状

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:向鬼神求福，是现在人们崇尚的事，古时候也是这样。可是不拿易的爻来核对，祭祀也是白办。占的时候，先看卦里的官爻，鬼神是什么情形就知道了。官鬼既能带来实实在在的灾祸，也能带来福气和保佑，所以看它就能知道鬼神的情形。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Seeking blessing from ghosts and spirits is what people of the present day esteem, and it has been so since ancient times. But if it is not checked against the lines of the Yi, even the sacrificial rites are performed in vain. In divining, look first at the Pressure line in the hexagram and you will know the condition of the ghost or spirit. The Pressure line can bring real disaster and can bring blessing and protection, so looking at it tells you the condition.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7104–7105 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7104–7105*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 徼福鬼神，乃当今之所尚； | 向鬼神求福，是现在人们崇尚的事； | Seeking blessing from ghosts and spirits is what people of the present day esteem; |
| 祷尔上下，在古昔而皆然。 | 向天地神明祈祷，古时候也都是这样。 | praying to the powers above and below has been the same since ancient times. |
| 不质正于易爻，亦虚行乎祀典； | 不拿易的爻来核对，祭祀的典礼也是白办； | without checking it against the lines of the Yi, even the sacrificial rites are performed in vain; |
| 先看卦内官爻，便知鬼神情状。 | 先看卦里的官爻，就知道鬼神的情形。 | look first at the Pressure line within the hexagram and you will know the condition of the ghost or spirit. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7106 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7106*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼能为实祸，能为福佑。 | 官鬼既能带来实实在在的灾祸，也能带来福气和保佑。 | The Pressure line can bring real disaster and can bring blessing and protection. |
| 故观此亦知其情状也。 | 所以看它，也就知道鬼神的情形了。 | So by looking at it one also knows the condition. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3370 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3370*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼能为祸福，故观此可知其情状也。 | 官鬼能带来灾祸，也能带来福气，所以看它就能知道鬼神的情形。 | The Pressure line can bring both harm and blessing, so by looking at it one can know the condition. |

> **按**:「徼」是求（卜筮大全篇末的生僻字注）。旧注写「实祸」「福佑」，王洪绪写「祸福」，意思一样。《黄金策》有一个本子在这一章标着「存赋不注」，只有赋文；旧注见卜筮大全，王洪绪的注见卜筮正宗。
>
> **Note:** 'Jiao' means to seek (the book's own glossary of rare characters at the end of the chapter). The old commentary writes 'real disaster' and 'blessing and protection' where Wang Hongxu writes 'harm and blessing'; the meaning is the same. One edition of the Huangjin Ce marks this chapter 'the verse kept, no commentary', giving only the verse; the old commentary is found in the Bushi Daquan and Wang Hongxu's commentary in the Bushi Zhengzong.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3370 行:徼福鬼神，乃当今之所尚;同文 第 3370 行:祷尔上下，在古昔而皆然;同文 第 3370 行:不质正于易爻，亦虚行乎祀典;同文 第 3370 行:先看卦内官爻，便知鬼神情状
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1374 行:徼福鬼神，乃当今之所尚;同文 第 1374 行:祷尔上下，在古昔而皆然;同文 第 1374 行:不质正于易爻，亦虚行乎祀典;同文 第 1374 行:先看卦内官爻，便知鬼神情状

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.22、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.94 旺相为神、休囚为鬼，阳爻是男、阴爻是女；鬼爻临长生是少年鬼、帝旺壮年鬼、衰弱老年鬼、胎养小儿鬼；乾宫西北方、巽宫东南方之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻旺相是神，休囚是鬼；阳爻是男，阴爻是女。鬼爻临长生是少年的鬼，临帝旺是壮年的鬼，临衰弱是老年的鬼，临胎、养是小孩的鬼。乾宫的鬼在西北方，巽宫的鬼在东南方。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line that is strong is a spirit; resting or trapped, it is a ghost. Yang is male and yin female. A Pressure line at Birth is a young person's ghost, at Peak a ghost of the prime of life, at decline an old person's ghost, and at Womb or Nurture a child's ghost. A ghost of the Qian palace lies to the northwest, and a ghost of the Xun palace to the southeast.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7108 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7108*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺神衰鬼，方隅乾巽堪推； | 旺的是神，衰的是鬼，方位可以从乾宫、巽宫推出来； | The strong are spirits and the weak are ghosts; the direction can be worked out from the Qian and Xun palaces; |
| 阴女阳男，老幼旺衰可决。 | 阴爻是女、阳爻是男，年老年幼可以从旺衰判定。 | yin is female and yang male, and whether old or young is settled by strength or weakness. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7109–7110 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7109–7110*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡鬼爻旺相，为神。 | 凡是鬼爻旺相，就是神。 | Whenever the Pressure line is strong, it is a spirit. |
| 休囚，为鬼。 | 休囚，就是鬼。 | Resting or trapped, it is a ghost. |
| 阳为男，阴为女。 | 阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。 | Yang is male, yin female. |
| 临长生，少年鬼； | 临长生，是少年的鬼； | At Birth, a young person's ghost; |
| 临帝旺，壮年鬼； | 临帝旺，是壮年的鬼； | at Peak, a ghost of the prime of life; |
| 临衰弱，老年鬼； | 临衰弱，是老年的鬼； | at decline, an old person's ghost; |
| 胎养，小儿鬼。 | 临胎、养，是小孩的鬼。 | at Womb and Nurture, a child's ghost. |
| 乾宫，西北方； | 乾宫的鬼，在西北方； | A ghost of the Qian palace is in the northwest; |
| 巽宫，东南方鬼也。 | 巽宫的鬼，在东南方。 | a ghost of the Xun palace is in the southeast. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3372 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3372*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡鬼爻旺相是神，休囚是鬼，阳为神为男，阴为鬼为女。 | 凡是鬼爻旺相就是神，休囚就是鬼；阳爻是神、是男的，阴爻是鬼、是女的。 | Whenever the Pressure line is strong it is a spirit, and resting or trapped it is a ghost; yang is a spirit and male, yin is a ghost and female. |
| 乾宫西北方，巽宫东南方之鬼也。 | 乾宫是西北方的鬼，巽宫是东南方的鬼。 | The Qian palace is a ghost of the northwest, and the Xun palace a ghost of the southeast. |

> **打架**:神和鬼、男和女是一件事还是两件事：旧注分成两条，旺相是神、休囚是鬼看旺衰，阳爻是男、阴爻是女看阴阳；王洪绪把它们连成一条，「阳为神为男，阴为鬼为女」。照旧注，一条旺相的阴爻是一个女的神；照王洪绪，阴爻就是女鬼。另外王洪绪没有旧注按长生、帝旺、衰弱、胎养分年龄的那一段。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether spirit-or-ghost and male-or-female are one matter or two: the old commentary makes two rules, strong or weak deciding spirit or ghost and yang or yin deciding male or female; Wang Hongxu joins them into one, 'yang is a spirit and male, yin is a ghost and female'. On the old commentary a strong yin line is a female spirit; on Wang Hongxu a yin line is a female ghost. Wang Hongxu also lacks the old commentary's passage that gives the age by Birth, Peak, decline and Womb and Nurture. The books do not decide between them.

> **按**:「长生」「帝旺」是十二长生里的两位（长生、沐浴、冠带、临官、帝旺、衰、病、死、墓、绝、胎、养）；「衰弱」按字面译作衰。「隅」是角落（卜筮大全篇末生僻字注），「方隅」是方位。
>
> **Note:** Birth and Peak are two of the twelve life stages (Birth, Bath, Cap, Office, Peak, Decline, Sickness, Death, Tomb, Extinction, Womb, Nurture); 'weak' here is rendered as decline. 'Yu' is a corner (the book's glossary), and 'fangyu' means the direction.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3372 行:旺神衰鬼，方隅乾巽堪推;同文 第 3372 行:阴女阳男，老幼旺衰可决
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1375 行:旺神衰鬼，方隅乾巽堪推;同文 第 1375 行:阴女阳男，老幼旺衰可决

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.93、LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.ZS.23

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.95 八宫推鬼神：乾宫许天灯斗愿、兑宫口愿伤神、坎北朝圣象、艮城隍宅土、离南殿、坤土地坟陵、震树神或杖死男鬼、巽缢死或颠仆之阴人

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:按鬼所在的卦宫推：在乾宫，是许过点天灯、拜北斗的愿；在兑宫，是口头许的愿或者伤神；在坎宫，是北方朝拜的圣像；在艮宫，是城隍或宅里的土神；在离宫，是南边的殿宇；在坤宫，是土地神或坟墓陵园；在震宫，是树神，或者被杖打死的男鬼；在巽宫，一定是吊死的，或者癫狂跌倒而死的女人。把八宫仔细推敲，各路鬼自然会显应出来。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Work it out by the palace in which the ghost lies. In the Qian palace there was a vow of a heavenly lamp or to the Dipper; in the Dui palace, a spoken vow or an injuring spirit; in Kan, the sacred image worshipped in the north; in Gen, the City God or the earth spirit of the house; in Li, the southern hall; in Kun, the Earth God or graves and tombs; in Zhen, a tree spirit or a male ghost beaten to death with a staff; in Xun, surely one who hanged himself, or a woman who fell in a fit. Work out the eight palaces carefully and the various ghosts will make themselves apparent.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7110–7112 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7110–7112*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若在乾宫，必许天灯斗愿； | 鬼若在乾宫，一定是许过点天灯、拜北斗的愿； | If it is in the Qian palace, there has surely been a vow of a heavenly lamp or to the Dipper; |
| 如居兑卦。 | 如果在兑卦， | if it lies in the Dui trigram, |
| 定然口愿伤神。 | 一定是口头许的愿，或者是伤神。 | it is surely a spoken vow or an injuring spirit. |
| 坎是北朝，艮则城隍宅土； | 在坎是北方朝拜的圣像，在艮就是城隍或宅里的土神； | Kan is the sacred image of the north, and Gen is the City God or the earth spirit of the house; |
| 离为南殿，坤则土地坟陵。 | 离是南边的殿宇，坤就是土地神或坟墓陵园； | Li is the southern hall, and Kun is the Earth God or graves and tombs; |
| 震恐树神，或杖伤之男鬼； | 震要防是树神，或者被棍杖打伤的男鬼； | Zhen may well be a tree spirit, or a male ghost beaten to death with a staff; |
| 巽必缢死，或癫仆之阴人。 | 巽一定是吊死的，或者癫狂跌倒而死的女人。 | Xun is surely one who hanged himself, or a woman who fell in a fit. |
| 八宫仔细推详，诸鬼自能显应。 | 把八个宫仔细推敲，各路鬼自然会显应出来。 | Work out the eight palaces carefully, and the various ghosts will make themselves apparent. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7113 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7113*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以八卦推之。 | 这是按八卦来推。 | This is worked out by the eight trigrams. |
| 乾象为天，鬼在此宫，值火，许点天灯类。 | 乾的象是天，鬼在乾宫，遇到火，是许过点天灯之类的愿。 | Qian is the image of heaven; a ghost in this palace, meeting Fire, means a vow such as lighting a heavenly lamp. |
| 金鬼亦然。 | 金鬼也是这样。 | A Metal ghost is the same. |
| 木鬼许放风筝。 | 木鬼是许过放风筝的愿。 | A Wood ghost means a vow to fly a kite. |
| 子丑二爻，许拜北斗。 | 落在子、丑两个地支的爻，是许过拜北斗的愿。 | On the lines of Zi and Chou, a vow to pay homage to the Dipper. |
| 坤土属阴，故是坟陵土地。 | 坤土属阴，所以是坟陵和土地。 | Kun Earth is yin, so it is graves and tombs and the Earth God. |
| 艮土属阳，故是城隍宅土。 | 艮土属阳，所以是城隍和宅里的土神。 | Gen Earth is yang, so it is the City God and the earth spirit of the house. |
| 离居南，故推南殿。 | 离在南方，所以推作南边的殿宇。 | Li lies in the south, so it is taken as the southern hall. |
| 坎子地，故推北朝圣象。 | 坎是子的位置，所以推作北方朝拜的圣像。 | Kan is the place of Zi, so it is taken as the sacred image worshipped in the north. |
| 震为木，故树神； | 震属木，所以是树神； | Zhen is Wood, so it is a tree spirit; |
| 带白虎或自刑，则杖死之鬼。 | 带白虎或自刑，就是被杖打死的鬼。 | with the White Tiger or self-punishment, it is a ghost beaten to death with a staff. |
| 巽为绳，故主缢死之鬼； | 巽象绳子，所以主吊死的鬼； | Xun is a rope, so it means a hanged ghost; |
| 不带鰧蛇，或非刑爻，则是风症死者。 | 不带螣蛇，或者不是刑爻，就是得风症死的人。 | without the Coiling Snake, or if it is not a punishing line, it is one who died of a wind disorder. |
| 兑为口，故有呪咀口愿，又属金，故推伤神。 | 兑象口，所以有咒骂和口头许的愿；又属金，所以推作伤神。 | Dui is the mouth, so there are curses and spoken vows; it is also Metal, so it is taken as an injuring spirit. |
| 盖乾三连，是浑成之金； | 因为乾卦三画相连，是浑然一体的金； | For Qian has three unbroken lines and is whole, undivided Metal; |
| 兑象上缺，是伤损之金； | 兑卦的象上面缺了一口，是受了损伤的金； | Dui is open at the top and is damaged Metal; |
| 故以兑为伤神，而不以乾为伤神。 | 所以把兑当作伤神，而不把乾当作伤神。 | so Dui is taken as the injuring spirit, and Qian is not. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3375 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以八卦推之，乾象为天神，在此宫属火，宜许点天灯斗愿类。 | 这是按八卦来推：乾的象是天神，鬼在这个宫又属火，该是许过点天灯、拜北斗之类的愿。 | This is worked out by the eight trigrams: Qian is the image of the heavenly spirit, and a ghost in this palace, being Fire, should mean a vow such as lighting a heavenly lamp or to the Dipper. |

> **按**:王洪绪引经文时，把「震恐树神，或杖伤之男鬼」写成「震恐神煞杖伤之男鬼」，把「八宫」写成「八卦」，「颠仆」写成「癫仆」的另一写法；他自己的注只讲了乾宫一例，其余各宫的推法只见于旧注。旧注里的「木鬼许放风筝」「子丑二爻许拜北斗」经文里没有，是旧注自己添的。「呪咀」是咒骂诅咒；「囹圄」「癫」的解释见卜筮大全篇末生僻字注。旧注最后一段解释了为什么以兑而不以乾为伤神：乾三画连是完整的金，兑上缺是有损伤的金。
>
> **Note:** When Wang Hongxu quotes the verse he writes 'Zhen may well be a spirit-star beaten with a staff' where the Bushi Daquan has 'a tree spirit, or a male ghost beaten to death', and 'the eight trigrams' for 'the eight palaces'; his own commentary treats only the Qian example, and the workings for the other palaces are found only in the old commentary. 'A Wood ghost means a vow to fly a kite' and 'on the lines of Zi and Chou, a vow to the Dipper' are not in the verse and are the old commentary's own additions. 'Zhouzu' means cursing and imprecation. The last passage of the old commentary explains why Dui and not Qian is the injuring spirit: Qian's three unbroken lines are whole Metal, and Dui, open at the top, is damaged Metal.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3374 行:若在乾宫，必许天灯斗愿;同文 第 3374 行:定然口愿伤神;同文 第 3374 行:坎是北朝，艮则城隍宅土;同文 第 3374 行:离为南殿，坤则土地坟陵;异文(相似 0.8) 第 3374 行:震恐神煞杖伤之男鬼;异文(相似 0.9) 第 3374 行:巽必缢死或颠仆之阴人;异文(相似 0.92) 第 3374 行:八卦仔细推详，诸鬼自能显应
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1375 行:若在乾宫，必许天灯斗愿;同文 第 1375 行:定然口愿伤神;同文 第 1375 行:坎是北朝，艮则城隍宅土;同文 第 1376 行:离为南殿，坤则土地坟陵;同文 第 1376 行:震恐树神，或杖伤之男鬼;同文 第 1376 行:巽必缢死，或癫仆之阴人;异文(相似 0.92) 第 1376 行:八宫仔细推详，诸鬼自然显应

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

## LY.ILL.HJC.96 六神推鬼神：勾陈土神为祸、朱雀咒诅口舌、白虎血神、玄武死于不明之鬼（化兄弟则盗贼）、青龙善愿、螣蛇施相之神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:按鬼爻带的六神推：带勾陈，是土神在作祸；带朱雀，是有人咒骂诅咒；带白虎，是血神，有带血的女伤，在阳爻上是凶暴恶死的鬼；带玄武，是死得不明不白的鬼，变成兄弟或从兄弟变出来的，是盗贼；带青龙，是善愿；带螣蛇，是施相之神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Work it out by the six spirits the Pressure line carries. With the Hook Earth, an earth spirit is making trouble; with the Vermilion Bird, curses and imprecations; with the White Tiger, the blood spirit, a woman's injury with blood, and on a yang line a violent ghost who died an ugly death; with the Dark Tortoise, a ghost who died in an unclear way, and if it changes into Peer or has come out of Peer, a thief or bandit; with the Azure Dragon, a good vow; with the Coiling Snake, the spirit of Shixiang.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7114 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7114*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 更值勾陈，必有土神见碍； | 再遇到勾陈，一定有土神来作梗； | Where the Hook Earth is added, an earth spirit is surely obstructing; |
| 如临朱雀，定然呪咀相侵。 | 如果临朱雀，一定是咒骂诅咒来侵扰。 | if it falls on the Vermilion Bird, curses and imprecations surely intrude. |
| 白虎血亡，玄武则死于不明之鬼。 | 白虎是血亡，玄武就是死得不明不白的鬼； | The White Tiger is death by blood, and the Dark Tortoise is a ghost who died in an unclear way; |
| 青龙善愿，螣蛇则犯乎施相之神。 | 青龙是善愿，螣蛇就是冒犯了施相之神。 | the Azure Dragon is a good vow, and the Coiling Snake means the spirit of Shixiang has been offended. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7115–7117 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7115–7117*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以六神推之。 | 这是按六神来推。 | This is worked out by the six spirits. |
| 勾陈职专田土，鬼爻临之，乃是土神为祸。 | 勾陈专管田地土壤，鬼爻带着它，就是土神在作祸。 | The Hook Earth is charged with fields and soil; when the Pressure line carries it, an earth spirit is making trouble. |
| 朱雀为口舌，故有呪咀。 | 朱雀主口舌，所以有咒骂诅咒。 | The Vermilion Bird is talk and quarrels, so there are curses. |
| 白虎为血神，故有带血女伤； | 白虎是血神，所以有带血的女伤； | The White Tiger is the blood spirit, so there is a woman's injury with blood; |
| 居阳则凶暴恶死之鬼也。 | 在阳爻上，就是凶暴、死得凶恶的鬼。 | on a yang line it is a violent ghost who died an ugly death. |
| 玄武是幽暗，故有不明死鬼； | 玄武是幽暗，所以有死得不明不白的鬼； | The Dark Tortoise is obscurity, so there is a ghost who died in an unclear way; |
| 化兄弟，或从兄弟化出，则是盗贼。 | 变成兄弟，或者从兄弟变出来，就是盗贼。 | if it changes into Peer, or has come out of a change from Peer, it is a thief or bandit. |
| 青龙是喜神，故有善愿。 | 青龙是喜神，所以有善愿。 | The Azure Dragon is the spirit of joy, so there is a good vow. |
| 鰧蛇推施相之神，今俗祷谢，必以面作小蛇，献之取验。 | 螣蛇推作施相之神，现在民间祷告酬谢，一定用面做成小蛇献上去，来求应验。 | The Coiling Snake is taken as the spirit of Shixiang; in present custom, when prayers are offered and thanks given, a small snake is always made of dough and offered up to gain proof. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3377 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3377*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以六神推之。 | 这是按六神来推。 | This is worked out by the six spirits. |
| 勾陈职专田土，鬼爻临之，乃是土神为祸类。 | 勾陈专管田地土壤，鬼爻带着它，就是土神作祸这一类。 | The Hook Earth is charged with fields and soil; when the Pressure line carries it, it is of the kind where an earth spirit makes trouble. |

> **按**:旧注把六神一个一个讲了，王洪绪只讲了勾陈一例。「带血女伤」书里没有再解释，按字面译。「施相」书里没有解释；旧注说现在民间酬谢螣蛇这一路神，用面捏一条小蛇献上，所以译成人名式的「施相之神」。王洪绪引经文时，「螣蛇则犯乎施相之神」写作「螣蛇则犯乎施相之人」，「白虎血亡」写作「白虎血神」；这几处字面不同，书里没有说哪个对。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary takes the six spirits one by one; Wang Hongxu treats only the Hook Earth. 'A woman's injury with blood' is not explained further in the book and is rendered literally. 'Shixiang' is not explained; the old commentary says that in present custom thanks to this spirit are given with a small snake moulded of dough, so it is rendered as a name. When Wang Hongxu quotes the verse he writes 'the Coiling Snake offends the person of Shixiang' for 'the spirit of Shixiang', and 'the White Tiger the blood spirit' for 'the White Tiger death by blood'; the books do not say which is right.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3377 行:更值勾陈，必有土神见碍;异文(相似 0.8) 第 3377 行:如临朱雀，定然咒诅相侵;异文(相似 0.92) 第 3377 行:白虎血神，玄武则死于不明之鬼;异文(相似 0.92) 第 3377 行:青龙善愿，螣蛇则犯乎施相之
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1376 行:更值勾陈，必有土神见碍;异文(相似 0.8) 第 1376 行:如临朱雀，定然咒诅相侵;同文 第 1376 行:白虎血亡。玄武则死于不明之鬼;同文 第 1376 行:青龙善愿，螣蛇则犯乎施相之神

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.HJC.97 五行推鬼神：金是伤司刀兵鬼、火是灶神香愿、木是枷锁、水是河伯江神；旧注细分火鬼二爻灶君、午火衰弱烧死鬼、木鬼带螣蛇吊死、水鬼衰弱落水鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:按鬼所属的五行推：金是被刀兵所伤的鬼，旺的是伤神，衰的是伤司手下的部众；火，一定是灶神或者香愿，落在二爻是灶君，衰弱的午火是被烧死的鬼；木是枷锁，东方的神鬼，变金或从金变出来的是戴枷锁的鬼，带螣蛇是吊死的鬼，木变木再加白虎是被夹死的鬼；水是河伯江神，旺相是江神河伯，衰弱是落水的鬼，在震、巽宫或木变水，是从船上掉下水淹死的鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Work it out by the element the ghost belongs to. Metal is a ghost wounded by blade and weapon: strong, an injuring spirit; weak, the followers of the Injuring Office. Fire is surely the Kitchen God or an incense vow: on the second line the Kitchen God, and a weak Wu Fire is a ghost burned to death. Wood is cangue and lock, a spirit or ghost of the east: changing into Metal or coming from Metal, a ghost in shackles; with the Coiling Snake, a hanged ghost; Wood changing into Wood with the White Tiger added, a ghost pressed to death. Water is the Lord of the River and the river god: strong, a river god; weak, a ghost who fell into the water; in the Zhen or Xun palace or Wood changing into Water, a ghost who fell from a boat and drowned.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7119 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7119*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金乃伤司，火定灶神香愿； | 金是伤司，火一定是灶神和香愿； | Metal is the Injuring Office, and Fire is surely the Kitchen God or an incense vow; |
| 木为枷锁，水为河伯江神。 | 木是枷锁，水是河伯江神。 | Wood is cangue and lock, and Water is the Lord of the River and the river god. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7120 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7120*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以五行推之。 | 这是按五行来推。 | This is worked out by the five elements. |
| 金主刀兵所伤之鬼； | 金主被刀兵伤害的鬼； | Metal governs ghosts wounded by blade and weapon; |
| 旺是伤神，衰是伤司部众。 | 旺的是伤神，衰的是伤司手下的部众。 | if strong it is an injuring spirit, if weak the followers of the Injuring Office. |
| 火鬼在二爻，是灶君，其余香信。 | 火鬼在二爻，是灶君；其余的是香愿。 | A Fire ghost on the second line is the Kitchen God; otherwise it is an incense pledge. |
| 本宫内卦，是本境土地，神佛香愿； | 在本宫的内卦，是本地的土地神、神佛的香愿； | In the inner trigram of the home palace, it is the local Earth God and an incense vow to a god or Buddha; |
| 他宫，别境香愿。 | 在别的宫，是别处的香愿。 | in another palace, an incense vow elsewhere. |
| 在六爻，远方香信。 | 在六爻，是远方的香信。 | On the sixth line, an incense pledge from afar. |
| 在五爻，途中所许。 | 在五爻，是路上许下的愿。 | On the fifth line, a vow made on the road. |
| 持世当境，社会中香愿也。 | 持世，就是本境，是社里、会里的香愿。 | Holding the Self position means the local area: an incense vow of the village society. |
| 午火衰弱，必是烧死之鬼。 | 午火衰弱，一定是被火烧死的鬼。 | A weak Wu Fire is surely a ghost who was burned to death. |
| 木鬼，东方神鬼。 | 木鬼，是东方的神鬼。 | A Wood ghost is a spirit or ghost of the east. |
| 化金，或从金化，是械锁鬼。 | 变成金，或者从金变出来，是戴枷锁的鬼。 | Changing into Metal, or coming from a change from Metal, it is a ghost in shackles and locks. |
| 带鰧蛇，吊死之鬼。 | 带螣蛇，是吊死的鬼。 | With the Coiling Snake, a hanged ghost. |
| 木化木，又加白虎，夹死鬼。 | 木变木，再加白虎，是被夹死的鬼。 | Wood changing into Wood, with the White Tiger added, a ghost pressed to death. |
| 水鬼旺相，江神河伯； | 水鬼旺相，是江神河伯； | A strong Water ghost is a river god or the Lord of the River; |
| 衰弱，落水鬼。 | 衰弱，是落水的鬼。 | if weak, a ghost who fell into the water. |
| 在震巽二宫，或木化水，必是船上堕水死鬼。 | 在震宫、巽宫，或者木变水，一定是从船上掉下水淹死的鬼。 | In the Zhen and Xun palaces, or Wood changing into Water, it is surely a ghost who fell from a boat and drowned. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3379 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3379*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以五行推之。 | 这是按五行来推。 | This is worked out by the five elements. |
| 金乃刀兵所伤之鬼，旺是伤神，衰是伤鬼类。 | 金是被刀兵伤害的鬼，旺的是伤神，衰的是伤鬼这一类。 | Metal is a ghost wounded by blade and weapon; if strong it is an injuring spirit, if weak a ghost of the wounded kind. |

> **打架**:要不要把五行落实到一位具体的神：旧注给出很具体的名目（火鬼在二爻是灶君，金旺是伤神、衰是伤司部众，水旺是江神河伯），王洪绪只留了金的一例；增删卜易的觉子认为不必把金官指为关公、岳公这一类具体的神，应该问病人心里疑的是哪位神，再占一卦验证（见 LY.ILL.ZS.24）。三处怎样落实到神，说法各不相同，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to fix an element to a particular named spirit: the old commentary gives very specific names (a Fire ghost on the second line is the Kitchen God; strong Metal is an injuring spirit and weak Metal the followers of the Injuring Office; strong Water is a river god), while Wang Hongxu keeps only the Metal example; the Master Awakened (Jue Zi) in the Zengshan Buyi holds that one should not name Metal Pressure as Guan Gong, Yue Gong or the like, but ask which spirit the patient has in mind and divine again to check (see LY.ILL.ZS.24). The three differ on how far to name the spirit, and the books do not decide.

> **按**:「伤司」是主刀兵伤害的司，「部众」是它手下的众鬼；「香信」是香愿。「夹死鬼」旧注没有再解释，按字面译作被夹死的鬼。「河泊」是王洪绪引经文时对「河伯」的写法。「持世」是鬼爻落在世爻的位置，这里指鬼就是本地的，是社会中的香愿。
>
> **Note:** The 'Injuring Office' is the office in charge of wounds by blade and weapon and its 'followers' are the ghosts under it; 'incense pledge' is a vow of incense. 'A ghost pressed to death' is not explained further in the old commentary and is rendered literally. 'Hebo' is Wang Hongxu's way of writing 'Lord of the River' when he quotes the verse. 'Holding the Self position' means the ghost sits on the Self line's place, here meaning the ghost belongs to the locality: a vow made in the village society.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3379 行:金乃伤司，火定灶神香愿;异文(相似 0.8) 第 3379 行:木为枷锁，水为河泊之神
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1377 行:金乃伤司，火定灶神香愿;同文 第 1377 行:木为枷锁，水为河伯江神

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.98 鬼是土爻要分类：阴土阳土，震巽宫或木化土是树头土，临应冲世或日辰带土鬼冲克是飞来土，日月动变皆带土是五方土

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻是土的要分清是哪一类：阴爻上是阴土，阳爻上是阳土；在震宫、巽宫，或者从木变出来的，是树头（木头）上的土；落在应爻、冲世爻的位置上，或者日辰带着土鬼来冲来克，是飞来的土；日、月、动爻、变爻都带土，是五方的土。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Where the ghost is an Earth line, tell which kind: on a yin line, yin earth, on a yang line, yang earth; in the Zhen or Xun palace, or coming from a change from Wood, earth at the head of a tree (wood-head earth); standing on the Other line and clashing the Self line, or with the Day carrying an Earth ghost that clashes or controls, flying earth; if the Day, Month, moving line and changed line all carry Earth, earth of the five directions.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7122 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7122*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若见土爻，当分厂类。 | 如果看到土爻，就该分清是哪一类。 | If you see an Earth line, you should tell which kind it is. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7123 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7123*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土爻临鬼，阴是阴土，阳是阳土。 | 土爻带鬼，阴爻是阴土，阳爻是阳土。 | When an Earth line carries the ghost, a yin one is yin earth and a yang one is yang earth. |
| 在震巽二宫，或从木化皆然，或树头土。 | 在震宫、巽宫，或者从木变出来的，都一样，也可能是树头上的土。 | In the Zhen and Xun palaces, or when it comes from a change from Wood, it is likewise, or it may be earth at the head of a tree. |
| 临应冲世，或日辰带土鬼相冲相克，是飞来土。 | 落在应爻、冲世爻的位置，或者日辰带着土鬼来冲来克，是飞来的土。 | Standing on the Other line and clashing the Self line, or when the Day carries an Earth ghost that clashes or controls, it is flying earth. |
| 若日月动变皆带土，五方土也。 | 如果日、月、动爻、变爻都带土，就是五方的土。 | If the Day, Month, moving line and changed line all carry Earth, it is earth of the five directions. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3381 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3381*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土鬼阴爻是阴土，阳爻是阳土，或从木化是木头土，临应冲世是飞来土，若日月动变者，五方土类也。 | 土鬼在阴爻上是阴土，在阳爻上是阳土；从木变出来的是木头土，落在应爻、冲世爻上是飞来土；如果日、月、动爻、变爻都是土，就是五方土这一类。 | An Earth ghost on a yin line is yin earth and on a yang line yang earth; one coming from a change from Wood is wood-head earth; standing on the Other line and clashing the Self line it is flying earth; if the Day, Month, moving and changed lines are all Earth, it is of the kind of earth of the five directions. |

> **按**:卜筮大全经文写「当分厂类」，「厂」是「厥」字的误刻，卜筮正宗写作「厥类」，译作「哪一类」。旧注说「树头土」，王洪绪说「木头土」，书里没有解释，按字面译。旧注多出「在震巽二宫」这一条。两家都只把土分成了几种，没有说每一种土对应什么事，所以本库只记分类，不替它添意思。
>
> **Note:** The Bushi Daquan verse reads 'chang lei', where 'chang' is a mis-cut for 'jue' (as in the Bushi Zhengzong's 'jue lei'), rendered 'which kind'. The old commentary says 'earth at the head of a tree' and Wang Hongxu 'wood-head earth'; neither is explained and both are rendered literally. The old commentary adds 'in the Zhen and Xun palaces'. Both only divide Earth into kinds and do not say what each kind stands for, so the library records the classification and adds no meaning of its own.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.88) 第 3381 行:若见土爻当分厥类
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1377 行:若见土爻，当分厂类

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.99

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.99 鬼墓是伏尸为祸、财库是藏神不安：鬼爻属金卦有丑动是鬼墓，妻财属木卦有未动是财库；不伤世则不为祸

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼的墓，是埋着的尸骨在作祸；财的库，是藏着的神不得安宁。看法：鬼爻属金，卦里有丑爻发动，就是鬼的墓；妻财属木，卦里有未爻发动，就是财的库；别的照这样推。但是不伤到世爻，就不会作祸。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The ghost's tomb means a buried corpse is making trouble; Wealth's storehouse means a hidden spirit is unsettled. How to see it: if the Pressure line is Metal and a Chou line in the hexagram moves, that is the ghost's tomb; if the Wealth line is Wood and a Wei line in the hexagram moves, that is Wealth's storehouse; work out the others the same way. But if it does not harm the Self line, it does no harm.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7125 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7125*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼墓乃伏尸为祸； | 鬼的墓，是埋着的尸骨在作祸； | The ghost's tomb means a buried corpse is making trouble; |
| 财库则藏神不安。 | 财的库，是藏着的神不得安宁。 | and Wealth's storehouse means a hidden spirit is unsettled. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7126 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7126*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻属金，卦有丑动是鬼墓。 | 鬼爻属金，卦里有丑爻发动，就是鬼的墓。 | If the Pressure line is Metal and a Chou line in the hexagram moves, that is the ghost's tomb. |
| 妻财属木，卦有未动是财库。 | 妻财属木，卦里有未爻发动，就是财的库。 | If the Wealth line is Wood and a Wei line in the hexagram moves, that is Wealth's storehouse. |
| 他仿此。 | 别的照这个办法推。 | Work out the others the same way. |
| 二事见家宅决中，今不尽述。 | 这两件事在家宅的断法里有讲，这里不全说了。 | These two matters are treated in the household section and are not fully described here. |
| 然不伤世则不为祸。 | 但是不伤到世爻，就不会作祸。 | But if it does not harm the Self line, it does no harm. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3383 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3383*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻属金，卦有丑动是金墓； | 鬼爻属金，卦里有丑爻发动，就是金的墓； | If the Pressure line is Metal and a Chou line in the hexagram moves, that is the tomb of Metal; |
| 妻财属木，卦有未动是木墓。 | 妻财属木，卦里有未爻发动，就是木的墓。 | if the Wealth line is Wood and a Wei line in the hexagram moves, that is the tomb of Wood. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个办法推。 | Follow this for the rest. |

> **按**:旧注末了有两句王洪绪没有：一句指向家宅的断法，一句给了条件「不伤世则不为祸」。王洪绪把旧注的「财库」直接写成「木墓」，意思相同（未是木的墓）。「墓」这里指的是五行入墓的那一支：金墓在丑，木墓在未。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary ends with two sentences that Wang Hongxu lacks: one pointing to the household section and one giving the condition 'if it does not harm the Self line, it does no harm'. Wang Hongxu writes the old commentary's 'Wealth's storehouse' directly as 'the tomb of Wood', which means the same (Wei is the tomb of Wood). The 'tomb' here is the branch in which an element enters its tomb: Chou for Metal, Wei for Wood.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3383 行:鬼墓乃伏尸为祸;同文 第 3383 行:财库则藏神不安
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1377 行:鬼墓乃伏尸为祸;同文 第 1377 行:财库则藏神不安

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.HJC.100

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.100 修造动土必是煞遇勾陈、口舌起因是土逢朱雀：土鬼加勾陈因修造动土不安，初爻穿井、二爻作灶、三四爻当门、五爻路边、六爻篱墙下；临朱雀则动土时暗有口舌咒诅

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:因为修造、动土出了事，是土鬼碰上了勾陈：落在初爻是挖井动土，二爻是砌灶动土，三、四爻是门口动土，五爻是路边动土，六爻是篱笆墙下动土；在兑宫是挖池动土，艮宫是筑墙动土，坤宫是造坟动土，坎宫加玄武是挖厕坑动土。以上如果带朱雀，就是动土的时候暗中有口舌和咒诅，或者有人多嘴惹事，因此成了祸。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Trouble from building or breaking ground is an Earth ghost meeting the Hook Earth: on the first line, digging a well; the second, building a stove; the third and fourth, breaking ground at the door; the fifth, by the road; the sixth, beneath the fence or wall; in the Dui palace, digging a pond; in the Gen palace, building a wall; in the Kun palace, building a grave; in the Kan palace with the Dark Tortoise, digging a privy pit. If any of these carries the Vermilion Bird, then at the time of breaking ground there were hidden quarrels and curses, or someone talked too much and stirred up trouble, and this became the misfortune.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7128 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7128*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 修造动土，必然杀遇勾陈； | 因为修造、动土而出事，一定是煞遇上了勾陈； | Trouble from building or breaking ground is surely a malign influence meeting the Hook Earth; |
| 口舌起因，乃是土逢朱雀。 | 口舌是非的起因，是土遇上了朱雀。 | and the cause of quarrels is Earth meeting the Vermilion Bird. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7129–7131 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7129–7131*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此亦言土鬼也。 | 这也是说土鬼。 | This too speaks of an Earth ghost. |
| 加勾陈，必因修造动土，以致不安。 | 再加勾陈，一定是因为修造、动土，弄得不得安宁。 | With the Hook Earth added, it is surely because of building or breaking ground that there is unrest. |
| 在初爻，穿井不安。 | 在初爻，是挖井弄得不安。 | On the first line, digging a well has caused the unrest. |
| 二爻，作灶动土。 | 二爻，是砌灶动土。 | The second line: building a stove and breaking ground. |
| 三爻四爻，当门动土。 | 三爻、四爻，是门口动土。 | The third and fourth lines: breaking ground at the door. |
| 五爻，路边动土。 | 五爻，是路边动土。 | The fifth line: breaking ground by the road. |
| 六爻篱笆墙下动土。 | 六爻，是篱笆、墙下动土。 | The sixth line: breaking ground beneath the fence or wall. |
| 兑宫，开池动土。 | 兑宫，是挖池塘动土。 | The Dui palace: digging a pond. |
| 艮宫，筑墙动土。 | 艮宫，是筑墙动土。 | The Gen palace: building a wall. |
| 坤宫，造坟动土。 | 坤宫，是造坟动土。 | The Kun palace: building a grave. |
| 坎宫加玄武，坑厕动土。 | 坎宫加玄武，是挖厕坑动土。 | The Kan palace with the Dark Tortoise: digging a privy pit. |
| 以上若临朱雀，则是动土时，暗有口舌，呪咀。 | 以上这些如果带朱雀，就是动土的时候，暗中有口舌是非、咒骂诅咒。 | If any of the above carries the Vermilion Bird, then at the time of breaking ground there were hidden quarrels and curses. |
| 或多口惹事，以致为祸。 | 或者是有人多嘴惹事，因此成了祸。 | Or a talkative person stirred up trouble, and this became the misfortune. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3385 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3385*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此亦土鬼也，如勾陈，必因修造动土以致不安。 | 这也是土鬼；带勾陈的话，一定是因为修造、动土才弄得不安。 | This too is an Earth ghost; if with the Hook Earth, it is surely because of building or breaking ground that there is unrest. |

> **按**:旧注列了一长串位置，王洪绪只讲了总的一句。旧注写「在初爻」「二爻」「三爻四爻」没有说指的是土鬼落在哪一爻，按上下文译作土鬼所在的爻位。「杀」这里就是「煞」，一种凶的力量。「呪咀」是咒骂诅咒。旧注的位置表里三爻、四爻都是「当门动土」，而上面六爻推鬼神一条里三爻是门、四爻是户，两处不完全对应；书里没有说明。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary gives a long list of positions and Wang Hongxu only the general sentence. The old commentary's 'on the first line', 'the second line' and 'the third and fourth lines' do not say which line is meant, and are rendered by context as the line on which the Earth ghost stands. 'Sha' here is the malign influence. 'Zhouzu' is cursing and imprecation. In the old commentary's list both the third and fourth lines are 'breaking ground at the door', while in the entry on the six lines the third is the gate and the fourth the door of the house, so the two do not correspond exactly; the book does not explain.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 3385 行:修造动土，必然煞遇勾陈;同文 第 3385 行:口舌起因，乃是土逢朱雀
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1377 行:修造动土，必然杀遇勾陈;同文 第 1378 行:口舌起因，乃是土逢朱雀

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.HJC.101

## LY.ILL.HJC.101 六爻推鬼神：初爻水鬼犯井神、二爻火鬼干司命（灶神）、三爻犯家堂、五爻求五路神、四爻遇世冲是出门撞见、六爻月合是远地相干

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:按鬼落在哪一爻推：水鬼在初爻，冒犯了井神；火鬼在二爻，触犯了司命（灶神）；鬼在三爻（门），冒犯了家堂的香火；鬼在五爻（路上），要求五路神，金鬼是五道、火鬼是五圣、木鬼是五郎神众、水鬼是五路神司，衰弱就是路边的鬼；鬼在四爻，遇到冲世，一定是出门撞见的；鬼在六爻，逢月建生合，是远方的神来干犯，月建、日辰生合，是天神那里有愿。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Work it out by the line on which the ghost falls. A Water ghost on the first line has offended the well god; a Fire ghost on the second has offended the Director of Fate (the Kitchen God); a ghost on the third (the gate) has offended the incense and fire of the household shrine; on the fifth (the road), seek the Gods of the Five Roads: a Metal ghost is the Five Paths, Fire the Five Sages, Wood the Five Lords and their company, Water the Officers of the Five Roads, and if weak a ghost of the roadside. A ghost on the fourth line meeting a clash with Self was surely met on going out; a ghost on the sixth line meeting a combination with the Month is a spirit from far away, and if the Month and the Day generate or combine with it there is a vow to a heavenly spirit.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7133 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7133*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 或犯井神，水在初爻遇鬼； | 也许冒犯了井神，是水在初爻遇到了鬼； | Perhaps the well god has been offended: Water on the first line meeting the ghost; |
| 或干司命，火临二位逢官。 | 也许触犯了司命，是火在二爻遇到了官。 | or the Director of Fate has been disturbed: Fire on the second place meeting Pressure. |
| 若在门头，须犯家堂部属； | 如果在门这一头，一定是冒犯了家堂的部属； | If it is at the door, the retinue of the household shrine has been offended; |
| 如临道上，当求五路神祗。 | 如果在路上这一位，该去求五路神。 | if it is on the road, one should seek the Gods of the Five Roads. |
| 四遇世冲，鬼必出门撞见； | 四爻遇到冲世，鬼一定是出门时撞见的； | The fourth line meeting a clash with Self means the ghost was surely met on going out; |
| 六逢月合，神须远地相干。 | 六爻逢到月建相合，神一定是从远处来犯的。 | the sixth line meeting a combination with the Month means the spirit comes from a distant place. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7134–7136 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7134–7136*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以六爻推之也。 | 这是按六个爻位来推。 | This is worked out by the six line positions. |
| 初爻水鬼，井泉童子不安。 | 初爻是水鬼，井泉童子不得安宁。 | A Water ghost on the first line: the child spirit of the well and spring is unsettled. |
| 二爻火鬼，灶神见责。 | 二爻是火鬼，灶神怪罪。 | A Fire ghost on the second line: the Kitchen God is reproaching. |
| 三爻为门，官鬼临之，当犯家堂香火。 | 三爻是门，官鬼落在这里，是冒犯了家堂的香火。 | The third line is the gate; when Pressure sits there, the incense and fire of the household shrine has been offended. |
| 四爻为户，若遇官克世冲世，必曾出门撞见鬼崇。 | 四爻是户，如果官爻克世爻、冲世爻，一定是曾经出门撞见了鬼崇。 | The fourth line is the door of the house; if Pressure controls or clashes the Self line, one surely met a ghostly influence on going out. |
| 五爻临金鬼，五道； | 五爻临金鬼，是五道； | The fifth line with a Metal ghost: the Five Paths; |
| 火鬼，五圣； | 火鬼，是五圣； | a Fire ghost: the Five Sages; |
| 木鬼，五郎神众； | 木鬼，是五郎神众； | a Wood ghost: the Five Lords and their company; |
| 水鬼，五路神司。 | 水鬼，是五路神司。 | a Water ghost: the Officers of the Five Roads. |
| 衰弱，则道路边鬼。 | 衰弱的话，就是路边的鬼。 | If weak, it is a ghost of the roadside. |
| 在六爻，定远方鬼。 | 在六爻，一定是远方的鬼。 | On the sixth line, it is surely a ghost from afar. |
| 月建日辰生合，乃天神中有愿。 | 月建、日辰生它、合它，是天神那里有愿。 | If the Month and the Day generate or combine with it, there is a vow to a heavenly spirit. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3387 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3387*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼临于初爻断井神，火鬼临于二爻断司命，如鬼临三爻断家堂，如临五爻断路头五圣，临四爻断出门撞祟，临六爻断远处染邪。 | 水鬼在初爻，断是井神；火鬼在二爻，断是司命；鬼在三爻，断是家堂；在五爻，断是路头五圣；在四爻，断是出门撞上了邪祟；在六爻，断是在远处沾染了邪气。 | A Water ghost on the first line is judged the well god; a Fire ghost on the second, the Director of Fate; a ghost on the third, the household shrine; on the fifth, the Five Sages of the road-head; on the fourth, running into an evil influence on going out; on the sixth, catching an evil taint in a far place. |

> **按**:经文「四遇世冲」，王洪绪引作「四遇世神」；「神祗」他也写作「神祇」的同一个意思。王洪绪比旧注少了几样：旧注按五行分五爻上的五道、五圣、五郎、五路，还有「衰弱则道路边鬼」和「月建日辰生合，乃天神中有愿」；王洪绪把五爻一律断作路头五圣。「司命」是管人命运的灶神一类的神，这里旧注和经文都把它对到二爻的灶神。
>
> **Note:** Where the verse says 'the fourth line meeting a clash with Self' (shi chong), Wang Hongxu quotes 'the fourth line meeting the Self spirit' (shi shen). Wang Hongxu has less than the old commentary: the old commentary divides the fifth line by element into the Five Paths, the Five Sages, the Five Lords and the Five Roads, adds 'if weak, a ghost of the roadside' and 'if the Month and Day generate or combine, there is a vow to a heavenly spirit'; Wang Hongxu judges every ghost on the fifth line as the Five Sages of the road-head. The 'Director of Fate' is a spirit in charge of people's destinies of the Kitchen God kind, and both the verse and the old commentary match it to the Kitchen God of the second line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3387 行:或犯井神，水在初爻遇鬼;同文 第 3387 行:或干司命，火临二位逢官;同文 第 3387 行:若在门头，须犯家堂部属;同文 第 3387 行:如临道上，当求五路神祗;异文(相似 0.9) 第 3387 行:四遇世神，鬼必出门撞见;同文 第 3387 行:六逢月合，神须远地相干
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1378 行:或犯井神，水在初爻遇鬼;同文 第 1378 行:或干司命，火临二位逢官;同文 第 1378 行:若在门头，须犯家堂部属;同文 第 1378 行:如临道上，当求五路神祗;同文 第 1379 行:四遇世冲鬼，必出门撞见;同文 第 1379 行:六逢月合神，须远地相干

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.100、LY.ILL.HJC.102

## LY.ILL.HJC.102 以卦身推是什么人的鬼：鬼克身是冤家债主、身克鬼是妻妾阴人，我生它是卑幼儿童僧道、它生我是祖宗尊长父母，无生合克刑是兄弟朋友

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:拿卦身来推是什么人的鬼：鬼克卦身，是冤家债主的鬼，一定难以退去，病也不容易好；卦身克鬼，是妻妾一类女人的鬼；卦身生鬼，是晚辈、小孩、僧人道士的鬼（子孙）；鬼生卦身，是祖宗、长辈、父母的鬼（父母）；两者比和，是兄弟朋友的鬼，阴爻上是妯娌姊妹的鬼；没有生、合、克、刑，也一定是兄弟朋友。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Work out whose ghost it is by the Body. A ghost that controls the Body is the ghost of an enemy or creditor, hard to drive off, and the illness will not easily settle; the Body controlling the ghost is the ghost of a wife, concubine or woman; the Body generating the ghost is the ghost of a junior, child, monk or Daoist (Output); a ghost generating the Body is the ghost of an ancestor, elder or parent (Resource); where the two are in harmony it is the ghost of a brother or friend (Peer), and on a yin line of a sister-in-law or sister; with no generating, combining, controlling or punishing, it is surely a brother or friend.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7138–7139 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7138–7139*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼克身，冤家债主； | 鬼克卦身，是冤家债主； | If the ghost controls the Body, an enemy or creditor; |
| 身克鬼，妻妾阴人。 | 卦身克鬼，是妻妾一类的女人； | if the Body controls the ghost, a wife or concubine, a woman; |
| 我去生他，卑幼儿童僧道； | 我去生它，是晚辈、小孩、僧人道士； | if I generate it, a junior, a child, a monk or Daoist; |
| 他来生我，祖宗尊长爹娘。 | 它来生我，是祖宗、长辈、父母。 | if it generates me, an ancestor, elder, father or mother. |
| 若无生合克刑，必是弟兄朋友。 | 如果没有生、合、克、刑，一定是兄弟朋友。 | if there is no generating, combining, controlling or punishing, it is surely a brother or friend. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7140 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7140*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以卦身推之。 | 这是按卦身来推。 | This is worked out by the Body. |
| 鬼生卦身为父母，卦身生鬼为子孙； | 鬼生卦身的是父母（父母爻），卦身生鬼的是子孙（子孙爻）； | A ghost that generates the Body is a parent (Resource); the Body generating the ghost is a descendant (Output); |
| 鬼克卦身为冤仇，卦身克鬼为妻妾。 | 鬼克卦身的是冤仇，卦身克鬼的是妻妾。 | a ghost controlling the Body is an enemy; the Body controlling the ghost is a wife or concubine. |
| 二者比和为兄弟朋友； | 两者比和（五行相同）的，是兄弟朋友； | Where the two are in harmony (the same element), it is Peer: brothers and friends; |
| 阴爻是妯娌姊妹之鬼。 | 阴爻的，是妯娌、姊妹的鬼。 | a yin line is the ghost of a sister-in-law or a sister. |
| 子孙，小口卑幼之鬼。 | 子孙，是小孩、晚辈的鬼。 | Output (descendants) means the ghosts of the young and juniors. |
| 父母，尊长祖宗之鬼。 | 父母，是长辈、祖宗的鬼。 | Resource (parents) means the ghosts of elders and ancestors. |
| 财爻，妻妾、阴人之鬼。 | 财爻，是妻妾、女人的鬼。 | The Wealth line means the ghosts of a wife, concubine or woman. |
| 惟鬼克身，冤家债主之鬼，必难退遣。 | 只有鬼克卦身，是冤家债主的鬼，一定难以打发。 | Only a ghost that controls the Body, the ghost of an enemy or creditor, is hard to be rid of. |
| 病亦未易安。 | 病也不容易好。 | The illness too will not easily settle. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3389–3391 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, lines 3389–3391*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此以卦身推之。 | 这是按卦身来推。 | This is worked out by the Body. |
| 鬼生卦身为父母，卦身生鬼为子孙，鬼克卦身为冤仇，卦身克鬼为妻妾，二者比合为兄弟朋友姊妹之鬼。 | 鬼生卦身的是父母，卦身生鬼的是子孙，鬼克卦身的是冤仇，卦身克鬼的是妻妾，两者比合的是兄弟朋友姊妹的鬼。 | A ghost that generates the Body is a parent (Resource), the Body generating the ghost is a descendant (Output), a ghost controlling the Body is an enemy, the Body controlling the ghost is a wife or concubine, and where the two are in harmony it is the ghost of a brother (Peer), friend or sister. |

> **按**:经文只说「身」，旧注和王洪绪都说明这是「卦身」。旧注多出的四句（阴爻是妯娌姊妹、子孙是小口卑幼、父母是尊长祖宗、财爻是妻妾阴人）和最后「惟鬼克身…病亦未易安」一句王洪绪没有。月卦身法本身，增删卜易的野鹤认为不验（见 LY.ILL.ZS.01）；他的鬼神章（LY.ILL.ZS.23–25）也没有用卦身去认是谁的鬼，而是按鬼爻的五行和旺衰认。
>
> **Note:** The verse says only 'the Body', and both the old commentary and Wang Hongxu make clear that it is the hexagram Body. The four sentences the old commentary adds (a yin line is a sister-in-law or sister, Output is the ghost of the young, Resource of elders and ancestors, the Wealth line of a wife, concubine or woman) and its last 'only a ghost that controls the Body... the illness will not easily settle' Wang Hongxu lacks. The month-Body method itself the Old Man of Yehe in the Zengshan Buyi held unreliable (see LY.ILL.ZS.01); his chapter on ghosts and spirits (LY.ILL.ZS.23-25) does not use the Body to tell whose ghost it is either, but goes by the element and strength of the Pressure line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3389 行:鬼克身冤家债主;同文 第 3389 行:身克鬼妻妾阴人;同文 第 3389 行:我去生他，卑幼儿童僧道;异文(相似 0.8) 第 3389 行:他来生我，宗祖尊长爹娘;异文(相似 0.83) 第 3389 行:若无生合克刑，必是兄弟朋友
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1379 行:鬼克身，冤家债主;同文 第 1379 行:身克鬼，妻妾阴人;同文 第 1379 行:我去生他，卑幼儿童僧道;同文 第 1379 行:他来生我，祖宗尊长爹娘;同文 第 1379 行:若无生合克刑，必是弟兄朋友

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.35、LY.ILL.HJC.101、LY.ILL.HJC.103、LY.ILL.ZS.01、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.HJC.103 鬼带刑爻必非善终之鬼：旧注按地支分辰压死、午汤火烫、酉刀箭、亥落水、寅虎噬、巳蛇伤、戌犬咬、丑牛触，旺则为神道；王洪绪说按五行所属推

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼带刑爻，一定是没有善终的鬼。怎么死的：旧注按鬼的地支分，辰是被墙壁压死，午是被热汤烈火烫死烧死，酉是被刀箭所伤，亥是投河落水，寅也许是被老虎咬死，巳是被蛇咬伤，戌是被狗咬，丑是被牛顶；旺相的成了神道，像刘郡王、猛将这样的神。王洪绪说要按鬼所属的五行去推是怎么死的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost carrying a punishing line is surely one who did not die a good death. How he died: the old commentary goes by the ghost's branch — Chen, crushed by a wall; Wu, scalded or burned by hot water or fire; You, wounded by blade or arrow; Hai, threw himself into a river or fell into water; Yin, perhaps devoured by a tiger; Si, bitten by a snake; Xu, bitten by a dog; Chou, gored by an ox — and if strong it becomes a spirit-way, like the god Prince Liu, the Fierce General. Wang Hongxu says to work out the manner of death from the element the ghost belongs to.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7142 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7142*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 刑不善终； | 鬼带刑，死得不好； | If it carries punishment, the death was not a good one; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7143 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7143*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼带刑爻，必非善终之鬼。 | 鬼带着刑爻，一定是没有善终的鬼。 | A ghost carrying a punishing line is surely one who did not die a good death. |
| 辰是墙壁压死鬼，午是汤火烧烫鬼，酉是刀箭所伤鬼，亥是投河落水鬼。 | 辰是被墙壁压死的鬼，午是被开水和火烧烫死的鬼，酉是被刀箭伤死的鬼，亥是投河落水而死的鬼。 | Chen is a ghost crushed by a wall, Wu a ghost scalded or burned by hot water or fire, You a ghost wounded by blade or arrow, Hai a ghost who threw himself into a river or fell into water. |
| 寅或虎噬，巳或蛇伤，戌或犬咬，丑或牛触。 | 寅，也许是被老虎咬死；巳，也许是被蛇咬伤；戌，也许是被狗咬；丑，也许是被牛顶。 | Yin may be one devoured by a tiger, Si one bitten by a snake, Xu one bitten by a dog, Chou one gored by an ox. |
| 旺则为神道，刘郡王猛将之神。 | 旺相的，就成了神道，像刘郡王、猛将这样的神。 | If strong, it becomes a spirit-way, such as the god Prince Liu, the Fierce General. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3393 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3393*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼带刑爻，必非善终之鬼，当以五行所属推其何死鬼； | 鬼带刑爻，一定是没有善终的鬼，要按它所属的五行推是怎么死的鬼； | A ghost carrying a punishing line is surely one who did not die a good death; work out what kind of death from the element it belongs to; |

> **打架**:怎么推是怎么死的：旧注按鬼爻的地支分（辰压死、午烫死、酉刀箭、亥落水、寅虎、巳蛇、戌犬、丑牛）；王洪绪说「当以五行所属推其何死鬼」，按五行推，不按地支。两种办法给出的名目不完全重合（例如增删卜易 LY.ILL.ZS.23 按五行说金是刀剑亡鬼、木是刑杖悬梁、水是水死、火是汤火焚烧、土是墙倒屋塌，和旧注的辰午酉亥有部分重合）。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How to work out how the ghost died: the old commentary goes by the branch of the Pressure line (Chen crushed, Wu scalded, You blade and arrow, Hai water, Yin tiger, Si snake, Xu dog, Chou ox); Wang Hongxu says 'work out the manner of death from the element it belongs to', by element and not by branch. The two methods do not give exactly the same categories (for instance the Zengshan Buyi, LY.ILL.ZS.23, goes by element — Metal a ghost of blade and sword, Wood of the staff or the hanging beam, Water of drowning, Fire of hot water and burning, Earth of a collapsed wall — which overlaps in part with the old commentary's Chen, Wu, You and Hai). The books do not decide.

> **按**:「刑爻」这里指带刑的爻，书里没有列出哪些是刑。「刘郡王猛将之神」是民间信奉的神，书里没有解释，按字面译作刘郡王、猛将。这一条的经文只有「刑不善终」四字，和下一条「绝则无祀」在王洪绪那里连成一段注。
>
> **Note:** 'A punishing line' means a line carrying punishment (xing); the book does not list which they are. 'Prince Liu, the Fierce General' is a spirit worshipped among the people and is not explained in the book, so is rendered literally. The verse here is only the four characters 'punishment, no good death', and in Wang Hongxu it forms one passage of commentary with the next entry, 'extinction, no one to sacrifice'.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.102、LY.ILL.HJC.104、LY.ILL.ZS.23

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.104 鬼不上卦看它伏在哪一爻下：伏父母下是家先、伏子孙下是小口小儿、伏空下是无收管的孤魂野鬼，临绝爻是无祀之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻不上卦，就看它伏在哪一爻下面，便知道是什么鬼祟：伏在父母爻下，是家里已故的祖先；伏在子孙（福神）爻下，是小孩一类；伏在空亡的爻下，是没有人收管的鬼，是孤魂野鬼；如果临在绝爻上，是没有人祭祀的鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is absent from the hexagram, see under which line it is hidden and you will know what kind of ghost it is: hidden under the Resource line, the family's forebears; under the Output (fortune spirit) line, children and the like; under a Void line, a ghost that no one keeps, a lonely wandering ghost; and if it meets an Extinction line, a ghost to whom no one sacrifices.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7145 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7145*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 绝则无祀。 | 鬼临绝，就是没有人祭祀的鬼。 | If it meets Extinction, there is no one to make offerings to it. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7146 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7146*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼不上卦，看伏何爻下，便知是何鬼崇。 | 鬼爻不上卦，就看它伏在哪一爻下面，便知道是什么鬼崇。 | If the ghost is absent from the hexagram, look under which line it is hidden, and you will know what kind of ghostly influence it is. |
| 如伏父下，为家先。 | 如果伏在父母爻下，是家里已故的祖先。 | If it is hidden under the Resource line, it is the family's forebears. |
| 伏福下，为小口类。 | 伏在子孙（福神）爻下，是小孩这一类。 | If hidden under the Output (fortune spirit) line, it is of the kind of small children. |
| 若伏空下，乃是无收管之鬼，是孤魂野鬼。 | 如果伏在空亡爻下，是没有人收管的鬼，是孤魂野鬼。 | If it is hidden under a Void line, it is a ghost with no one to keep it, a lonely wandering ghost. |
| 若临绝爻，乃无祀之鬼。 | 如果临在绝爻上，是没有人祭祀的鬼。 | If it meets an Extinction line, it is a ghost with no one to sacrifice to it. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3393 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3393*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 不上卦看伏何爻下，便知是何鬼祟，如伏父下为家先，伏福下为小儿类。 | 鬼不上卦，看它伏在哪一爻下面，便知是什么鬼祟：伏在父母爻下是家先，伏在福神（子孙）下是小孩一类。 | If it is absent from the hexagram, see under which line it is hidden and you will know what ghostly influence it is: hidden under the Resource line, the family's forebears; hidden under the Output (fortune spirit) line, children and the like. |

**书中卦例** — 《卜筮正宗》第 4527 行 · *Case from the book; Bushi Zhengzong, line 4527*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 寅月戊辰日，占病有何鬼神为祸，得小畜卦： | 寅月戊辰日，占病人有什么鬼神作祸，得小畜卦： | In the Yin month, on the Wuchen day, he divined what ghost or spirit was making trouble in an illness, and obtained Xiaoxu: |

**书中卦例** — 《卜筮正宗》第 4534 行 · *Case from the book; Bushi Zhengzong, line 4534*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 断曰：凡卜鬼神以官爻为用，今官鬼伏辰爻之下，与飞神作合，又得日辰合住。 | 断说：凡是占鬼神，以官爻为用；现在官鬼伏在辰爻下面，和飞神合在一起，又被日辰合住。 | Judgment: in all divining of ghosts and spirits the Pressure line is the Subject; now Pressure is hidden beneath the Chen line, combines with the flying line, and is further held by a combination with the Day. |
| 予想伏合乃藏匿之象，酉金是正气之神，第三爻为房室，即断曰：汝家房中藏有神像作祟。 | 我想，伏着又被合住，是藏起来的象；酉金是正气之神；第三爻是房室；就断说：你家房里藏着一尊神像在作祟。 | I thought that being hidden and combined is the image of concealment; You Metal is a spirit of proper energy; the third line is the bedroom; so I judged: a statue of a spirit is hidden in a room of your house and is making trouble. |
| 彼曰：有铜达摩祖师在匣中。 | 他说：有一尊铜的达摩祖师像在匣子里。 | He said: there is a bronze figure of Patriarch Damo in a box. |
| 命其送于寺内，其痛亦愈。 | 叫他把像送到寺里去，他的病痛也好了。 | I told him to send it to a temple, and his pain was cured as well. |

> **按**:这一条经文只有「绝则无祀」四字；王洪绪把它和上一条「刑不善终」并成一段注，后半是「不上卦看伏何爻下」，和旧注相同。卜筮正宗后面的书中例（小畜卦）用的是伏鬼的看法：官鬼酉金伏在第三爻辰土下面，辰酉相合，又被戊辰日合住；但断「房中藏着神像」用的是「伏合是藏匿之象、酉金是正气之神、第三爻是房室」，不是上面旧注这条里的父母下是家先、子孙下是小儿。
>
> **Note:** The verse here is only the four characters 'extinction, no one to sacrifice'; Wang Hongxu joins it with the previous entry, 'punishment, no good death', into one passage of commentary, whose second half, 'if absent from the hexagram, see under which line it is hidden', is the same as the old commentary. The case that follows in the Bushi Zhengzong (Xiaoxu) uses the reading of a hidden ghost: the You Metal Pressure lies hidden beneath the third line, Chen Earth; Chen and You combine and the Wuchen day also holds it in combination; but the judgment 'a statue is hidden in the room' rests on 'hidden and combined is the image of concealment, You Metal is a spirit of proper energy, the third line is the bedroom', not on the old commentary's 'under Resource the family forebears, under Output children'.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.103、LY.ILL.HJC.105、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.HJC.105 鬼临日月定然新死亡灵：卦无官鬼而日辰是鬼，必是新死的人为祸；日辰是鬼而卦中又有鬼，是近日新许的愿未酬

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼落在日辰上，一定是刚死不久的亡灵：卦里没有官鬼、日辰是鬼的，一定是刚死的人在作祸；日辰是鬼、卦里又有鬼的，是因为祈祷，近来新许下的愿还没有还。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost that falls on the Day is surely a newly dead spirit: if there is no Pressure in the hexagram and the Day is the ghost, a newly dead person is surely making trouble; if the Day is the ghost and the hexagram also has a ghost, it is because of a prayer: a vow newly made lately has not been paid.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7148 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7148*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如临日月，定然新死亡灵； | 如果鬼落在日、月上，一定是刚死不久的亡灵； | If the ghost falls on the Day or the Month, it is surely a newly dead spirit; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7149 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7149*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦无官鬼，而日辰是鬼者，必然新死亡人为祸。 | 卦里没有官鬼，而日辰是鬼的，一定是刚死的人在作祸。 | If there is no Pressure in the hexagram but the Day is the ghost, a newly dead person is surely making trouble. |
| 若日辰是鬼，而卦中又有鬼，必因祈祷，而近日新许之愿未酬。 | 如果日辰是鬼，而卦里又有鬼，一定是因为祈祷，近来新许下的愿还没有还。 | If the Day is the ghost and the hexagram also has a ghost, it is surely because of a prayer: a vow newly made lately has not been paid. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3395 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3395*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦无官鬼而日辰是鬼者，必然新死亡灵为祸。 | 卦里没有官鬼而日辰是鬼的，一定是刚死的亡灵在作祸。 | If there is no Pressure in the hexagram but the Day is the ghost, a newly dead spirit is surely making trouble. |
| 若日辰是鬼，而卦中又有鬼，是近日新许之愿未酬也。 | 如果日辰是鬼，而卦里又有鬼，是近来新许下的愿还没有还。 | If the Day is the ghost and the hexagram also has a ghost, it is a vow newly made lately that has not been paid. |

> **按**:经文说的是「日月」，旧注和王洪绪都只讲日辰是鬼，没有说月建是鬼怎么办。旧注比王洪绪多一层原因：「必因祈祷」。这一条和病体章里「日辰带鬼，亟为祈祷」（LY.ILL.HJC.49、LY.ILL.HJC.50）是同一个看法，那里讲怎样认是哪一路神。
>
> **Note:** The verse speaks of 'Day and Month', but both the old commentary and Wang Hongxu speak only of the Day being the ghost and do not say what to do when the Month is. The old commentary gives one more reason than Wang Hongxu: 'surely because of a prayer'. This entry is the same view as 'when the Day carries the ghost, pray urgently' in the chapter on the patient's condition (LY.ILL.HJC.49 and LY.ILL.HJC.50), where the question of which spirit it is is treated.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3395 行:如临日月，定然新死亡灵
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1382 行:如临日月，定然新死亡灵

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.104、LY.ILL.HJC.106、LY.ILL.HJC.49、LY.ILL.HJC.50

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.106 鬼自入墓刑决是狱中囚犯：未日占得木爻官鬼谓鬼自入墓，是死于囹圄的囚人之鬼，更带刑爻必死于非命；旺相发动则是庙神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼自己入墓、带刑，一定是关在牢里的囚犯的鬼：比如在未日占卦，得到木爻的官鬼，叫做鬼自己入墓，一定是死在牢狱里的人的鬼；再带刑爻，一定是死于非命，或者死在坟墓之间；旺相又发动的，就是庙里的神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost that enters its own tomb or is punished is surely the ghost of a prisoner in jail: if one divines on a Wei day and gets a Wood Pressure line, the ghost is said to enter its own tomb, and it is surely the ghost of one who died in prison; if it also carries a punishing line, he surely died an unnatural death or died among graves; and if strong and moving, it is a temple god.

**《黄金策·鬼神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7151 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Ghosts and spirits", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7151*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 自入墓刑，决是狱中囚犯。 | 鬼自己入墓、带刑，一定是关在牢里的囚犯。 | If the ghost enters its own tomb or is punished, it is surely a prisoner in jail. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7152 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7152*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如未日占卦，得木爻官鬼，谓鬼自入墓。 | 比如在未日占卦，得到木爻的官鬼，叫做鬼自己入墓。 | For example, if one divines on a Wei day and gets a Wood Pressure line, the ghost is said to enter its own tomb. |
| 必是死于囹圄囚人之鬼。 | 一定是死在牢狱里的囚犯的鬼。 | It is surely the ghost of a prisoner who died in jail. |
| 更带刑爻，必死于非命，或死于坟墓间者。 | 再带刑爻，一定是死于非命，或者死在坟墓之间的。 | If it also carries a punishing line, he surely died an unnatural death, or died among graves. |
| 旺相发动，则是庙神。 | 旺相又发动的，就是庙里的神。 | If strong and moving, it is a temple god. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3397 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 3397*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如未日占卦，得木爻官鬼入墓，必是死于囹圄囚狱之鬼； | 比如在未日占卦，得到木爻官鬼入墓，一定是死在牢狱里的囚徒的鬼； | For example, if one divines on a Wei day and gets a Wood Pressure line entering its tomb, it is surely the ghost of one who died in prison; |
| 旺相发动则是庙神。 | 旺相发动的，就是庙神。 | if strong and moving, it is a temple god. |

> **按**:「囹圄」是监狱（卜筮大全篇末生僻字注）。「自入墓」的例子是：未是木的墓，在未日占卦，得木爻官鬼，被日辰带进了墓。旧注多一句「更带刑爻，必死于非命，或死于坟墓间者」，王洪绪没有。
>
> **Note:** 'Lingyu' is a prison (the book's glossary at the end of the chapter). The example of 'entering its own tomb' is: Wei is the tomb of Wood, so on a Wei day, with a Wood Pressure line, the ghost is taken into its tomb by the Day. The old commentary has one more sentence, 'if it also carries a punishing line, he surely died an unnatural death or died among graves', which Wang Hongxu lacks.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3397 行:自入墓刑，决是狱中囚犯
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1382 行:自入墓刑，决是狱中囚犯

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.105、LY.ILL.HJC.107
