## LY.ILL.YM.49 金鬼的来历：生前好勇（性属金），或亡于干戈锋镝、病丧于喉肺、为义为利，是成鬼之因；成鬼能善则上帝命其所司，位西方、权秋令、主兵事，按金象考

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前好勇的鬼，是就它的性情说，所以属金；或者死在兵器锋镝之下，以及病死在喉、肺，为义、为利而死，这是就它怎样成鬼说，所以也归金类。成了鬼又能行善的，上帝命它掌管的事，如果位在西方、权在秋令、主管兵事，就按金象来考察。注说：这是察看鬼神的来历。金鬼一定生前好勇，就性情说，它死在刀兵之下；就它成为鬼神说，这位神掌管什么事，可能属水、属火、属土、属金、属木；就所掌管的属性说，比如关帝、岳王的神勇和义属金，七煞、丧煞，体属金、掌管杀伐，也属金。遁甲里的七煞是庚，这就叫体属金。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost who loved courage in life is Metal, speaking of its nature; or one who died under weapons and arrowheads, or of illness of the throat or lungs, or over righteousness or profit, is spoken of as how it became a ghost, and so is also classed as Metal. One who, having become a ghost, can do good, and whom the Supreme Lord has appointed to a charge, if its seat is in the west, its authority in the autumn season and its charge in military affairs, is examined by the Metal image. The note says: this is to examine where the ghost or spirit came from. A Metal ghost must have loved courage in life; by nature, that ghost died by blade and weapon; as to its becoming a ghost or spirit, what this spirit governs may be Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood; as to what its charge belongs to, for example, the valour and righteousness of Guan and Yue belong to Metal, and the Seven Killings and the Mourning Killing, whose body is Metal and whose charge is killing, belong to Metal. The Seven Killings of the Dunjia is Geng, which is what is meant by a body of Metal.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1278 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1278*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前好勇之鬼，言其性故属金，或亡于干戈锋镝之下，及病丧于喉、肺、义、利，言其成鬼，故金类之。 | 生前好勇的鬼，是就它的性情说，所以属金；或者死在兵器锋镝之下，以及病死在喉、肺，为义、为利而死，这是就它怎样成鬼说，所以归入金类。 | A ghost who loved courage in life is Metal, speaking of its nature; or one who died under weapons and arrowheads, or of illness of the throat or lungs, or over righteousness or profit, is spoken of as how it became a ghost, and so is classed as Metal. |
| 成鬼而能有善，上帝命其所司，若位于西方，权于秋令，主于兵事，则金象而考也。 | 成了鬼又能行善的，上帝命它掌管的事，如果位在西方、权在秋令、主管兵事，就按金象来考察。 | One who, having become a ghost, can do good, and whom the Supreme Lord has appointed to a charge, if its seat is in the west, its authority in the autumn season and its charge in military affairs, is examined by the Metal image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1279 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1279*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此察鬼神所由来。 | 这是察看鬼神是怎么来的。 | This is to examine where the ghost or spirit came from. |
| 金鬼，其鬼必生前好勇，以性言之，其鬼亡于刀兵； | 金鬼，这个鬼生前一定好勇，就性情说，它死在刀兵之下； | A Metal ghost must have loved courage in life; by nature, that ghost died by blade and weapon; |
| 以成鬼神言之，其神所司何事，或水或火或土或金或木； | 就它成为鬼神说，这位神所掌管的是什么事，可能属水、属火、属土、属金、属木； | as to its becoming a ghost or spirit, what this spirit governs may be Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood; |
| 以所司属言之，如关岳神勇义属金，七煞丧煞，体金司煞属金也。 | 就所掌管的属性说，比如关帝、岳王的神勇和义属金，七煞、丧煞，体属金、掌管杀伐的也属金。 | as to what its charge belongs to, for example, the valour and righteousness of Guan and Yue belong to Metal, and the Seven Killings and the Mourning Killing, whose body is Metal and whose charge is killing, belong to Metal. |
| 遁甲七煞乃庚，是谓体金。 | 遁甲里的七煞就是庚，这就叫体属金。 | The Seven Killings of the Dunjia is Geng, which is what is meant by a body of Metal. |

> **按**:这一条讲的是 LY.ILL.YM.42 里「性、司、成」三样怎么用。「性」是生前的性情（好勇），「成」是怎么成鬼的（死于刀兵），「司」是成神以后上帝派它管什么（西方、秋令、兵事）；三样各自可以属金，也可以三样属的不一样（注里说「所司」可以属水火土金木任何一行）。「体金」是本体属金：七煞在遁甲里是庚，庚是金，所以七煞体金；关、岳靠神勇与义归金，是靠所司的性质。
>
> **Note:** This entry shows how the three of 'nature, charge and origin' in LY.ILL.YM.42 are used. 'Nature' is the disposition in life (love of courage), 'origin' is how it became a ghost (died by blade), and 'charge' is what the Supreme Lord set it to govern after it became a spirit (the west, autumn, military affairs); each of the three can be Metal, or the three can belong to different phases (the note says the 'charge' may belong to any of Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood). 'Body of Metal' means the substance is Metal: the Seven Killings in the Dunjia is Geng, and Geng is Metal, so the Seven Killings has a body of Metal, while Guan and Yue go under Metal through the nature of their charge, valour and righteousness.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.48、LY.ILL.YM.50、LY.ILL.YM.42、LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.YY.28

---

## LY.ILL.YM.50 水鬼的来历：生前谋利于水，或亡于江海风波汤卤，病丧于腰肾茶酒，凡司河泊舟楫，按水象考；出身显圣者亦可推而变化无穷

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前靠水谋利的，或者死在江海风浪、开水卤水之中，以及病死在腰、肾，因茶、酒而死的，凡是掌管河泊舟船的，就按水象来考察。注说：凡是靠出身、来历显了圣的，也可以这样推，变化就无穷无尽了；大慈在南海显道，三官从龙神那里受生，都仿照这样。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who sought profit by water in life, or died in river and sea, wind and waves, or scalding water and brine, or of illness of the waist and kidneys, or over tea and wine, and whose charge is rivers, moorings, boats and oars, is examined by the Water image. The note says: those who manifested as holy beings from their origins can likewise be worked out, and then the variations are without end; Great Compassion manifested the Way in the Southern Sea, and the Three Officials received their birth from the Dragon Spirit; follow this.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1280 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1280*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前谋利于水，或亡于江海风波汤卤之中，及病丧于腰、肾、茶、酒，凡司河泊舟楫，则水象而考也。 | 生前靠水谋利的，或者死在江海风浪、开水卤水之中，以及病死在腰、肾，因茶、酒而死的，凡是掌管河泊舟船的，就按水象来考察。 | One who sought profit by water in life, or died in river and sea, wind and waves, or scalding water and brine, or of illness of the waist and kidneys, or over tea and wine, and whose charge is rivers, moorings, boats and oars, is examined by the Water image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1281 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1281*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡出身显圣，亦可推之，则变化无穷矣。 | 凡是靠出身显了圣的，也可以这样推，变化就无穷无尽了。 | Those who manifested as holy beings from their origins can likewise be worked out, and then the variations are without end. |
| 大慈显道南海，三官生受龙神，仿此。 | 大慈在南海显道，三官从龙神那里受生，都仿照这样。 | Great Compassion manifested the Way in the Southern Sea, and the Three Officials received their birth from the Dragon Spirit; follow this. |

> **按**:「凡出身显圣」原文没有再解释，这里译成「凡是靠出身显了圣的」。「大慈」指大慈大悲的观世音；「三官生受龙神」的读法原文没有讲，这里按字面译。这一条和金的那一条（LY.ILL.YM.49）是平行的：性（谋利于水）、成（亡于江海风波）、司（河泊舟楫），最后一句提醒神的来历还有另一条路——靠出身显圣，那就没有一定之规了。
>
> **Note:** The original does not explain 'those who manifested as holy beings from their origins', and it is rendered as it stands. 'Great Compassion' is the Guanyin of great compassion and mercy; how 'the Three Officials received birth from the Dragon Spirit' is to be read is not stated, and it is translated literally. This entry runs parallel to the Metal one (LY.ILL.YM.49): nature (seeking profit by water), origin (dying in river and sea) and charge (rivers, moorings, boats); the last sentence warns that a spirit may also come by another road, manifesting from its origins, for which there is no fixed rule.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.49、LY.ILL.YM.51、LY.ILL.YM.45、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

---

## LY.ILL.YM.51 木鬼的来历：生前陶情花乡、立业桑麻，或亡于笞杖树木之下、丧于捆缚疯癫，凡司栽植砍伐，按木象考；两可不能一见则审累重（南木神应水官者体木司水）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前在花乡里陶冶性情、靠桑麻立业的，或者死在笞杖、树木之下，死于捆绑、疯癫的，凡是掌管栽种砍伐的，就按木象来考察。注说：凡是两种都说得通、不能只取一种的，要审察哪种分量重，比如南木神对应水官的，是体属木、掌管水。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life took his pleasure in the land of flowers and made his living from mulberry and hemp, or died under the rod or beneath a tree, or by binding or madness, and whose charge is planting and felling, is examined by the Wood image. The note says: where either reading is possible and one cannot be settled on by a single look, one must weigh which carries more; for example, the Southern Wood God answering a Water Pressure line has a body of Wood and a charge of Water.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1283–1284 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1283–1284*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前陶情花乡，立业桑麻，或亡于笞杖树木之下，丧于捆缚、疯癫，凡司栽植砍伐，则木象而考也。 | 生前在花乡里陶冶性情、靠桑麻立业的，或者死在笞杖、树木之下，死于捆绑、疯癫的，凡是掌管栽种砍伐的，就按木象来考察。 | One who in life took his pleasure in the land of flowers and made his living from mulberry and hemp, or died under the rod or beneath a tree, or by binding or madness, and whose charge is planting and felling, is examined by the Wood image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1285 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1285*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡两可不能一见，当审累重，如南木神应水官者，体木司水也。 | 凡是两种都说得通、不能只取一种的，应当审察哪种分量重，比如南木神对应水官的，是体属木、掌管水。 | Where either reading is possible and one cannot be settled on by a single look, one must weigh which carries more; for example, the Southern Wood God answering a Water Pressure line has a body of Wood and a charge of Water. |

> **按**:这一条的注比正文重要：它给了一条判别规则，当一位神的「体」（本身属哪一行）和它的「所司」（掌管的属哪一行）不是同一行，不能只取一个，要看哪个分量重；例是南木神，本身是木神，可是掌管水，所以官鬼是水时要按「体木司水」去读。这是对 LY.ILL.YM.49 里「性、司、成」三样可以各属一行的补充。
>
> **Note:** The note of this entry matters more than the text: it gives a rule of discrimination, that when a spirit's 'body' (which phase it is itself) and its 'charge' (which phase what it governs is) are not the same phase, one cannot take only one, and must weigh which carries more; the example is the Southern Wood God, a Wood spirit by nature yet governing water, so that when the Pressure line is Water it is read as 'body Wood, charge Water'. It supplements what LY.ILL.YM.49 says, that nature, charge and origin may each belong to a different phase.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.50、LY.ILL.YM.52、LY.ILL.YM.46、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

---

## LY.ILL.YM.52 火鬼的来历：生为炉煅之事，死于烈焰疮痍血衂胎产，凡司鼎釜孕育，按火象考；宜推而广之

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前做炉锻的事，死在烈火、疮痍、流血、胎产中的，凡是掌管鼎釜和孕育的，就按火象来考察。注说：应当推广开来，用五行、六神、诸星去配。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life worked at furnace and forge, or died in raging flame, sores and wounds, bleeding, or childbirth, and whose charge is cauldrons and pots and pregnancy, is examined by the Fire image. The note says: one should extend it further, matching it with the five phases, the six spirits and the various stars.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1286 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1286*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生为炉煅之事，死于烈焰疮痍，血衂胎产，凡司鼎釜孕育，则火象而考也。 | 生前做炉锻的事，死在烈火、疮痍、流血、胎产中的，凡是掌管鼎釜和孕育的，就按火象来考察。 | One who in life worked at furnace and forge, or died in raging flame, sores and wounds, bleeding, or childbirth, and whose charge is cauldrons and pots and pregnancy, is examined by the Fire image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1287 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1287*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 宜推而广之，五行六神诸星配之。 | 应当推广开来，用五行、六神、诸星去配。 | One should extend it further, matching it with the five phases, the six spirits and the various stars. |

> **按**:「血衂」的「衂」同「衄」，流血、鼻出血；这里译作流血。这一条和 LY.ILL.YM.49–LY.ILL.YM.51、YM.53 是平行的，只是火的注只有一句「宜推而广之」，没有像木那条一样给出判别规则。
>
> **Note:** 'Xue nu' (bleeding) uses a variant of the character for nosebleed and is rendered as bleeding. This entry runs parallel to LY.ILL.YM.49 to YM.51 and YM.53, except that the note on Fire has only the one sentence 'one should extend it further', and gives no rule of discrimination as the one on Wood does.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.51、LY.ILL.YM.53、LY.ILL.YM.47、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.53 土鬼的来历：生前土技农耕，死于瘟疫脾胃土石之难，凡司动作，按土象考；惟灵显东南

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前做土工、务农耕作的，死在瘟疫、脾胃的病、土石之难中的，凡是掌管动土动作的，就按土象来考察。注说：只是灵验多显现在东南。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life had an earth craft or farmed, or died of epidemic, illness of the spleen and stomach, or the calamity of earth and stone, and whose charge is works and movement, is examined by the Earth image. The note says: only, its power is manifest chiefly in the southeast.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1288 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1288*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前土技农耕，死于瘟疫脾胃土石之难，凡司动作，则土象而考也。 | 生前做土工、务农耕作的，死在瘟疫、脾胃的病、土石之难中的，凡是掌管动土动作的，就按土象来考察。 | One who in life had an earth craft or farmed, or died of epidemic, illness of the spleen and stomach, or the calamity of earth and stone, and whose charge is works and movement, is examined by the Earth image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1289 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1289*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 惟灵显东南。 | 只是灵验多显现在东南。 | Only, its power is manifest chiefly in the southeast. |

> **按**:「惟灵显东南」承接土神那条（LY.ILL.YM.48）：东南土薄，土曹的名号多，土鬼的灵验也多显在东南。这一条和 LY.ILL.YM.49–LY.ILL.YM.52 合起来，是五行各自的「性、成、司」的一张表。
>
> **Note:** 'Only its power is manifest in the southeast' follows the entry on Earth spirits (LY.ILL.YM.48): in the southeast the earth is thin and the bureau titles are many, and the power of an Earth ghost is chiefly manifest there too. Together with LY.ILL.YM.49 to YM.52 this makes a table of the 'nature, origin and charge' of each of the five phases.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.52、LY.ILL.YM.54、LY.ILL.YM.48、LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.54 以动爻当鬼、按数推：阳男阴女、午南子北；一动一鬼、三动三鬼；爻与鬼皆动，则以鬼所属之方为方、鬼所属之数为数（例：鬼动值土断五鬼）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:有一种办法是拿动爻当鬼：阳的是男、阴的是女，午来自南方、子来自北方；动一爻就是一个鬼，动三爻就是三个鬼；如果爻和鬼都动，就以鬼所属的方位为方位、鬼所属的数为数。注说：鬼静而别的爻动，考察动的爻；鬼动而别的爻也动，考察鬼爻；动的按爻数算，鬼按数算，比如鬼动而值土，就断成五鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** There is a method of taking the moving lines as the ghosts: yang is male and yin female, Wu comes from the south and Zi from the north; one moving line is one ghost and three moving lines are three ghosts; if the lines and the ghost all move, take the direction the ghost belongs to as the direction and the number the ghost belongs to as the number. The note says: if the ghost is still and other lines move, examine the moving lines; if the ghost moves and other lines move too, examine the Pressure line; the moving ones are counted by lines and the ghost by number, so that if the ghost moves and falls on Earth it is judged five ghosts.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1290 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1290*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 法有以动为鬼，阳男阴女之分，午南子北之来，一动一鬼，三动三鬼，爻以鬼皆动，则以鬼属之方为方，鬼属之数为数也。 | 有一种办法是拿动爻当鬼：阳的是男、阴的是女，午来自南方、子来自北方；动一爻就是一个鬼，动三爻就是三个鬼；如果爻和鬼都动，就以鬼所属的方位为方位、鬼所属的数为数。 | There is a method of taking the moving lines as the ghosts: yang is male and yin female, Wu comes from the south and Zi from the north; one moving line is one ghost and three moving lines are three ghosts; if the lines and the ghost all move, take the direction the ghost belongs to as the direction and the number the ghost belongs to as the number. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1291 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1291*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼静爻动考动爻，鬼动爻动考鬼爻，动与爻计，鬼与数计，如鬼动值土，断五鬼也。 | 鬼静而别的爻动，就考察动爻；鬼动而别的爻也动，就考察鬼爻；动的按爻数算，鬼按数算，比如鬼动而值土，就断成五鬼。 | If the ghost is still and other lines move, examine the moving lines; if the ghost moves and other lines move too, examine the Pressure line; the moving ones are counted by lines and the ghost by number, so that if the ghost moves and falls on Earth it is judged five ghosts. |

> **按**:「一动一鬼，三动三鬼」数的是动爻的条数；「鬼所属之数」是五行的数，例子里土断五鬼，正是通常的生成数里土为五（水一、火二、木三、金四、土五），但这一段里书没有列出这张表。「午南子北」是把午配南、子配北，和 LY.ILL.YM.29 里离在南、坎在北是同一种配法。
>
> **Note:** 'One moving line, one ghost; three moving, three ghosts' counts the number of moving lines; 'the number the ghost belongs to' is the number of its phase, and in the example an Earth ghost is judged five, which matches the usual generating numbers in which Earth is five (Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five), though the book does not list these in this passage. 'Wu the south, Zi the north' assigns Wu to the south and Zi to the north, the same assignment as Li in the south and Kan in the north in LY.ILL.YM.29.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.53、LY.ILL.YM.55、LY.ILL.YM.29、LY.ILL.HJC.94

## LY.ILL.YM.55 考鬼的四种情形：鬼爻发动考其宫、卦爻俱静求之外卦、无鬼索伏考其飞爻、伏鬼若空则无鬼祟惟修德；此专考鬼、非断神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻发动，就考察它所在的宫；卦爻都静，就从外卦去找；卦里没有鬼，就去找伏着的鬼，考察它上面的飞爻；伏鬼如果又落空，就致力于修德。注说：鬼爻独发，用八宫来考察这个鬼；爻和鬼都静，用外卦来考察；鬼爻隐伏，用鬼上面的飞爻来考察；鬼伏着又落空，就是没有鬼祟，只要修德就行了。这一节是专门考察鬼，不是判断神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line moves, examine its palace; if the hexagram and lines are all still, seek it in the outer trigram; if there is no ghost, look for the hidden one and examine the flying line above it; if the hidden ghost is also Void, devote oneself to cultivating virtue. The note says: if the Pressure line moves alone, examine the ghost by the eight palaces; if the lines and the ghost are all still, examine it by the outer trigram; if the Pressure line is hidden, examine it by the flying line above the ghost; if the hidden ghost is again Void, there is no haunting by a ghost, and one need only cultivate virtue. This section is solely to examine the ghost, not to judge the spirit.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1292 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1292*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻发动，考之宫也，卦爻俱静，求之外也，无鬼索伏，考之飞爻，伏鬼若空，务之修德也。 | 鬼爻发动，就考察它所在的宫；卦爻都静，就从外卦去找；卦里没有鬼，就去找伏着的鬼，考察它上面的飞爻；伏鬼如果又落空，就致力于修德。 | If the Pressure line moves, examine its palace; if the hexagram and lines are all still, seek it in the outer trigram; if there is no ghost, look for the hidden one and examine the flying line above it; if the hidden ghost is also Void, devote oneself to cultivating virtue. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1293 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1293*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻独发，以八宫考鬼，爻与鬼皆静，以外卦考鬼，鬼爻隐伏，以鬼上飞爻考鬼，鬼伏复空，乃无鬼祟，惟修德而已。 | 鬼爻独自发动，用八宫来考察这个鬼；爻和鬼都静，用外卦来考察这个鬼；鬼爻隐伏，用鬼上面的飞爻来考察；鬼伏着又落空，就是没有鬼祟，只要修德就行了。 | If the Pressure line moves alone, examine the ghost by the eight palaces; if the lines and the ghost are all still, examine it by the outer trigram; if the Pressure line is hidden, examine it by the flying line above the ghost; if the hidden ghost is again Void, there is no haunting by a ghost, and one need only cultivate virtue. |
| 此专考鬼，非断神也。 | 这一节是专门考察鬼，不是判断神。 | This is solely to examine the ghost, not to judge the spirit. |

> **按**:这一条和 LY.ILL.YM.29「病从何起」是同一套四步（动看宫、静看外卦、无鬼看伏鬼的飞爻），只是那里问的是病从哪个方向来，这里问的是作祟的是什么。第四步「伏鬼若空」得出的结论是没有鬼祟，只有修德，这是全章「祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身」（LY.ILL.YM.64）那个结论的一个具体出口。注里最后一句「此专考鬼，非断神」把这一节和下一节（LY.ILL.YM.56 讲专断神）分开：前者是找到那个鬼，后者是给它定神名。
>
> **Note:** This entry is the same four steps as LY.ILL.YM.29, 'where the illness rose from' (moving, look at the palace; still, look at the outer trigram; no ghost, look at the flying line above a hidden ghost), except that there the question was from what direction the illness came, and here it is what is doing the haunting. The conclusion of the fourth step, 'if the hidden ghost is also Void', is that there is no haunting and only virtue remains, which is one concrete outlet of the chapter's final conclusion, 'of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself' (LY.ILL.YM.64). The note's last sentence, 'solely to examine the ghost, not to judge the spirit', separates this section from the next (LY.ILL.YM.56, which is solely for judging the spirit): the first finds the ghost and the second names the spirit.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.54、LY.ILL.YM.56、LY.ILL.YM.29、LY.ILL.YM.64、LY.ILL.HJC.104

---

## LY.ILL.YM.56 断神的五种专法：一官鬼专五行、二官鬼专六神、动官鬼专八卦、乱官鬼专诸星、世官鬼专六爻——卦有一官以五行、二官以六神（下临白虎为武神、上临青龙为树神）、动鬼以八宫（鬼动乾天神、鬼动坤地祗）、乱动以何星临官

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里只有一个官鬼，专门用五行来断；有两个官鬼，专门用六神来断；官鬼发动的，专门用八卦；官鬼乱动的，专门用诸星；官鬼在世爻上的，专门用六爻。注说：卦里有一个官，用五行取象；有两个官，用六神取象，比如下面的官临白虎，就说是武神，上面的官临青龙，就说是树神；有动的鬼，用八宫取象，比如鬼在乾宫动，就说是天神，鬼在坤宫动，就说是地祇；卦爻乱动、或者其中有官在动，就看哪颗星临在官上来取象。这一节是专门断神的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With one Pressure line, go by the five phases alone; with two Pressure lines, go by the six spirits alone; with a moving Pressure line, go by the eight trigrams alone; with Pressure lines moving in disorder, go by the various stars alone; with the Pressure line on the Self, go by the six lines alone. The note says: where the hexagram has one Pressure line, take the image by the five phases; where it has two, take the image by the six spirits, so that if the lower one carries the White Tiger it is called a martial spirit, and if the upper one carries the Azure Dragon it is called a tree spirit; where it has a moving ghost, take the image by the eight palaces, so that a ghost moving in Qian is called a heavenly spirit and in Kun an earthly deity; where the lines move in disorder, or a Pressure line among them moves, take the image by whichever star sits on the Pressure line. This section is solely for judging the spirit.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1294 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1294*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫一官鬼而专于五行，二官鬼而专于六神，动官鬼而专八卦，乱官鬼而专诸星，世官鬼而专六爻也。 | 卦里只有一个官鬼，专门用五行来断；有两个官鬼，专门用六神；官鬼发动的，专门用八卦；官鬼乱动的，专门用诸星；官鬼在世爻上的，专门用六爻。 | With one Pressure line, go by the five phases alone; with two Pressure lines, go by the six spirits alone; with a moving Pressure line, go by the eight trigrams alone; with Pressure lines moving in disorder, go by the various stars alone; with the Pressure line on the Self, go by the six lines alone. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1295 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1295*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦有一官，以五行像之。 | 卦里有一个官，用五行取象。 | Where the hexagram has one Pressure line, take the image by the five phases. |
| 卦有二官，以六神像之，如下临白虎，则曰武神，上临青龙，则曰树神也。 | 卦里有两个官，用六神取象，比如下面的官临白虎，就说是武神，上面的官临青龙，就说是树神。 | Where it has two, take the image by the six spirits: for example, if the lower one carries the White Tiger, call it a martial spirit, and if the upper one carries the Azure Dragon, call it a tree spirit. |
| 卦有动鬼，以八宫像之，如鬼动乾，则曰天神，如鬼动坤，则曰地祗也。 | 卦里有动的鬼，用八宫取象，比如鬼在乾宫动，就说是天神，鬼在坤宫动，就说是地祇。 | Where the hexagram has a moving ghost, take the image by the eight palaces: for example, if the ghost moves in Qian, call it a heavenly spirit, and if in Kun, an earthly deity. |
| 卦爻乱动，或中有官动，以何星临官像之。 | 卦爻乱动，或者其中有官在动，就看哪颗星临在官上来取象。 | Where the lines move in disorder, or a Pressure line among them moves, take the image by whichever star sits on the Pressure line. |
| 此专断神也。 | 这一节是专门断神的。 | This is solely for judging the spirit. |

> **按**:这一条给出了后面六段（LY.ILL.YM.57 到 YM.62）的分工：五行（前面 LY.ILL.YM.44–YM.48 已讲）、六神（LY.ILL.YM.59）、八宫（LY.ILL.YM.57）、诸星（LY.ILL.YM.62）、六爻（LY.ILL.YM.58）；六亲（LY.ILL.YM.60）和十二支（LY.ILL.YM.61）在这一条的总目里没有列出，但下面紧接着还有。「乱官鬼」的「乱」是乱动、多爻纷动。原文「世官鬼而专六爻」只说官鬼在世爻上，后面讲六爻（LY.ILL.YM.58）时又分了「现在何爻」和「世鬼居何象」两种。
>
> **Note:** This entry sets out the division of labour among the following sections (LY.ILL.YM.57 to YM.62): the five phases (already treated in LY.ILL.YM.44 to YM.48), the six spirits (LY.ILL.YM.59), the eight palaces (LY.ILL.YM.57), the various stars (LY.ILL.YM.62) and the six lines (LY.ILL.YM.58); the six relations (LY.ILL.YM.60) and the twelve branches (LY.ILL.YM.61) are not listed in this summary but follow immediately. 'Disorderly Pressure' means Pressure lines moving in confusion, many lines stirring at once. The text's 'the Pressure line on the Self, go by the six lines alone' says only that the Pressure line is on the Self, and the later treatment of the six lines (LY.ILL.YM.58) distinguishes 'which line it appears on' from 'in what image the ghost on the Self lies'.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.55、LY.ILL.YM.57、LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.YM.59、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.101

## LY.ILL.YM.57 官鬼独发按八宫断神鬼：乾天神寺庙父老僧人王侯之灵；坤地祗坟墓母妪后妃；震九天之神蚕桑舟车辱身折足鬼雷火部；巽风波镇海花果园林长妇女；坎北斗北极百川四海；离荧惑香火灶中女；艮山神五岳荒塚童儿；兑释之宗咒咀尸祝师尼酒色

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:神和鬼虽有分别，考察的办法却没有两样。所以官在乾宫发动，是触犯了天神，是冲犯了寺庙，是年老父辈、僧人的鬼，是王侯宫殿的灵；在坤宫动，是亵渎了地祇，是妨碍了坟墓，是母亲、老妇人的鬼，是皇后妃子的神；震是东方，是九天的神，是道的宗主，是掌管蚕桑舟车的，是受辱折了脚的鬼，是雷火部的威灵；巽是东南，是掌管风浪、镇守大海的神，是花果园林里作祟的，是长女、妇人的魂；坎是北方，在天是北斗，在地是北极，是百川、四海、恒河的灵，它的广大是这样，天下的水神火神，确实不能改易；离是火德，在星是荧惑，对读书人和百姓是香火，对一家是灶神，对骨肉亲人是中女的鬼；艮是山神，是五岳，是荒坟，以及童子的鬼；兑是西方，是佛家的宗主，是咒骂诅咒的孽，是巫祝的欺诳，是和尚尼姑的鬼，是酒色的邪。注说：这是官鬼独自发动、其他卦爻都静时，用八宫的象来断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Spirits and ghosts differ, but the way of examining them is not two. So when the Pressure line moves in Qian, a heavenly spirit has been offended, a temple has been struck against, and it is the ghost of an elder or a monk, the spirit of a prince or marquis and his great halls; moving in Kun, the earthly deity has been profaned and the graves obstructed, and it is the ghost of a mother or an old woman, the spirit of an empress or a consort; Zhen is the east: a spirit of the Nine Heavens, the head of the Way, in charge of silkworms and mulberries, boats and carts, the ghost of one who was disgraced or broke a leg, the awesome spirit of the Thunder and Fire department; Xun is the southeast: a spirit in charge of wind and waves and of pacifying the sea, a haunting of flowers, fruit, gardens and groves, the soul of an eldest daughter or a woman; Kan is the north: in heaven the Northern Dipper, on earth the North Pole, the spirit of the hundred rivers, the four seas and the Ganges, so vast is it, and the spirits of water and fire under heaven are firmly beyond change; Li is the virtue of Fire: among the stars Yinghuo, to gentry and commoners the incense fire, in the household the stove, and among kin the ghost of the middle daughter; Gen is the mountain spirit, the Five Peaks, a wild grave, and the ghost of a boy or child; Dui is the west, the head of the Buddhists: the baleful work of curses, the deceit of impersonator and invoker, the ghost of a monk or nun, the evil of wine and lust. The note says: this is judgment by the images of the eight palaces when the Pressure line moves alone and the other lines are all still.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1296 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1296*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 神鬼有殊，考求无二，是故官动乎乾，谓干天神，谓冲寺庙，父老僧人之鬼，王侯都厦之灵也。 | 神和鬼虽有分别，考察的办法却没有两样。所以官在乾宫发动，是触犯了天神，是冲犯了寺庙，是年老父辈、僧人的鬼，是王侯宫殿的灵。 | Spirits and ghosts differ, but the way of examining them is not two. So when the Pressure line moves in Qian, a heavenly spirit has been offended, a temple has been struck against, and it is the ghost of an elder or a monk, the spirit of a prince or marquis and his great halls. |
| 动乎坤，谓渎地祗，谓妨坟墓，母妪之鬼，后妃之神也。 | 在坤宫动，是亵渎了地祇，是妨碍了坟墓，是母亲、老妇人的鬼，是皇后妃子的神。 | Moving in Kun, the earthly deity has been profaned and the graves obstructed; it is the ghost of a mother or an old woman, the spirit of an empress or a consort. |
| 震东方也，九天之神，道之宗，蚕桑舟车之司也，辱身折足之鬼，雷火部之威灵也。 | 震是东方，是九天的神，是道的宗主，是掌管蚕桑舟车的，是受辱折了脚的人的鬼，是雷火部的威灵。 | Zhen is the east: a spirit of the Nine Heavens, the head of the Way, in charge of silkworms and mulberries, boats and carts; the ghost of one who was disgraced or broke a leg, the awesome spirit of the Thunder and Fire department. |
| 巽东南也，其司风波镇海之神也，花果园林之祟，长妇女之魄也。 | 巽是东南，是掌管风浪、镇守大海的神，是花果园林里作祟的，是长女、妇人的魂。 | Xun is the southeast: a spirit in charge of wind and waves and of pacifying the sea; a haunting of flowers, fruit, gardens and groves, the soul of an eldest daughter or a woman. |
| 坎北方也，在天为北斗，在地为北极，百川四海恒河之灵，其广大也； | 坎是北方，在天是北斗，在地是北极，是百川、四海、恒河的灵，它的广大是这样； | Kan is the north: in heaven it is the Northern Dipper, on earth the North Pole, the spirit of the hundred rivers, the four seas and the Ganges, so vast is it; |
| 盖天下水火之神，确乎其不可易也。 | 天下的水神、火神，确实不能改易。 | for the spirits of water and fire under heaven are firmly beyond change. |
| 离火德也，于星荧惑，于士庶曰香火，于家曰灶，于骨肉曰中女之鬼也。 | 离是火德，在星是荧惑，对读书人和百姓是香火，对一家是灶神，对骨肉亲人是中女的鬼。 | Li is the virtue of Fire: among the stars it is Yinghuo, to gentry and commoners it is the incense fire, in the household it is the stove, and among kin it is the ghost of the middle daughter. |
| 艮为山神，为五岳，为荒塚，及童儿之鬼也。 | 艮是山神，是五岳，是荒坟，以及童子、小孩的鬼。 | Gen is the mountain spirit, the Five Peaks, a wild grave, and the ghost of a boy or child. |
| 兑为西方，释之宗也，咒咀之孽，尸祝之诳，师尼之鬼，酒色之邪也。 | 兑是西方，是佛家的宗主；是咒骂诅咒的孽，是巫祝的欺诳，是和尚尼姑的鬼，是酒色的邪。 | Dui is the west, the head of the Buddhists: the baleful work of curses, the deceit of impersonator and invoker, the ghost of a monk or nun, the evil of wine and lust. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1297 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1297*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 言官鬼独发，卦爻皆静，以八宫象断如此。 | 这是说官鬼独自发动、卦爻都静的时候，用八宫的象来断，是这样的。 | This says that when the Pressure line moves alone and the hexagram's other lines are all still, judgment by the images of the eight palaces is as above. |

> **打架**:八宫各配什么鬼神，黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.95）和易冒大体同路，细目不同：乾都是天神一路；坤都有坟墓、地祇；坎、离都是水火之神。不同的地方——艮，黄金策是城隍、宅土，易冒是山神、五岳、荒坟、童子之鬼；巽，黄金策是缢死或颠仆的阴人，易冒是掌管风浪镇海的神、花果园林之祟、长女妇人之魄；兑，黄金策是口愿、伤神，易冒是佛家宗主、咒诅、巫祝之诳、僧尼之鬼、酒色之邪；震，黄金策是树神或杖死的男鬼，易冒是九天之神、蚕桑舟车之司、辱身折足之鬼、雷火部的威灵。两本书都没有说谁对谁错。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What each palace stands for among ghosts and spirits: the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.95) and the Yi Mao run roughly the same way, with differences of detail: in Qian both have the heavenly spirits; in Kun both have graves and the earthly deity; in Kan and Li both have the spirits of water and fire. Where they differ: for Gen, the Huangjin Ce has the City God and the earth of the house, the Yi Mao the mountain spirit, the Five Peaks, a wild grave and the ghost of a boy; for Xun, the Huangjin Ce has a woman who hanged herself or fell in a fit, the Yi Mao a spirit in charge of wind and waves and pacifying the sea, the haunting of flowers, fruit, gardens and groves, and the soul of an eldest daughter or a woman; for Dui, the Huangjin Ce has a spoken vow or an injuring spirit, the Yi Mao the head of the Buddhists, curses, the deceit of impersonators and invokers, the ghost of a monk or nun and the evil of wine and lust; for Zhen, the Huangjin Ce has a tree spirit or a male ghost beaten to death with a staff, the Yi Mao a spirit of the Nine Heavens, one in charge of silkworms, boats and carts, the ghost of one disgraced or with a broken leg, and the awesome spirit of the Thunder and Fire department. Neither book says which is right.

> **按**:这一条原文里「官」是官鬼的省写；「干天神」的「干」是触犯。「荧惑」是火星，「九天」是九重天。「师尼」是和尚、尼姑一类的出家人。「盖天下水火之神，确乎其不可易也」承接坎（水）、离（火）两条，意思是水神、火神的名号大体是固定的。这一条与黄金策的八宫（LY.ILL.HJC.95）、易隐的鬼神名目（LY.ILL.YY.28）是同一件事。
>
> **Note:** In the original 'guan' (official) is short for the Pressure line. 'Gan' in 'offended a heavenly spirit' is to offend or infringe. 'Yinghuo' is the planet Mars, and the 'Nine Heavens' are the nine layers of heaven. 'Monks and nuns' covers those who have left the household life. 'The spirits of water and fire under heaven are firmly beyond change' follows the entries on Kan (Water) and Li (Fire) and means that the names of water and fire spirits are more or less fixed. This entry treats the same matter as the Huangjin Ce's eight palaces (LY.ILL.HJC.95) and the Yi Yin's names of spirits and ghosts (LY.ILL.YY.28).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.56、LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.24

## LY.ILL.YM.58 按爻位断神鬼：初爻井祟、二爻灶祟、三爻家堂之祟、四爻门户之祟、五爻道路之祟、六爻坟墓之祟；世在初为家神、二为土神、三为园神、四为五神、上为山神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:初爻，是井祟，是井里的鬼，是掌管居住的神；二爻，是灶祟，是灶下的亡魂，是掌管境内城邑的神；三爻，是家堂的祟，是闺房里的鬼，是掌管都郡桥埠的神；四爻，是门户的祟，是关隘的神；五爻，是道路的祟，是王侯的神；六爻，是坟墓的祟，是慈悲的尊神，是奴仆的亡魂。世在初爻的，是家神；世在二爻的，是土神；世在三爻，是园神；世在四爻，是五神；世在上爻，是山神。注说：这是卦里得一个鬼，现在哪一爻，以及世爻带鬼落在什么象上，用六爻的象来断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The first line: a haunting by the well, a ghost in the well, a spirit in charge of dwellings; the second: a haunting by the stove, one who died beside the stove, a spirit in charge of the district and its town; the third: a haunting of the household shrine, a ghost of the women's quarters, a spirit in charge of cities, prefectures, bridges and wharves; the fourth: a haunting of the doors, a spirit of passes and defiles; the fifth: a haunting of the road, a spirit of princes and marquises; the sixth: a haunting of the graves, a compassionate honoured one, one who died a servant. With the Self on the first line, a household spirit; on the second, an earth spirit; on the third, a garden spirit; on the fourth, the Five Spirits; on the top, a mountain spirit. The note says: where the hexagram has one ghost, on whichever line it appears, and in whatever image the ghost on the Self lies, judgment by the images of the six lines is as above.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1298–1300 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1298–1300*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初爻井祟，井中之鬼，司居之神也； | 初爻，是井祟，是井里的鬼，是掌管居住的神； | The first line: a haunting by the well, a ghost in the well, a spirit in charge of dwellings; |
| 二爻灶祟，灶下之亡，司境邑之神也； | 二爻，是灶祟，是灶下的亡魂，是掌管境内城邑的神； | the second: a haunting by the stove, one who died beside the stove, a spirit in charge of the district and its town; |
| 三爻家堂之祟，闺壸之鬼，司都郡桥埠之神也； | 三爻，是家堂的祟，是闺房里的鬼，是掌管都郡桥埠的神； | the third: a haunting of the household shrine, a ghost of the women's quarters, a spirit in charge of cities, prefectures, bridges and wharves; |
| 四爻门户之祟，关隘之神； | 四爻，是门户的祟，是关隘的神； | the fourth: a haunting of the doors, a spirit of passes and defiles; |
| 五爻道路之祟，王侯之神； | 五爻，是道路的祟，是王侯的神； | the fifth: a haunting of the road, a spirit of princes and marquises; |
| 六爻坟墓之祟，慈悲之尊，奴仆之亡也。 | 六爻，是坟墓的祟，是慈悲的尊神，是奴仆的亡魂。 | the sixth: a haunting of the graves, a compassionate honoured one, one who died a servant. |
| 世于初者，家神也，世于二者，土神也，世于三，为园神，世于四，为五神，世于上，为山神也。 | 世在初爻的，是家神；世在二爻的，是土神；世在三爻，是园神；世在四爻，是五神；世在上爻，是山神。 | With the Self on the first line, a household spirit; on the second, an earth spirit; on the third, a garden spirit; on the fourth, the Five Spirits; on the top, a mountain spirit. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1301 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1301*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 言卦得一鬼，现在何爻，及世鬼居何象，以六爻象断如此。 | 这是说卦里得一个鬼，现在哪一爻，以及世爻带鬼落在什么象上，用六爻的象来断，是这样的。 | This says that where the hexagram has one ghost, on whichever line it appears, and in whatever image the ghost on the Self lies, judgment by the images of the six lines is as above. |

> **按**:这一条给两张表：一张是鬼落在哪一爻（六条），一张是世爻带鬼时世在哪一爻（五条，三个爻位之外的第五爻没有列）。第一张里「初井、二灶、三家堂、四门户、五道路、六坟墓」是一处住宅由内到外、由近到远的次序，和黄金策的六爻推鬼神（LY.ILL.HJC.101）前三条（井神、灶神/司命、家堂）相同，第五爻的道路也相同；第四爻黄金策讲的是出门撞见，这里是门户与关隘，着眼点不同。「五神」在第二张表里是第四爻的世，书里没有解释是哪五位神。
>
> **Note:** This entry gives two tables: one for the line the ghost falls on (six items), and one for which line the Self is on when the Self carries the ghost (five items, the fifth line not being listed). In the first, 'first the well, second the stove, third the household shrine, fourth the doors, fifth the road, sixth the graves' runs in order through a house from inside out and near to far, and agrees with the Huangjin Ce's reading by the six lines (LY.ILL.HJC.101) in its first three items (the well god, the stove god or Director of Fate, the household shrine) and in the road at the fifth line; for the fourth line the Huangjin Ce speaks of a meeting on going out, and here it is doors and passes, a different angle. The 'Five Spirits' for the Self on the fourth line in the second table are not explained in the book.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.57、LY.ILL.YM.59、LY.ILL.HJC.101、LY.ILL.HJC.111、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.59 按六神断神鬼：青龙喜庆财利之神桃李之妖胎产之鬼；朱雀离神词讼囹圄冤鬼；勾陈土神禾黍社稷工巧折跌之鬼；螣蛇妖邪怪异飞游砍伐绳结惊狂之鬼；白虎金甲之神丧服之祟；玄武强梁之神盗贼河井妓女之魄

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:青龙的象，是掌管喜庆财利的神，东方的圣贤，文官班列里的师相；是桃李的精怪，是胎产而死的鬼。朱雀也是离的神，掌管词讼、纠察善恶，掌管交易，主管生育，守在端门，权衡中鼎；是言语触犯了天曹，或者是牢里被冤枉的鬼。勾陈是一位土神，掌管禾黍，主管社稷；是工匠巧人的亡魂，是摔断跌伤的鬼。螣蛇属火，是黄帝的左右；是妖邪怪异的骚扰，是飞游砍伐的神，是绳结、惊狂的鬼。白虎是金甲的神，是丧服的祟，是兵灾凶险、老虎横行，被称作元帅的就像它。北方的玄武，是强横的神，是盗贼的鬼，是河里井里死去的，是妓女的魂。注说：卦里有两个鬼，上面的鬼临哪个六神、下面的鬼临哪个六神，用六神的象来断，是这样的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The image of the Azure Dragon: a spirit in charge of festivity and gain, a sage of the east, a teacher-minister among the civil ranks; a demon of peach and plum, a ghost of childbirth. The Vermilion Bird is also a spirit of Li: it presides over suits, oversees good and evil, has charge of trade and governs birth, hunts at the Main Gate and weighs the central cauldron; a case of words offending the Heavenly Bureau, or a ghost wrongly imprisoned. Hook Earth is an earth spirit, in charge of millet and grain and governing the altars of soil and grain; the departed spirit of a craftsman, a ghost of breaks and falls. The Coiling Snake belongs to Fire and attends the Yellow Emperor; it is the disturbance of demons and oddities, a spirit of flying wanderers and felling, a ghost of knotted cords, fright and madness. The White Tiger is a golden-armoured spirit, a haunting of mourning dress, the disaster of weapons and the rampage of tigers; those called marshals are its image. The Dark Tortoise of the north is a violent spirit, a ghost of thieves and bandits, one who died in a river or well, the soul of a prostitute. The note says: where the hexagram has two ghosts, whichever spirit the upper one carries and whichever the lower one carries, judgment by the images of the six spirits is as above.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1302 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1302*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙之为象也，司喜庆财利之神，东方圣贤，文班之师相，为桃李之妖，胎产之鬼也。 | 青龙的象，是掌管喜庆财利的神，东方的圣贤，文官班列里的师相；是桃李的精怪，是胎产而死的鬼。 | The image of the Azure Dragon: a spirit in charge of festivity and gain, a sage of the east, a teacher-minister among the civil ranks; a demon of peach and plum, a ghost of childbirth. |
| 朱雀亦离神也，司词讼、紏善恶，掌交易，主生育，狩端门，权中鼎，或言语之干天曹，或囹圄之妄冤鬼也。 | 朱雀也是离的神，掌管词讼、纠察善恶，掌管交易，主管生育，守在端门，权衡中鼎；是言语触犯了天曹，或者是牢里被冤枉的鬼。 | The Vermilion Bird is also a spirit of Li: it presides over suits, oversees good and evil, has charge of trade and governs birth, hunts at the Main Gate and weighs the central cauldron; a case of words offending the Heavenly Bureau, or a ghost wrongly imprisoned. |
| 勾陈一土神也，司禾黍，主社稷，为工巧之亡，折跌之鬼也。 | 勾陈是一位土神，掌管禾黍，主管社稷；是工匠巧人的亡魂，是摔断跌伤的鬼。 | Hook Earth is an earth spirit, in charge of millet and grain and governing the altars of soil and grain; the departed spirit of a craftsman, a ghost of breaks and falls. |
| 螣蛇属火，黄帝之左右也，为妖邪怪异之扰，飞游砍伐之神，绳结惊狂之鬼也。 | 螣蛇属火，是黄帝的左右；是妖邪怪异的骚扰，是飞游砍伐的神，是绳结、惊狂的鬼。 | The Coiling Snake belongs to Fire and attends the Yellow Emperor; it is the disturbance of demons and oddities, a spirit of flying wanderers and felling, a ghost of knotted cords, fright and madness. |
| 白虎为金甲之神，为丧服之祟，兵凶虎横，称呼元帅者像焉。 | 白虎是金甲的神，是丧服的祟，是兵灾凶险、老虎横行，被称作元帅的就像它。 | The White Tiger is a golden-armoured spirit, a haunting of mourning dress, the disaster of weapons and the rampage of tigers; those called marshals are its image. |
| 北方元武，强梁之神，盗贼之鬼，河井之亡，妓女之魄也。 | 北方的玄武，是强横的神，是盗贼的鬼，是河里井里死去的，是妓女的魂。 | The Dark Tortoise of the north is a violent spirit, a ghost of thieves and bandits, one who died in a river or well, the soul of a prostitute. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1303 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1303*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦得二鬼，上临何神，下临何神，以六神像断如此。 | 卦里有两个鬼，上面的鬼临哪个六神、下面的鬼临哪个六神，用六神的象来断，是这样的。 | Where the hexagram has two ghosts, whichever spirit the upper one carries and whichever the lower one carries, judgment by the images of the six spirits is as above. |

> **按**:「元武」是「玄武」避讳的写法。朱雀那一句「狩端门，权中鼎」原文很简，「端门」是宫殿的正门，「中鼎」是中央的鼎，这里照字面译，没有加解释。与黄金策六神推鬼神（LY.ILL.HJC.96）、易隐鬼崇六神（LY.ILL.YY.17）是同一件事：白虎都是血、丧、兵一路，玄武都是盗贼、暗昧一路，青龙都是喜庆一路，细目不同。
>
> **Note:** 'Yuan wu' is the taboo-avoiding form of 'Xuan wu' (the Dark Tortoise). The Vermilion Bird's 'hunts at the Main Gate and weighs the central cauldron' is very terse in the original; the 'Main Gate' is the front gate of a palace and the 'central cauldron' the cauldron at the centre, and both are translated literally without explanation. It treats the same matter as the Huangjin Ce's reading by the six spirits (LY.ILL.HJC.96) and the Yi Yin's six spirits in haunting (LY.ILL.YY.17): the White Tiger is always of the blood, mourning and weapons kind, the Dark Tortoise of thieves and obscurity, the Azure Dragon of festivity, with details differing.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.YM.60、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.YY.17、LY.ILL.YM.26
