## LY.ILL.YM.60 按六亲断神鬼：父母为祖先、妻财为臣妾、兄弟为手足之缠、子孙为儿女之孽，世是本宗、应是异姓；六亲有动以六亲象断，化鬼及独发者尤甚

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻是祖先，妻财爻是臣妾，兄弟爻是手足纠缠，子孙爻是儿女带来的孽；世是本宗，应是异姓。注说：六亲有动的，用六亲的象来断；只有变出鬼的和独自发动的，尤其厉害；世是本家的鬼，应是别姓的亡魂。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Resource line is the ancestors, the Wealth line is servants and concubines, the Peer line is the entanglement of brothers and kin, and the Output line is the affliction of sons and daughters; the Self is the same lineage, and the Other is a different surname. The note says: where a relation line moves, judgment by the images of the six relations is as above; one that changes into a ghost or moves alone is especially severe; the Self is a ghost of one's own house, and the Other is the departed of another surname.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1304 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1304*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母为祖先，妻财为臣妾，兄弟为手足之缠，子孙为儿女之孽，世是本宗，应是异姓。 | 父母爻是祖先，妻财爻是臣妾，兄弟爻是手足纠缠，子孙爻是儿女带来的孽；世是本宗，应是异姓。 | The Resource line is the ancestors, the Wealth line is servants and concubines, the Peer line is the entanglement of brothers and kin, and the Output line is the affliction of sons and daughters; the Self is the same lineage, and the Other is a different surname. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1305 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1305*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六亲有动，则以六亲像断如此。 | 六亲有动的，就用六亲的象来断，是这样的。 | Where a relation line moves, judgment by the images of the six relations is as above. |
| 惟化鬼及独发者尤甚。 | 只有变出鬼的和独自发动的，尤其厉害。 | One that changes into a ghost or moves alone is especially severe. |
| 世，本家鬼也，应，他姓亡也。 | 世是本家的鬼，应是别姓的亡魂。 | The Self is a ghost of one's own house, and the Other is the departed of another surname. |

> **按**:这一条的用法是：官鬼旁边的六亲爻在动，或者官鬼变成了某个六亲，就按那个六亲去认是谁的鬼。和黄金策「鬼动化出六亲即以化出者断」（LY.ILL.HJC.108）、易隐「鬼崇是谁的鬼」（LY.ILL.YY.30）是同一件事；易冒的写法更短，只列六亲各是谁（父母祖先、妻财臣妾、兄弟手足、子孙儿女），没有再分阴阳老少。注里的这一段夹在正文中间，原文的括号在「他姓亡也」处收尾，后面的「犯亥子……」仍是正文（见 LY.ILL.YM.61）。
>
> **Note:** The use is this: when a relation line beside the Pressure line moves, or the Pressure line changes into a certain relation, one identifies whose ghost it is by that relation. It is the same matter as the Huangjin Ce's 'a ghost moving into a relation is judged by what it turns into' (LY.ILL.HJC.108) and the Yi Yin's 'whose ghost it is' (LY.ILL.YY.30); the Yi Mao writes it more briefly, listing only who each relation is (Resource the ancestors, Wealth servants and concubines, Peer brothers and kin, Output sons and daughters), without going on to divide by yin and yang, old and young. The note in this passage sits in the middle of the running text, the original bracket closing at 'the departed of another surname', and the 'if it offends Hai and Zi...' that follows is still the text (see LY.ILL.YM.61).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.59、LY.ILL.YM.61、LY.ILL.HJC.108、LY.ILL.HJC.102、LY.ILL.YY.30

---

## LY.ILL.YM.61 按十二支断神鬼：犯亥子则天将斗神可祷、犯巳午则火德财神可禳、树神东岳乃寅卯之官、佛氏丧车乃申酉之象、辰戌为处处之土神、丑未为家家之地鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:犯了亥、子，就是天将斗神，可以祷告；犯了巳、午，就是火德财神，可以禳解；树神、东岳，是寅、卯的官；佛家的丧车，是申、酉的象；辰、戌是处处都有的土神，丑、未是家家都有的地鬼。注说：十二支的神值在鬼上，就用十二支的象来断，是这样的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If it offends Hai and Zi, the Heavenly Generals and the spirit of the Dipper may be prayed to; if it offends Si and Wu, the Fire Virtue and the god of wealth may be exorcised; the tree spirit and the Eastern Peak are officials of Yin and Mao; the Buddhist funeral carriage is the image of Shen and You; Chen and Xu are the earth spirits found everywhere, and Chou and Wei the earth ghosts found in every house. The note says: where a spirit of the twelve branches sits on the ghost, judgment by the images of the twelve branches is as above.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1305–1306 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1305–1306*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 犯亥子，则天将斗神可祷也； | 犯了亥、子，就是天将斗神，可以祷告； | If it offends Hai and Zi, the Heavenly Generals and the spirit of the Dipper may be prayed to; |
| 犯巳午，则火德财神可禳也； | 犯了巳、午，就是火德财神，可以禳解； | if it offends Si and Wu, the Fire Virtue and the god of wealth may be exorcised; |
| 树神东岳，乃寅卯之官，佛氏丧车，乃申酉之象； | 树神、东岳，是寅、卯的官；佛家的丧车，是申、酉的象； | the tree spirit and the Eastern Peak are officials of Yin and Mao; the Buddhist funeral carriage is the image of Shen and You; |
| 辰戌为处处之土神，丑未为家家之地鬼。 | 辰、戌是处处都有的土神，丑、未是家家都有的地鬼。 | Chen and Xu are the earth spirits found everywhere, and Chou and Wei the earth ghosts found in every house. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1307 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1307*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 十二支神值鬼，则以十二支像断如此。 | 十二支的神值在鬼上，就用十二支的象来断，是这样的。 | Where a spirit of the twelve branches sits on the ghost, judgment by the images of the twelve branches is as above. |

> **按**:原文里这一段跨了两行：第三句「树神东岳，乃寅卯之官，佛氏丧车，」在上一行结尾，「乃申酉之象；」在下一行开头，这里合起来读。「亥子」是北、水，配天将斗神；「巳午」是南、火，配火德财神；「寅卯」是东、木，配树神东岳；「申酉」是西、金，配佛家丧车。「辰戌」与「丑未」是四土，分成处处的土神和家家的地鬼。「可祷」「可禳」两个词分开用：亥子是神，可以向它祷告；巳午是火德财神，是可以禳解的。
>
> **Note:** In the original this passage runs over two lines: the third sentence, 'the tree spirit and the Eastern Peak are officials of Yin and Mao, the Buddhist funeral carriage', ends the first line and 'is the image of Shen and You' begins the next, and they are read here together. Hai and Zi are north and Water, matched with the Heavenly Generals and the spirit of the Dipper; Si and Wu are south and Fire, matched with the Fire Virtue and the god of wealth; Yin and Mao are east and Wood, matched with the tree spirit and the Eastern Peak; Shen and You are west and Metal, matched with the Buddhist funeral carriage. Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei are the four Earths, divided into the earth spirits found everywhere and the earth ghosts found in every house. 'May be prayed to' and 'may be exorcised' are used separately: with Hai and Zi it is a spirit that may be prayed to, and with Si and Wu it is the Fire Virtue and the god of wealth, which may be exorcised.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.60、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.YY.28

---

## LY.ILL.YM.62 按诸星断神鬼：贵人冠冕之神、天喜利市吉庆、丧门丧服、天火火德天触灶司天贼强梁劫夺、天狱笞杖徒流、官符主讼、羊刃司兵、驿马商旅之冤、天河觉溺之屈、华盖僧道、咸池娼妓、暗金产妇、太岁土德、刀针斩绞、坵墓坟茔、丧车棺椁、浴盆婴童之溺、孤神绝嗣之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:还有官鬼出现在贵人上的，是戴冠冕的神；天喜，是带来生意兴隆、吉庆的神；丧门，是丧服的神；天火、火德、天触、灶司、天贼，是强横劫夺而死的亡魂；天狱，是受笞刑、杖刑、徒刑、流放的鬼；官符主管诉讼，羊刃掌管兵刃，驿马是行商旅客的冤魂，天河是溺水者的冤屈；华盖是僧人道士，咸池是娼妓，暗金是产妇，血衂是血鬼；太岁是土德；刀针是被斩杀、绞死、受伤，坵墓是坟墓的咎，丧车是棺椁的殃，折煞是摔断跌伤的鬼；飞廉是游神，将星是武神，天医是医神，天马是被马踏的；浴盆是婴孩溺死，摧尸是伏尸的祸，孤神是断了后嗣的鬼；太阴、月德从王母那里受福，太阳、天德靠上帝保佑。注说：鬼临哪颗星宿，就用诸星的象来断；天河是水库，是天上浩大的府库，所以从辰起逐月顺行，刀针是金宿，正月应当从乾起，所以从亥起顺行；其余详见诸星章。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Further, a Pressure line appearing on the Noble is a spirit in cap and robes of office; Heavenly Joy is a spirit of good trade and celebration; Mourning Gate is a spirit of mourning dress; Heavenly Fire, Fire Virtue, Heavenly Touch, Stove Official and Heavenly Thief are the departed of violence and robbery; Heavenly Prison is a ghost of flogging, beating, penal servitude and exile; Official Talisman presides over lawsuits, Goat Blade governs weapons, the Post Horse is the grievance of merchants and travellers, and Heavenly River is the wrong of the drowned; Huagai is monks and Daoists, Xianchi is courtesans, Hidden Metal is women in childbirth, and Bleeding is a blood ghost; the Tai Sui is the Earth Virtue; Blade and Needle is beheading, strangling and wounds, Mounds and Tombs is the fault of the graves, Funeral Carriage is the misfortune of coffins, and Breaking Killing is a ghost of breaks and falls; Feilian is the wandering spirit, the General Star is the martial spirit, Heavenly Doctor is the medicine spirit, and Heavenly Horse is trampling by a horse; Bath Basin is the drowning of infants, Corpse Breaker is the calamity of the buried corpse, and Lone Spirit is a ghost with no heir; Great Yin and Month Virtue receive blessing from the Queen Mother, the Sun and Heavenly Virtue depend on the protection of the Supreme Lord. The note says: whichever star the ghost falls on, judgment by the images of the various stars is as above; Heavenly River is a water store, the vast treasury of heaven, and so it runs forward month by month starting from Chen; Blade and Needle is a Metal lodge, and the first month should start from Qian, so it begins at Hai and runs forward; the rest is detailed in the chapter on the stars.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1308 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1308*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 犹有官鬼现于贵人者，冠冕之神也； | 还有官鬼出现在贵人上的，是戴冠冕的神； | Further, a Pressure line appearing on the Noble is a spirit in cap and robes of office; |
| 天喜者，利市吉庆之神也； | 天喜，是带来生意兴隆、吉庆的神； | Heavenly Joy is a spirit of good trade and celebration; |
| 丧门，丧服神也； | 丧门，是丧服的神； | Mourning Gate is a spirit of mourning dress; |
| 天火、火德，天触、灶司、天贼，强梁劫夺之亡； | 天火、火德、天触、灶司、天贼，是强横劫夺而死的亡魂； | Heavenly Fire, Fire Virtue, Heavenly Touch, Stove Official and Heavenly Thief are the departed of violence, robbery and plunder; |
| 天狱，笞杖徒流之鬼； | 天狱，是受笞刑、杖刑、徒刑、流放的鬼； | Heavenly Prison is a ghost of flogging, beating, penal servitude and exile; |
| 官符主讼，羊刃司兵，驿马为商旅之冤，天河为觉溺之屈； | 官符主管诉讼，羊刃掌管兵刃，驿马是行商旅客的冤魂，天河是觉后溺水的冤屈； | Official Talisman presides over lawsuits, Goat Blade governs weapons, the Post Horse is the grievance of merchants and travellers, and Heavenly River is the wrong of those who wake to drown; |
| 华盖，僧道也，咸池，娼妓也，暗金，产妇也，血衂，血鬼也； | 华盖是僧人道士，咸池是娼妓，暗金是产妇，血衂是血鬼； | Huagai is monks and Daoists, Xianchi is courtesans, Hidden Metal is women in childbirth, and Bleeding is a blood ghost; |
| 太岁，土德； | 太岁是土德； | the Tai Sui is the Earth Virtue; |
| 刀针，斩绞伤也，坵墓，坟茔之咎，丧车，棺椁之殃，折煞，折跌之鬼； | 刀针是被斩杀、绞死、受伤，坵墓是坟墓的咎，丧车是棺椁的殃，折煞是摔断跌伤的鬼； | Blade and Needle is beheading, strangling and wounds, Mounds and Tombs is the fault of the graves, Funeral Carriage is the misfortune of coffins, and Breaking Killing is a ghost of breaks and falls; |
| 飞廉，游神，将星，武神，天医，医神，天马，马踏； | 飞廉是游神，将星是武神，天医是医神，天马是被马踏的； | Feilian is the wandering spirit, the General Star is the martial spirit, Heavenly Doctor is the medicine spirit, and Heavenly Horse is trampling by a horse; |
| 浴盆，婴童之溺，摧尸，伏尸之祸，孤神，绝嗣之鬼； | 浴盆是婴孩溺死，摧尸是伏尸的祸，孤神是断了后嗣的鬼； | Bath Basin is the drowning of infants, Corpse Breaker is the calamity of the buried corpse, and Lone Spirit is a ghost with no heir; |
| 太阴月德，受福于王母，太阳天德，赖祐于上帝也。 | 太阴、月德从王母那里受福，太阳、天德靠上帝保佑。 | Great Yin and Month Virtue receive blessing from the Queen Mother; the Sun and Heavenly Virtue depend on the protection of the Supreme Lord. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1309 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1309*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼值何宿，则以诸星像断如此。 | 鬼临哪颗星宿，就用诸星的象来断，是这样的。 | Whichever star the ghost falls on, judgment by the images of the various stars is as above. |
| 天河水库，乃天之浩府，故正自辰逐月顺行，刀针金宿，正月当自乾起，故始亥顺行也。 | 天河是水库，是天上浩大的府库，所以从辰起，逐月顺行；刀针是金宿，正月应当从乾起，所以从亥起、顺着走。 | Heavenly River is a water store, the vast treasury of heaven, and so it runs forward month by month starting from Chen; Blade and Needle is a Metal lodge, and the first month should start from Qian, so it begins at Hai and runs forward. |
| 余详诸星章内。 | 其余详见诸星章。 | The rest is detailed in the chapter on the stars. |

> **按**:「天火、火德，天触、灶司、天贼，强梁劫夺之亡」这一句，谓语「强横劫夺而死的亡魂」管到哪几个星，原文的逗号并不清楚，这里照字面把它放在这一串的末尾。「天河为觉溺之屈」的「觉溺」原文没有解释，这里按字面译作「觉后溺水」，读法没有把握。星名的译名在本仓库各处保持一致：咸池 Xianchi、驿马 Post Horse、浴盆 Bath Basin、丧车 Funeral Carriage。这些星怎么查，详见易冒诸星章。
>
> **Note:** In 'Heavenly Fire, Fire Virtue, Heavenly Touch, Stove Official, Heavenly Thief: the departed of violence and robbery', it is not clear from the commas how many of the stars the predicate 'the departed of violence and robbery' extends to, and it is placed literally at the end of the run. The 'awake-drown' in 'Heavenly River is the wrong of those who wake to drown' is not explained in the original, and is translated literally as 'wake to drown', the reading being uncertain. Star names (Noble, Heavenly Joy, Mourning Gate, Heavenly Fire, Heavenly Prison, Official Talisman, Goat Blade, Post Horse, Heavenly River, Huagai, Xianchi and so on) keep the same renderings used elsewhere in this repository: Xianchi, Post Horse, Bath Basin, Funeral Carriage. How the stars and spirit-stars are found is set out in the Yi Mao's chapters on the stars.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.61、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.YY.29

---

## LY.ILL.YM.63 察鬼神但以五行八宫变化而像之，必实指鬼神而泥之则诬：四穹之内无处非鬼神，各乡所尊不同，千古之上未有之神何所知，千古之下再成之神何所拟

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:天地四方之内，没有哪里没有鬼神，但各地方各有风俗所尊奉的，地水火风所掌管的，有的只显灵在一个乡，有的应验在一个国，很难一一细说。俗话说，千古以前还没有的神，怎么知道；千古以后再成的神，又拿什么去比拟。考察鬼神，只要用五行、八宫，变化着去取象，作为后学的规范，古今都能通用。如果一定要落实指定是哪位鬼神、还死抠着不放，那就是诬妄了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Within the four vaults of heaven there is no place without ghosts and spirits, yet each quarter has those its customs revere and those that earth, water, fire and wind govern, some manifest in a single village, some proving true over a whole country, hard to set out one by one. It is said: of spirits that did not exist a thousand ages ago, how can they be known; of spirits that will come to be a thousand ages hence, what can they be compared to. In examining ghosts and spirits, simply take the image by the five phases and the eight palaces with their changes, as a pattern for later learners, and it applies to past and present alike. To point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1310 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1310*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫四穹之内，无处而非鬼神，然有方隅风俗之所尊，地水火风之所司，或显一乡，或征一国，难以繁陈。 | 天地四方之内，没有哪里没有鬼神，但各地方各有风俗所尊奉的，地水火风所掌管的，有的只显灵在一个乡，有的应验在一个国，很难一一细说。 | Within the four vaults of heaven there is no place without ghosts and spirits, yet each quarter has those its customs revere and those that earth, water, fire and wind govern, some manifest in a single village, some proving true over a whole country, hard to set out one by one. |
| 语谓千古而上，未有之神何所知，千古而下，再成之神何所拟。 | 俗话说，千古以前还没有的神，怎么知道；千古以后再成的神，又拿什么去比拟。 | It is said: of spirits that did not exist a thousand ages ago, how can they be known; of spirits that will come to be a thousand ages hence, what can they be compared to. |
| 察鬼神者，但以五行八宫，变化而像之，以为后学之范，虽今古可通也。 | 考察鬼神，只要用五行、八宫，变化着去取象，作为后学的规范，那么古今都能通用。 | In examining ghosts and spirits, simply take the image by the five phases and the eight palaces with their changes, as a pattern for later learners, and it applies to past and present alike. |
| 必实指鬼神而泥之，则诬矣。 | 如果一定要落实指定是哪位鬼神、还死抠着不放，那就是诬妄了。 | To point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim. |

> **打架**:要不要落实到某一位神：易冒在本章前面（LY.ILL.YM.44 到 YM.62）列了大量神名，这里却说「必实指鬼神而泥之，则诬矣」，前后的关系是：名单是举例，办法是用五行八宫去取象。增删卜易的觉子和李我平批评的是把名单当成定论，即金官就指关公岳公（LY.ILL.ZS.24、ZS.25），而且李我平点名批评了《易冒》指定关岳、并说本书疾病章讲鬼神是「从俗」；从这一段看，易冒自己在这里已经说了不能泥于某一位。黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.93 到 HJC.104）和易隐（LY.ILL.YY.28）则直接给出神名和鬼的死因，没有这样的收束。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to pin it to a particular god: the Yi Mao lists a large number of names earlier in the chapter (LY.ILL.YM.44 to YM.62), yet here says 'to point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim'; the relation between the two is that the lists are examples and the method is to take the image by the five phases and the eight palaces. What Jue Zi and Li Woping of the Zengshan Buyi criticise is taking the list as a conclusion, that is, naming Guan Gong or Yue Gong at once for a Metal Pressure line (LY.ILL.ZS.24, ZS.25), and Li Woping names the Yi Mao for pinning Guan and Yue and says its talk of ghosts and spirits in the illness chapter 'follows the common custom'; from this passage the Yi Mao itself already says here that one must not cling to a particular one. The Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.93 to HJC.104) and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.28) give names of gods and the causes of death of ghosts directly, without such a closing qualification.

> **按**:「地水火风」是佛家说的四大，这里指各地方由地、水、火、风所司的神。「千古而上，未有之神何所知」这句话的意思是，一个人死后成神，是后来的事；古书里没有的神，不能用古书去对。这一段在 LY.ILL.YM.42 总纲里埋下的「鬼神不是全然虚妄」和这里的「必实指而泥之则诬」合在一起，就是这一章的态度：承认有，但不为一个具体的名字负责。
>
> **Note:** 'Earth, water, fire and wind' are the four elements of the Buddhist teaching, here the spirits of each place that are governed by earth, water, fire and wind. 'Of spirits that did not exist a thousand ages ago, how can they be known' means that a person becoming a spirit after death is a later matter, and a spirit not found in the old books cannot be matched against the old books. What was planted in the outline (LY.ILL.YM.42), 'ghosts and spirits are not wholly false', together with this 'to point insistently and cling to it is to make a false claim', is the attitude of the chapter: to admit that they exist, but not to answer for any particular name.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.YM.64、LY.ILL.YM.42、LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.HJC.93、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25、LY.ILL.YY.28

---

## LY.ILL.YM.64 神多为祐、鬼多为祟，灾病多因自孽：不洗心悔过而聚巫陈牲媚鬼神，鬼神未必享；炰杀生命以祈己命，益重其孽；祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:大体上神多半是保佑的，鬼多半是作祟的；灾病的到来，多半是自己造的孽。如果不洗心悔过、除掉孽的根本，只是聚集巫师、摆上牲畜去讨好鬼神，鬼神未必享用。何况宰杀生灵来为自己的性命祈祷，那是加重自己的罪孽。所以说：祭祀没有比解禳更好的，解禳没有比修身更好的，说的就是这个。我在鬼神这一章里，再三告诫。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In general spirits mostly bless and ghosts mostly haunt; for calamity and illness mostly come from one's own doing, and if one does not cleanse the heart and repent to remove the root of the fault, but merely gathers shamans and lays out animal victims to please ghosts and spirits, the ghosts and spirits will not necessarily partake. How much more so to roast and slaughter living creatures to pray for my own life: that only makes the fault heavier. Hence it is said: of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself; this is what it means. In this chapter on ghosts and spirits I have given warning again and again.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1311 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1311*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大抵神多为祐，鬼多为祟，盖灾病之来，多因自孽，若不洗心悔过，以除孽本，徒聚巫陈牲以媚鬼神，鬼神未必享也。 | 大体上神多半是保佑的，鬼多半是作祟的；因为灾病的到来，多半是自己造的孽，如果不洗心悔过、除掉孽的根本，只是聚集巫师、摆上牲畜去讨好鬼神，鬼神未必享用。 | In general spirits mostly bless and ghosts mostly haunt; for calamity and illness mostly come from one's own doing, and if one does not cleanse the heart and repent to remove the root of the fault, but merely gathers shamans and lays out animal victims to please ghosts and spirits, the ghosts and spirits will not necessarily partake. |
| 况炰杀生命，以祈我之命，是益重其孽也。 | 何况宰杀生灵来为自己的性命祈祷，那是加重自己的罪孽。 | How much more so to roast and slaughter living creatures to pray for my own life: that only makes the fault heavier. |
| 故曰：祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身，此之谓也。 | 所以说：祭祀没有比解禳更好的，解禳没有比修身更好的，说的就是这个。 | Hence it is said: of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself; this is what it means. |
| 吾于鬼神章，兽三致戒云。 | 我在鬼神这一章里，再三告诫。 | In this chapter on ghosts and spirits I have given warning again and again. |

> **打架**:祭祀送鬼有没有用：易冒说灾病多因自孽，不修身而只靠巫祝牲牢献媚鬼神，鬼神未必享用，杀生祈命更是加重罪孽，祭祀最好不过解禳、解禳最好不过修身；增删卜易的野鹤同样反对靠杀牲救命（LY.ILL.ZS.25），但承认时灾瘟癖照法祭送屡验；黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.93、HJC.50）和易隐（LY.ILL.YY.27、YY.31）则把祈禳的对象、祭品、方法一项项讲出来，把祭祀当作要认真做的事。三家不是互相否定，重点不同：易冒重修身，野鹤按病分，黄金策和易隐重办法。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether sacrifice and sending away ghosts is of use: the Yi Mao says calamity and illness mostly come from one's own doing, that without cultivating oneself, currying favour with ghosts and spirits by means of shamans and animal victims is not necessarily partaken of, that slaughtering to pray for life makes the fault heavier, and that of sacrifice none is better than exorcism and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself; the Old Man of Yehe in the Zengshan Buyi likewise opposes slaughter to save a life (LY.ILL.ZS.25), but admits that sacrifice and sending away by the method has been proved many times for seasonal calamities and abdominal masses; the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.93, HJC.50) and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.27, YY.31) set out object, offering and method of prayer and exorcism item by item and treat sacrifice as something to be done with care. The three do not deny one another; their emphasis differs: the Yi Mao stresses self-cultivation, the Old Man of Yehe distinguishes by illness, the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Yin stress procedure.

> **按**:「炰杀生命」的「炰」是煮烤，「牲」是祭祀用的牲畜，「巫」是巫师。「吾于鬼神章，兽三致戒云」的「兽」字放在这里说不通，像是「再」「屡」一类字的误刻；「三致戒」是再三告诫，这一层意思是清楚的，这里译成「再三告诫」，没有替这个字定读音。「祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身」是作者引的成语（前面有「故曰」），书里没有说出处。这一句正是 LY.ILL.YM.55 里「伏鬼若空则无鬼祟，惟修德」的落点。
>
> **Note:** 'Roast and slaughter' (pao sha) is boiling and roasting, 'victims' are the animals used in sacrifice, and 'shamans' are wu. The 'shou' (beast) in 'in the chapter on ghosts and spirits I have given warning shou-three times' does not make sense here and looks like a miscarved form of a word such as 'again' or 'repeatedly'; that it means 'has given warning three times over', that is, again and again, is clear, and it is rendered 'again and again' without deciding what the character should be. 'Of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself' is quoted by the author as a saying (it is introduced by 'hence it is said'), and the book gives no source. This sentence is where 'if the hidden ghost is also Void there is no haunting, and only cultivating virtue remains' in LY.ILL.YM.55 lands.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.YM.55、LY.ILL.HJC.93、LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.25、LY.ILL.YY.27、LY.ILL.YY.31

---

## LY.ILL.YY.27 鬼崇·祈禳：以官鬼为用，无鬼或鬼空绝则无鬼崇；官鬼生合世用宜祈祷、官持世用先祷后医、鬼动克世用而福动救助宜禳保、世用持财逢火鬼动宜众保；阳鬼为神、阴官为鬼、鬼带书贵为神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以官鬼为用爻：卦里没有鬼，或者鬼遇到空、绝，就没有鬼崇。官鬼来生、合世爻和用爻的，适合祈祷；官爻持着世爻、用爻的，先祈祷后医治；鬼发动刑害克冲世爻、用爻，而有福神发动来救助的，适合禳解保佑；世爻、用爻持着财、又遇火鬼发动的，适合大家一起保祷。问祈禳的是哪一路神鬼：阳鬼是神，阴官是鬼；鬼带文书、贵人的，是神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line is the Subject: if there is no ghost in the hexagram, or the ghost meets Void or Extinction, there are no ghostly influences. If the Pressure line generates or combines with the Self and the Subject, prayer is fitting; if Pressure holds the Self and Subject, pray first and treat afterwards; if the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self and Subject and Fortune moves to rescue and help, exorcism and protection suit; if the Self and Subject hold Wealth and a Fire ghost moves, a communal rite of protection suits. To ask which spirit or ghost the prayer is for: a yang ghost is a spirit and a yin Pressure line is a ghost; a ghost carrying the Document and the Noble is a spirit.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以官鬼为用，如卦无鬼及鬼逢空绝者，则无鬼崇也。 | 以官鬼为用爻；如果卦里没有鬼，或者鬼遇到空、绝，就没有鬼崇。 | The Pressure line is the Subject; if there is no ghost in the hexagram, or the ghost meets Void or Extinction, there are no ghostly influences. |
| 官鬼生合世用者，宜祈祷也。 | 官鬼来生、合世爻和用爻的，适合祈祷。 | If the Pressure line generates or combines with the Self and the Subject, prayer is fitting. |
| 官持世用者，先祷后医也。 | 官爻持着世爻、用爻的，先祈祷后医治。 | If Pressure holds the Self and the Subject, pray first and treat afterwards. |
| 鬼动刑害克冲世用，得福动来救助者，宜禳保也。 | 鬼发动刑、害、克、冲世爻、用爻，而有福神发动来救助的，适合禳解保佑。 | If the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self and Subject and Fortune moves to rescue and help, exorcism and protection suit. |
| 世用持财，逢火鬼动者，宜众保也。 | 世爻、用爻持着财，又遇火鬼发动的，适合大家一起保祷。 | If the Self and Subject hold Wealth and a Fire ghost moves, a communal rite of protection suits. |
| 问祈禳是何神鬼，则阳鬼为神，阴官为鬼也。 | 问祈禳的是哪一路神鬼：阳鬼是神，阴官是鬼。 | To ask which spirit or ghost the prayer and exorcism is for: a yang ghost is a spirit and a yin Pressure line is a ghost. |
| 又鬼带书贵者，为神也。 | 又，鬼带文书、贵人的，是神。 | Also a ghost carrying the Document and the Noble is a spirit. |

> **打架**:怎么分神和鬼：易隐说「阳鬼为神，阴官为鬼」，按鬼爻是阴爻还是阳爻分；这和卜筮正宗的王洪绪「阳为神为男，阴为鬼为女」一致（LY.ILL.HJC.94），而黄金策的旧注和增删卜易的觉子都是按旺衰分——旺相是神、休囚是鬼（LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.ZS.23），觉子还说鬼休囚或空破墓绝根本不是鬼神作祟。两种分法不同，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How to tell a spirit from a ghost: the Yi Yin says 'a yang ghost is a spirit, a yin Pressure line is a ghost', by whether the Pressure line is a yin or a yang line; this agrees with Wang Hongxu of the Bushi Zhengzong, 'yang is a spirit and male, yin a ghost and female' (LY.ILL.HJC.94), while the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce and Juezi of the Zengshan Buyi both go by strength — strong is a spirit, resting or trapped a ghost (LY.ILL.HJC.94 and LY.ILL.ZS.23), and Juezi adds that if the ghost is resting, trapped, Void, broken, in a tomb or in Extinction it is not a ghost or spirit making trouble at all. The two divide differently and the books do not decide.

> **按**:「祈祷」「禳保」「众保」是三种不同的仪式：祈祷是向神祈求，禳保是禳解并求保佑，众保是众人一起（如社里的会）保祷。「书贵」按字面是文书、贵人两颗星，书里没有再解释。
>
> **Note:** 'Qidao', 'rangbao' and 'zhongbao' are three different rites: prayer is asking a spirit, 'rangbao' is exorcism together with a request for protection, and 'zhongbao' is protection sought by many together (as in a village society). 'Shugui' is literally the Document and the Noble stars and the book does not explain further.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.26、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.HJC.93、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.ZS.23

---

## LY.ILL.YY.28 鬼崇·神与鬼的名目：阳金鬼庵堂关圣（在虎为七煞）、阴金鬼刀伤自刎痨瘵喘嗽虎伤之魂；阳木鬼东岳五圣家先、阴木鬼自缢疯疾跌扑刑杖死；阳水鬼观音真武三官水神河泊、阴水鬼投河服卤呕血；阳火鬼玄坛五福火神灶司、阴火鬼疮毒痨疫带血心疼烧死；阳土鬼城隍土谷太岁皮场大王、阴土鬼瘟疫咽喉臌胀黄疸压死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:按五行和阴阳分神和鬼：阳金鬼是庵堂、关圣，带白虎的是七煞；阴金鬼是死于刀伤、自刎、痨病、喘嗽、虎伤的魂。阳木鬼是东岳、五圣、家先香火；阴木鬼是自缢、疯病、跌扑、被杖刑打死的魂。阳水鬼是观音、真武、三官、水神、张神、河泊、水官；阴水鬼是投河、喝卤水、呕血而死的魂。阳火鬼是玄坛、五福、火神、灶司的香火；阴火鬼是死于疮毒、痨病、瘟疫、带血、心痛、烧死的魂。阳土鬼是城隍、土谷、太岁、皮场大王和土神；阴土鬼是死于瘟疫、咽喉病、臌胀、黄疸、被压死的魂。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Spirits and ghosts by element and by yin and yang: a yang Metal ghost is the temples and halls and Guan Sheng, and with the Tiger the Seven Killings; a yin Metal ghost is the souls of those who died of blade wounds, cutting their own throats, consumption, wheezing cough or a tiger's bite. A yang Wood ghost is the Eastern Peak, the Five Sages and the incense of family forebears; a yin Wood ghost the souls of those who hanged themselves or died of madness, falls and blows, or a beating with the rod. A yang Water ghost is Guanyin, Zhenwu, the Three Officials, the water spirits, the Zhang spirit, the Lord of the River and the Water Official; a yin Water ghost the souls of those who threw themselves into a river, drank brine or vomited blood. A yang Fire ghost is the incense of the Dark Altar, the Five Blessings, the Fire God and the Kitchen Official; a yin Fire ghost the souls of those who died of sores and poison, consumption, epidemic, bleeding, heart pain or burning. A yang Earth ghost is the City God, the Earth and Grain gods, the Tai Sui, the Great King of the Hide Market and earth spirits; a yin Earth ghost the souls of those who died of epidemic, throat disease, swelling, jaundice or being crushed.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 阳金鬼，庵堂关圣，在虎七煞也。 | 阳金鬼，是庵堂、关圣；带白虎的，是七煞。 | A yang Metal ghost is the temples and halls and Guan Sheng; with the Tiger, the Seven Killings. |
| 阴金鬼，则刀伤自刎痨瘵喘嗽虎伤之魂也。 | 阴金鬼，是死于刀伤、自刎、痨病、喘嗽、虎伤的魂。 | A yin Metal ghost is the souls of those who died of blade wounds, cutting their own throats, consumption, wheezing cough or a tiger's bite. |
| 阳木鬼，束岳五圣家先香火也。 | 阳木鬼，是东岳（原书写作「束岳」）、五圣、家先香火。 | A yang Wood ghost is the Eastern Peak (the book writes 'Shu Yue', probably a slip for 'Dong Yue'), the Five Sages and the incense of family forebears. |
| 阴木鬼，则自缢疯疾跌扑刑杖死之魂也。 | 阴木鬼，是自缢、疯病、跌扑、被杖刑打死的魂。 | A yin Wood ghost is the souls of those who hanged themselves or died of madness, falls and blows, or a beating with the rod. |
| 阳水鬼，观音真武三官水神张神河泊水官也。 | 阳水鬼，是观音、真武、三官、水神、张神、河泊、水官。 | A yang Water ghost is Guanyin, Zhenwu, the Three Officials, the water spirits, the Zhang spirit, the Lord of the River and the Water Official. |
| 阴水鬼，则投河服滷呕血之魂也。 | 阴水鬼，是投河、喝卤水、呕血而死的魂。 | A yin Water ghost is the souls of those who threw themselves into a river, drank brine or vomited blood. |
| 阳火鬼，玄坛五福火神灶司香火也。 | 阳火鬼，是玄坛、五福、火神、灶司的香火。 | A yang Fire ghost is the incense of the Dark Altar, the Five Blessings, the Fire God and the Kitchen Official. |
| 阴火鬼，则疮毒痨(此处之字上疒下去)疫瘟带血心疼烧死之魂也。 | 阴火鬼，是死于疮毒、痨病（此处原书有一个上面病字头、下面「去」字的生僻字）、疫瘟、带血、心疼、烧死的魂。 | A yin Fire ghost is the souls of those who died of sores and poison, consumption together with (a rare ailment character which the transcriber notes here, written with the sickness radical above and 'go' below), epidemic, bleeding, heart pain or burning. |
| 阳土鬼，城隍土谷太岁皮场大王土神也。 | 阳土鬼，是城隍、土谷、太岁、皮场大王和土神。 | A yang Earth ghost is the City God, the Earth and Grain gods, the Tai Sui, the Great King of the Hide Market and earth spirits. |
| 阴土鬼，则瘟疫咽喉臌胀黄疸压死之魂也。 | 阴土鬼，是死于瘟疫、咽喉病、臌胀、黄疸、被压死的魂。 | A yin Earth ghost is the souls of those who died of epidemic, throat disease, swelling, jaundice or being crushed. |

> **打架**:要不要把五行落实到具体的神：易隐按阳鬼、阴鬼列了很具体的名目（阳金鬼是庵堂关圣，阳木鬼是东岳五圣，阳水鬼是观音真武三官……），黄金策的旧注也列过（灶君、伤司、河伯江神，LY.ILL.HJC.97）；增删卜易的觉子认为不必把金官指为关公岳公这样的神，要问病人心里疑的是哪位再占一卦（LY.ILL.ZS.24），李我平更批评《易冒》《易林补遗》指定关岳（LY.ILL.ZS.25）。指名派和不指名派，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to fix the elements to particular named spirits: the Yi Yin gives very specific names by yang and yin ghost (a yang Metal ghost the temples and Guan Sheng, a yang Wood ghost the Eastern Peak and the Five Sages, a yang Water ghost Guanyin, Zhenwu and the Three Officials...), and the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce also lists names (the Kitchen God, the Injuring Office, the Lord of the River and river god, LY.ILL.HJC.97); Juezi of the Zengshan Buyi holds that one should not name Metal Pressure as Guan Gong, Yue Gong and the like but ask which spirit the patient has in mind and divine again (LY.ILL.ZS.24), and Li Woping criticises the Yi Mao and the Yilin Buyi for naming Guan and Yue (LY.ILL.ZS.25). The naming school and the non-naming school are not settled by the books.

> **按**:阳鬼是神的一列（庙里的神），阴鬼是鬼的一列（死于什么的魂）。「关圣」是关帝（关羽），「玄坛」是赵公明（财神），「真武」是北方之神，「三官」是天、地、水三官，「五福」「五圣」「张神」「皮场大王」是民间信奉的神，书里没有一一解释，按字面译。「七煞」「在虎七煞」书里没有再解释。「滷」是卤水（盐卤，自尽用）。整理本在「阴火鬼」一句里也有那个生僻的病字的说明。
>
> **Note:** The yang ghosts are the column of spirits (the gods of the temples) and the yin ghosts the column of ghosts (the souls of those who died of one cause or another). 'Guan Sheng' is Emperor Guan (Guan Yu), 'Xuantan' is Zhao Gongming (god of wealth), 'Zhenwu' is the god of the north, the 'Three Officials' are the Officials of Heaven, Earth and Water, and the 'Five Blessings', 'Five Sages', 'Zhang spirit' and 'Great King of the Hide Market' are gods worshipped among the people whom the book does not explain one by one, rendered literally. The 'Seven Killings' and 'with the Tiger the Seven Killings' are not explained further. 'Lu' is brine (salt brine, used to end one's life). In the sentence on the yin Fire ghost the transcriber again notes the rare ailment character.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.27、LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.103、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.YY.29 鬼崇·鬼带的煞：加金虎刑杀死于非命、居游魂外卦客死他乡、加天火天烛独火烧死、天贼天盗劫杀因盗致死、沐浴咸池奸淫娼妓、木狼天缢缢死、风波浴盆浮沉溺死、羊刃刀砧屠刽鬼、暗金血刃产亡鬼、天刑天狱地狱牢狱入狱牢狱鬼、刀砧羊刃劫杀自刎鬼、华盖孤神僧道绝嗣鬼、螣蛇天怪精怪妖邪；鬼居太岁是当年岁君、居月日时是游野神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看鬼带的煞：鬼带金、白虎、刑煞的，是死于非命；鬼落在游魂卦的外卦的，是客死他乡；带天火、天烛、独火煞的，是烧死；带天贼、天盗、劫煞的，是因盗致死；带沐浴、咸池煞的，是奸淫、娼妓；带木狼、天缢煞的，是缢死；带风波、浴盆、浮沉煞的，是溺死；带羊刃、刀砧煞的，是屠夫刽子手的鬼；带暗金、血刃煞的，是产亡的鬼；带天刑、天狱、地狱、牢狱、入狱煞的，是牢狱的鬼；带刀砧、羊刃、劫煞的，是自刎的鬼；带华盖、孤神的，是僧道、绝了后的鬼；带螣蛇、天怪煞的，是精怪妖邪。鬼落在太岁上的，是当年的岁君；落在月、日、时上的，是游荡在野外的神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** By the stars the ghost carries: a ghost carrying Metal, the Tiger and punishing stars is one who died an unnatural death; a ghost in the outer trigram of a Wandering Soul hexagram, one who died a stranger in a far land; with Heavenly Fire, Heavenly Candle and Lone Fire, burned to death; with Heavenly Thief, Heavenly Robber and Robbery-Killing, dead through robbers; with Bath and Xianchi, adultery and prostitution; with Wood Wolf and Heavenly Hanging, hanged; with Wind-and-Waves, Bath Basin and Floating-Sinking, drowned; with Goat Blade and Chopping Block, a butcher or executioner ghost; with Hidden Metal and Blood Blade, a ghost who died in childbirth; with Heavenly Punishment, Heavenly Prison, Earthly Prison, Jail and Entering-Prison, a jail ghost; with Chopping Block, Goat Blade and Robbery-Killing, a ghost who cut his own throat; with Canopy and Lone Spirit, a monk or Daoist, one who left no heir; with the Coiling Snake and Heavenly Monster, a sprite, monster or evil. A ghost on the Tai Sui is the Year Lord of the year, and one on the Month, Day or hour a wandering field spirit.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又鬼加金虎刑杀者，死于非命也。 | 又，鬼带金、白虎、刑煞的，是死于非命。 | Also a ghost carrying Metal, the Tiger and punishing stars: death by unnatural causes. |
| 鬼居游魂外卦者，客死他乡也。 | 鬼落在游魂卦的外卦里的，是客死他乡。 | A ghost in the outer trigram of a Wandering Soul hexagram: one who died a stranger in a far land. |
| 鬼加天火天烛独火杀者，烧死也。 | 鬼带天火、天烛、独火煞的，是烧死。 | A ghost carrying Heavenly Fire, Heavenly Candle and Lone Fire: burned to death. |
| 鬼加天贼天盗劫杀者，因盗致死也。 | 鬼带天贼、天盗、劫煞的，是因为盗贼致死。 | A ghost carrying Heavenly Thief, Heavenly Robber and Robbery-Killing: dead through robbers. |
| 鬼加沐浴咸池杀者，奸淫娼妓也。 | 鬼带沐浴、咸池煞的，是奸淫、娼妓。 | A ghost carrying Bath and Xianchi: adultery and prostitution. |
| 鬼加木狼，天缢杀者，缢死也。 | 鬼带木狼、天缢煞的，是缢死。 | A ghost carrying Wood Wolf and Heavenly Hanging: hanged. |
| 鬼加风波浴盆浮沉杀者，溺死也。 | 鬼带风波、浴盆、浮沉煞的，是溺死。 | A ghost carrying Wind-and-Waves, Bath Basin and Floating-Sinking: drowned. |
| 鬼加羊刃刀砧杀者，屠刽鬼也。 | 鬼带羊刃、刀砧煞的，是屠夫、刽子手的鬼。 | A ghost carrying Goat Blade and Chopping Block: a butcher or executioner ghost. |
| 鬼带暗金血刃杀者，产亡鬼也。 | 鬼带暗金、血刃煞的，是产亡的鬼。 | A ghost carrying Hidden Metal and Blood Blade: a ghost who died in childbirth. |
| 鬼带天刑天狱地狱牢狱入狱杀者，牢狱鬼也。 | 鬼带天刑、天狱、地狱、牢狱、入狱煞的，是牢狱的鬼。 | A ghost carrying Heavenly Punishment, Heavenly Prison, Earthly Prison, Jail and Entering-Prison: a jail ghost. |
| 鬼加刀砧羊刃劫杀者，自刎鬼也。 | 鬼带刀砧、羊刃、劫煞的，是自刎的鬼。 | A ghost carrying Chopping Block, Goat Blade and Robbery-Killing: a ghost who cut his own throat. |
| 鬼带华盖孤神者，僧道绝嗣鬼也。 | 鬼带华盖、孤神的，是僧道、绝了后的鬼。 | A ghost carrying Canopy and Lone Spirit: a monk or Daoist, one who left no heir. |
| 鬼加螣蛇天怪杀者，精怪妖邪也。 | 鬼带螣蛇、天怪煞的，是精怪妖邪。 | A ghost carrying the Coiling Snake and Heavenly Monster: a sprite, monster or evil. |
| 鬼居太岁者，当年岁君也。 | 鬼落在太岁上的，是当年的岁君。 | A ghost on the Tai Sui: the Year Lord of the year. |
| 鬼居月日时上者，游野神也。 | 鬼落在月、日、时上的，是游荡在野外的神。 | A ghost on the Month, Day or hour: a wandering field spirit. |

> **按**:天火、天烛、独火、天贼、天盗、劫煞、咸池、木狼、天缢、风波、浴盆、浮沉、羊刃、刀砧、暗金、血刃、天刑、天狱、地狱、牢狱、入狱、华盖、孤神、天怪都是神煞，书里只给了名目和它们指向的死因，没有解释每一颗从何而来。易冒有一套神煞起法（见易冒神煞章），但这里没有指明用哪一套。「岁君」就是太岁。「游魂」是八宫里每宫的第七卦。「刽」是刽子手，即行刑的人。
>
> **Note:** Heavenly Fire, Heavenly Candle, Lone Fire, Heavenly Thief, Heavenly Robber, Robbery-Killing, Xianchi, Wood Wolf, Heavenly Hanging, Wind-and-Waves, Bath Basin, Floating-Sinking, Goat Blade, Chopping Block, Hidden Metal, Blood Blade, Heavenly Punishment, Heavenly Prison, Earthly Prison, Jail, Entering-Prison, Canopy, Lone Spirit and Heavenly Monster are all spirit-stars; the book gives only the names and the causes of death they point to and does not explain where each comes from. The Yi Mao has a scheme for setting these stars (see its chapter on spirit-stars) but this passage does not say which scheme is meant. 'Year Lord' is the Tai Sui. 'Wandering Soul' is the seventh hexagram of each palace among the eight. A 'gui' is an executioner, one who carries out the punishment.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.YY.30、LY.ILL.HJC.103、LY.ILL.ZS.23

---

## LY.ILL.YY.30 鬼崇·是谁的鬼：父母伏鬼化鬼阳父阴母、兄弟阳兄弟阴姐妹、妻财阳妻奴阴妾婢、子孙阳男阴女；鬼在本宫内卦同姓、他宫外卦异姓；交重大鬼、单拆小鬼；衰墓老年旧鬼、生旺壮年新鬼、胎养孩童鬼；乾宫祖宗与父、坤宫祖妣与母、震坎艮男、巽离兑女；几个鬼按一水二火三木四金五土之数，旺相加倍、休如数、囚死减半

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看是谁的鬼：父母爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是父亲，阴爻是母亲；兄弟爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是兄弟，阴爻是姐妹；妻财爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是妻子和奴仆，阴爻是妾和婢女；子孙爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。鬼在本宫内卦的，是同姓的鬼；在别的宫、外卦的，是异姓的鬼。发动的是大鬼，不动的是小鬼；衰弱、入墓的是年老的旧鬼，生旺的是壮年的新鬼，胎、养是孩童的鬼（阳男阴女）。按八卦：乾宫的鬼是祖宗和父亲，坤宫的鬼是祖母和母亲，震、坎、艮宫的鬼是男的，巽、离、兑宫的鬼是女的。问有几个鬼，按一水、二火、三木、四金、五土的数来推，旺相的加倍，休的照原数，囚、死的减半。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whose ghost it is: Resource with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost — yang is the father, yin the mother; Peer with a ghost hidden beneath or changing into one — yang brothers, yin sisters; Wealth with a ghost — yang a wife or manservant, yin a concubine or maid; Output with a ghost — yang male, yin female. A ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace is a ghost of the same surname, and one in the outer trigram of another palace a ghost of another surname. A moving ghost is a great ghost and a still one a small ghost; weak or in a tomb, an old ghost of long standing; born or strong, a ghost of the prime of life, newly dead; at Womb or Nurture, a child's ghost (yang a boy, yin a girl). By the eight trigrams: a ghost in the Qian palace is an ancestor or the father, in Kun a grandmother or the mother, in Zhen, Kan or Gen male, in Xun, Li or Dui female. To ask how many ghosts there are, work by the numbers Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five: if strong, double it; if resting, as given; if trapped or dead, halve it.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又父母伏鬼化鬼者，阳父阴母也。 | 又，父母爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是父亲，阴爻是母亲。 | Also Resource with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang is the father, yin the mother. |
| 兄弟伏鬼化鬼者，阳兄弟，阴姐妹也。 | 兄弟爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是兄弟，阴爻是姐妹。 | Peer with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, brothers; yin, sisters. |
| 妻财伏鬼化鬼者，阳妻奴阴妾婢也。 | 妻财爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是妻子和奴仆，阴爻是妾和婢女。 | Wealth with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, a wife or manservant; yin, a concubine or maid. |
| 子孙伏鬼化鬼者，阳男阴女也。 | 子孙爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。 | Output with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, male; yin, female. |
| 鬼在本宫内卦，同姓鬼也。 | 鬼在本宫的内卦，是同姓的鬼。 | A ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace: a ghost of the same surname. |
| 鬼在他宫外卦，异姓鬼也。 | 鬼在别的宫、外卦，是异姓的鬼。 | A ghost in the outer trigram of another palace: a ghost of another surname. |
| 交重，大鬼。 | 发动的，是大鬼。 | Moving: a great ghost. |
| 单拆，小鬼。 | 不动的，是小鬼。 | Still: a small ghost. |
| 衰墓，老年旧鬼。 | 衰弱、入墓的，是年老的旧鬼。 | Weak or in a tomb: an old ghost of long standing. |
| 生旺，壮年新鬼。 | 生旺的，是壮年的新鬼。 | Born or strong: a ghost of the prime of life, newly dead. |
| 胎养，孩童鬼，阳男阴女也。 | 胎、养，是孩童的鬼，阳爻是男孩，阴爻是女孩。 | At Womb or Nurture: a child's ghost, yang a boy, yin a girl. |
| 又八卦乾宫鬼，祖宗与父。 | 又按八卦：乾宫的鬼是祖宗和父亲。 | Again by the eight trigrams: a ghost in the Qian palace is an ancestor or the father. |
| 坤宫鬼，祖妣与母也。 | 坤宫的鬼是祖母和母亲。 | A ghost in the Kun palace is a grandmother or the mother. |
| 震坎艮宫鬼，男也。 | 震、坎、艮宫的鬼是男的。 | A ghost in the Zhen, Kan or Gen palace is male. |
| 巽离兑宫鬼，女也。 | 巽、离、兑宫的鬼是女的。 | A ghost in the Xun, Li or Dui palace is female. |
| 问有几鬼，以一水二火三木四金五土之数推之。 | 问有几个鬼，按一水、二火、三木、四金、五土的数来推。 | To ask how many ghosts there are, work by the numbers: Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five. |
| 旺相加倍，休如数，囚死减半也。 | 旺相的加倍，休的照原数，囚、死的减半。 | If strong, double it; if resting, as given; if trapped or dead, halve it. |

> **按**:「交重」是发动的爻，「单拆」是不动的爻（单是阳爻、拆是阴爻）。「祖妣」是祖母。「坎」是中男，「艮」是少男，「巽」是长女，「离」是中女，「兑」是少女，这几宫的鬼按男女分。黄金策的旧注和王洪绪也有按长生、帝旺、衰弱、胎养分年龄、按乾巽分方位的说法（LY.ILL.HJC.94），这里多了按六亲、按本宫他宫分同姓异姓、按卦宫分男女和按五行之数推几个鬼几条；卦身推是谁的鬼见 LY.ILL.HJC.102。
>
> **Note:** 'Jiaozhong' is a moving line and 'danchai' a still line (dan a yang line, chai a yin line). 'Zubi' is the grandmother. Kan is the middle son, Gen the youngest son, Xun the eldest daughter, Li the middle daughter and Dui the youngest daughter, and the ghosts of these palaces are divided by sex. The old commentary and Wang Hongxu on the Huangjin Ce also have the age by Birth, Peak, decline, Womb and Nurture and the direction by Qian and Xun (LY.ILL.HJC.94); this adds telling by relation which ghost it is, the same or a different surname by home palace or another, sex by trigram, and the number of ghosts by the number of the element; for telling whose ghost it is by the Body see LY.ILL.HJC.102.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.YY.31、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.HJC.102、LY.ILL.HJC.104

## LY.ILL.YY.31 鬼崇·祭礼（曹子虚）：祈禳用何祭礼看鬼之食神；鬼属甲食在丙禄居巳宜炒鸡煎腐，乙食丁禄午宜干脯炒荳，丙食戊禄巳，丁食己禄午，戊食庚禄申宜三牲馒首果饼，己食辛禄酉宜鸡肉鱼馄饨，庚食壬禄亥宜猪首三牲，辛食癸禄子宜池鱼血羹麦面，壬食甲禄寅宜三牲时果新蔬，癸食乙禄卯宜鸡鹅鸭蔬果鸭蛋；送于何方以鬼爻支神定；子带龙雀素祭、带勾蛇虎武荤盘、伏化水鬼水父佛经、火鬼火父道醮、化财伏财福礼、化兄伏兄演戏众保

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:曹子虚说：祈禳用什么祭品礼数，要看鬼的食神。鬼属甲，食神在丙，禄在巳，用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒、礼、纸钱纸马送走；鬼属乙，食神在丁，禄在午，用干肉脯、炒豆、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属丙，食神在戊，禄在巳，同属甲的祭品；属丁，食神在己，禄在午，同属乙的祭品；属戊，食神在庚，禄在申，用三牲、馒头、果饼、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属己，食神在辛，禄在酉，用鸡肉、鱼、馄饨、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属庚，食神在壬，禄在亥，用猪头、三牲、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属辛，食神在癸，禄在子，用池里的鱼、血羹、麦面、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属壬，食神在甲，禄在寅，用三牲、时鲜的果子、新蔬菜、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属癸，食神在乙，禄在卯，用鸡、鹅、鸭、蔬果、鸭蛋、酒、礼、纸钱纸马。送到哪个方向，以鬼爻的地支来定。如果是神，祈祷的礼数把子孙当作福神：子孙带青龙、朱雀的，适合素祭；带勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武的，适合荤盘；子孙伏在水鬼或水父母下、或变出水鬼水父母的，适合念佛经；伏在火鬼或火父母下、或变出火鬼火父母的，适合道教的醮；子孙变出财、或伏在财下的，适合福礼；变出兄、或伏在兄下的，适合演戏、大家一起保祷。这是祈禳的占法。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Cao Zixu says: what sacrifice and rite to use for prayer and exorcism depends on the ghost's food-star. If the ghost belongs to Jia, the food-star is Bing and the emolument is at Si, and one sends it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses; Yi: food-star Ding, emolument at Wu, dried meat, fried beans, wine, gifts and paper money and horses; Bing: food-star Wu, emolument at Si, as for Jia; Ding: food-star Ji, emolument at Wu, as for Yi; Wu: food-star Geng, emolument at Shen, the three sacrificial animals, steamed buns, fruit cakes, wine and gifts; Ji: food-star Xin, emolument at You, chicken, fish, wonton, wine and gifts; Geng: food-star Ren, emolument at Hai, a pig's head, the three animals, wine and gifts; Xin: food-star Gui, emolument at Zi, pond fish, blood soup, wheat noodles, wine and gifts; Ren: food-star Jia, emolument at Yin, the three animals, seasonal fruit, fresh vegetables, wine and gifts; Gui: food-star Yi, emolument at Mao, chicken, goose, duck, vegetables and fruit, duck eggs, wine and gifts. The direction in which to send it is fixed by the branch of the Pressure line. If it is a spirit, in the rites of prayer Output is taken as the spirit of blessing: Output carrying the Dragon and the Bird, a vegetarian sacrifice; carrying the Hook, Snake, Tiger and Tortoise, a meat platter; hidden beneath a Water ghost or Water Resource, or changing into one, Buddhist scripture; beneath a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, or changing into one, a Daoist ritual; changing into or beneath Wealth, a blessing rite; changing into or beneath Peer, a staged play and communal protection. This is the divination of prayer and exorcism.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 曹子虚曰，祈禳用何祭礼，则视鬼之食神也。 | 曹子虚说：祈禳用什么祭品礼数，就看鬼的食神。 | Cao Zixu says: what sacrifice and rite to use for prayer and exorcism depends on the ghost's food-star. |
| 如鬼属甲，则食在丙而禄居巳也。 | 比如鬼属甲，食神在丙，禄在巳。 | For example, if the ghost belongs to Jia, its food-star is Bing and its emolument is at Si. |
| 宜用炒鸡煎腐酒礼财马送之也。 | 适合用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | Send it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019–3021 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, lines 3019–3021*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼属乙，则食在丁而禄居午也，宜用干脯炒荳酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属乙，食神在丁，禄在午，适合用干肉脯、炒豆子、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Yi, its food-star is Ding and its emolument is at Wu; send it off with dried meat, fried beans, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属丙，则食在戊而禄居巳也，宜用炒鸡煎腐酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属丙，食神在戊，禄在巳，适合用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Bing, its food-star is Wu and its emolument is at Si; send it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属丁，则食在己而禄居午也，宜用干脯炒荳酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属丁，食神在己，禄在午，适合用干肉脯、炒豆子、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ding, its food-star is Ji and its emolument is at Wu; send it off with dried meat, fried beans, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属戊，则食在庚而禄居申也，宜用三牲馒首果饼酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属戊，食神在庚，禄在申，适合用三牲、馒头、果饼、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Wu, its food-star is Geng and its emolument is at Shen; send it off with the three sacrificial animals, steamed buns, fruit cakes, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属己，则食在辛而禄居酉也，宜用鸡肉鱼馄饨酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属己，食神在辛，禄在酉，适合用鸡肉、鱼、馄饨、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ji, its food-star is Xin and its emolument is at You; send it off with chicken, fish, wonton, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属庚，则食在壬而禄居亥也，宜用猪首三牲酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属庚，食神在壬，禄在亥，适合用猪头、三牲、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Geng, its food-star is Ren and its emolument is at Hai; send it off with a pig's head, the three sacrificial animals, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属辛，则食在癸而禄居子也，宜用池鱼血羹麦面酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属辛，食神在癸，禄在子，适合用池里的鱼、血羹、麦面、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Xin, its food-star is Gui and its emolument is at Zi; send it off with pond fish, blood soup, wheat noodles, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属壬，则食在甲而禄居寅也，宜用三牲时果新蔬酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属壬，食神在甲，禄在寅，适合用三牲、时鲜果子、新蔬菜、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ren, its food-star is Jia and its emolument is at Yin; send it off with the three sacrificial animals, seasonal fruit, fresh vegetables, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属癸，则食在乙而禄居卯也，宜用鸡鹅鸭蔬果鸭蛋酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属癸，食神在乙，禄在卯，适合用鸡、鹅、鸭、蔬菜水果、鸭蛋、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Gui, its food-star is Yi and its emolument is at Mao; send it off with chicken, goose, duck, vegetables and fruit, duck eggs, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 若夫送于何方，则以鬼爻之支神定之也。 | 至于送到哪个方向，就以鬼爻的地支来定。 | As for the direction in which to send it, it is fixed by the branch of the Pressure line. |
| 其在于神，则祈祷之礼，以子孙为福神也。 | 如果是神，祈祷的礼数，把子孙当作福神。 | If it is a spirit, in the rites of prayer Output is taken as the spirit of blessing. |
| 子带龙雀者，宜素祭。 | 子孙带青龙、朱雀的，适合素祭。 | If Output carries the Dragon and the Bird, a vegetarian sacrifice suits. |
| 子带勾蛇虎武者，宜荤盘。 | 子孙带勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武的，适合荤盘。 | If Output carries the Hook, Snake, Tiger and Tortoise, a meat platter suits. |
| 子伏水鬼水父下，或化水鬼水父者，宜佛经。 | 子孙伏在水鬼或水父母下，或者变出水鬼水父母的，适合念佛经。 | If Output is hidden beneath a Water ghost or Water Resource, or changes into a Water ghost or Water Resource, Buddhist scripture suits. |
| 子伏火鬼火父下，或化火鬼火父者，宜道醮。 | 子孙伏在火鬼或火父母下，或者变出火鬼火父母的，适合道教的醮。 | If Output is hidden beneath a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, or changes into a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, a Daoist ritual suits. |
| 子化财伏财者，宜福礼，子化兄伏兄者，宜演戏众保也。 | 子孙变出财、或伏在财下的，适合福礼；子孙变出兄、或伏在兄下的，适合演戏、大家一起保祷。 | If Output changes into Wealth or is hidden beneath Wealth, a blessing rite suits; if Output changes into Peer or is hidden beneath Peer, a staged play and communal protection suit. |
| 此祈禳之占也。 | 这是祈禳的占法。 | This is the divination of prayer and exorcism. |

> **按**:这一条在原书里跨了 3019、3021 两行。「食神」按字面是「鬼所属的天干所生的天干」（甲生丙、乙生丁……），「禄居某」是那个食神的禄位（丙禄在巳、丁禄在午、戊禄在巳、己禄在午、庚禄在申、辛禄在酉、壬禄在亥、癸禄在子、甲禄在寅、乙禄在卯）；书里没有另作解释，这里按上下文理解。「三牲」是牛、羊、猪（或猪、鸡、鱼），「财马」是纸钱纸马，「醮」是道教的法事。书里在这一段没有给出为什么某个天干配某种祭品。
>
> **Note:** In the original this entry runs across lines 3019 and 3021. The 'food-star' (shishen) is literally the stem generated by the stem the ghost belongs to (Jia generates Bing, Yi generates Ding ...), and 'emolument at' is that food-star's emolument seat (Bing at Si, Ding at Wu, Wu at Si, Ji at Wu, Geng at Shen, Xin at You, Ren at Hai, Gui at Zi, Jia at Yin, Yi at Mao); the book does not explain this separately and it is understood here by context. The 'three sacrificial animals' are ox, sheep and pig (or pig, chicken and fish), 'caima' is paper money and paper horses, and 'jiao' is a Daoist rite. The book gives no reason in this passage why a given stem is matched with a given offering.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.30、LY.ILL.YY.32

## LY.ILL.YY.32 鬼崇·旧愿：子孙伏鬼下动克世用、或伏岁杀下动伤世用，必有旧愿未还；旺相愿大、休囚愿小；问因何许愿以官鬼为用——鬼伏世下用下是自己许、伏父母财子下是为父母妻子许、伏鬼下是因病讼许、伏兄下是因兄弟朋友争斗赌博许；六亲化出鬼亦然

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:又有旧愿没还的：子孙伏在鬼下面、发动克世爻、用爻，或者子孙伏在岁煞下面、发动伤世爻、用爻的，一定是有旧愿没有还；旺相的愿大，休囚的愿小。问是因为什么许的愿，以官鬼为用：鬼伏在世爻下、用爻下的，是为自己许的愿；鬼伏在父母、财、子孙下的，是为父母、妻子、子女许的愿；鬼伏在鬼下的，是因为病、官司许的愿；鬼伏在兄下的，是为兄弟朋友、争斗赌博许的愿。六亲变出鬼的也是这样（出自《源髓诀》）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** There is also an old vow not yet paid: Output hidden beneath the ghost and moving to control the Self and Subject, or Output hidden beneath the Year-killing star and moving to harm the Self and Subject, means surely an old vow is not yet paid; if strong the vow is large, if resting or trapped small. To ask why the vow was made, the Pressure line is the Subject: a ghost hidden beneath the Self or the Subject line, a vow made for oneself; hidden beneath Resource, Wealth or Output, made for parents, wife or children; hidden beneath a ghost, made over illness or lawsuit; hidden beneath Peer, made over brothers, friends, brawls or gambling. The same holds when a relation changes out into a ghost (from the Yuan Sui Jue).

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3021 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3021*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又有旧愿未还者，但子孙伏鬼下动克世用，或子孙伏岁杀下动伤世用者，必有旧愿未还也。 | 又有旧愿没还的：子孙伏在鬼下面、发动克世爻、用爻，或者子孙伏在岁煞下面、发动伤世爻、用爻的，一定是有旧愿没有还。 | There is also an old vow not yet paid: Output hidden beneath the ghost and moving to control the Self and Subject, or Output hidden beneath the Year-killing star and moving to harm the Self and Subject: there is surely an old vow not yet paid. |
| 旺相愿大，休囚愿小也。 | 旺相的愿大，休囚的愿小。 | If strong, the vow is large; if resting or trapped, small. |
| 至问因何许愿，以官鬼为用也。 | 至于问是因为什么许的愿，以官鬼为用。 | As for asking why the vow was made, the Pressure line is the Subject. |
| 鬼伏世下用下者，因自己许也，鬼伏父母财子下，因父母妻子许，鬼伏鬼下因病讼许，鬼伏兄下因兄弟朋友争斗赌博许也。 | 鬼伏在世爻下、用爻下的，是为自己许的愿；鬼伏在父母、财、子孙下的，是为父母、妻子、子女许的愿；鬼伏在鬼下，是因为病、官司许的愿；鬼伏在兄爻下，是为兄弟朋友、争斗赌博许的愿。 | A ghost hidden beneath the Self or the Subject line: a vow made for oneself; hidden beneath Resource, Wealth or Output: made for parents, wife or children; hidden beneath a ghost: made over illness or lawsuit; hidden beneath Peer: made over brothers, friends, brawls or gambling. |
| 六亲化出鬼者亦然(出源髓诀)。 | 六亲变出鬼的也是这样（出自《源髓诀》）。 | The same holds when a relation changes out into a ghost (from the Yuan Sui Jue). |

> **按**:「岁杀」按字面是岁上的煞，书里没有再解释。「出源髓诀」的「源髓诀」是易隐引的书名，书里没有再介绍。这一条和黄金策讲的「持世则未酬旧愿，伏为有口无心」（LY.ILL.HJC.110）是同一类判断：都是从鬼爻的位置看是不是有旧愿未还；黄金策看鬼是不是持世、伏世下，这里另看子孙伏在鬼下。
>
> **Note:** 'The Year-killing star' (suisha) is literally a killing star of the year and the book does not explain further. The 'Yuan Sui Jue' is a work the Yi Yin cites and the book does not introduce. This entry is a judgment of the same kind as the Huangjin Ce's 'holding the Self: the old vow is not paid; hidden: a spoken word with no heart in it' (LY.ILL.HJC.110): both tell from the position of the Pressure line whether an old vow is unpaid; the Huangjin Ce looks at whether the ghost holds the Self or is hidden beneath it, and this looks in addition at Output hidden beneath the ghost.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.31、LY.ILL.YY.33、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.HJC.110

## LY.ILL.ZS.23 占鬼神看鬼爻的五行与旺衰：鬼休囚或空破墓绝都不是鬼神作祟；旺相是神、休囚是鬼——金是武神西方神/刀剑亡鬼，木是文神东方神/依草附木之妖或刑杖悬梁之鬼，水是河海北方神/水死之鬼，火是火神南方神雷公电母窖灶神/汤火焚烧之鬼，土是土神中央神山川社稷神/墙倒屋塌土死之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占鬼神，卦里的鬼要是休囚，或者空、破、墓、绝，都不是鬼神在作祟。鬼爻旺相是神，休囚衰弱是鬼：临金，旺相是武神和西方的神，衰弱是死于刀剑的鬼；临木，旺相是文神和东方的神，衰弱是依草附木的妖、或被杖责悬梁而死的鬼；临水，旺相是河海和北方的神，衰弱是死在池井江湖里的鬼；临火，旺相是火神和南方的神、雷公电母、窖灶的神，衰弱是被汤火烧死的鬼；临土，旺相是土神和中央的神、掌管山川社稷的神，衰弱是墙倒屋塌压死的鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining ghosts and spirits, if the Pressure line in the hexagram is resting or trapped, or Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, it is not the harm of ghosts or spirits. A strong Pressure line is a god and a weak one a ghost: on Metal, if strong a martial god and a god of the west, if weak a ghost that died by sword or blade; on Wood, if strong a civil god and a god of the east, if weak a goblin clinging to grass and trees or a ghost that died by the rod or by hanging from a beam; on Water, if strong a god of river and sea and of the north, if weak a ghost that drowned in a pond, well, river or lake; on Fire, if strong the god of fire and a god of the south, the Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning, the gods of the cellar and the stove, if weak a ghost that died by scalding or burning; on Earth, if strong the god of the soil and a god of the centre and a god in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of the land and grain, if weak a ghost that died buried under a fallen wall or collapsed house.

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13199–13203 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 13199–13203*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：凡占鬼神，卦中鬼值休囚，及空破墓绝，皆非鬼神之害也。 | 觉子说：凡是占鬼神，卦里的鬼落在休囚上，以及空、破、墓、绝的，都不是鬼神在作祟。 | Juezi says: in all divinations about ghosts and spirits, if the Pressure line in the hexagram is resting or trapped, or Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, it is not the harm of ghosts or spirits. |
| 鬼临金，旺相者，武神及西方之神； | 鬼临金，旺相的，是武神和西方的神； | Pressure on Metal, if strong: a martial god or a god of the west; |
| 衰弱休囚者，刀剑身亡之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是死于刀剑的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died by sword or blade. |
| 鬼临木，旺相者，文神及东方之神； | 鬼临木，旺相的，是文神和东方的神； | Pressure on Wood, if strong: a civil god or a god of the east; |
| 衰弱休囚者，依草附木之妖，或是刑杖悬梁之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是依草附木的妖，或是被杖责、悬梁而死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a goblin that clings to grass and trees, or a ghost that died by the rod or by hanging from a beam. |
| 鬼临水，旺相者，河海及北方之神； | 鬼临水，旺相的，是河海和北方的神； | Pressure on Water, if strong: a god of river and sea or of the north; |
| 衰弱休囚者，池井江湖水死之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是死在池、井、江、湖里的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that drowned in a pond, well, river or lake. |
| 鬼临火，旺相者，火神及南方之神，雷公电母，窖灶之神； | 鬼临火，旺相的，是火神和南方的神，雷公电母，窖灶的神； | Pressure on Fire, if strong: the god of fire or a god of the south, the Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning, the gods of the cellar and the stove; |
| 衰弱休囚者，汤火焚烧之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是被汤火烧死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died by scalding or burning. |
| 鬼临土，旺相者，土神及中央之神，及掌管山川社稷之神； | 鬼临土，旺相的，是土神和中央的神，以及掌管山川社稷的神； | Pressure on Earth, if strong: the god of the soil or a god of the centre, or a god in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of the land and grain; |
| 衰弱休囚者，墙倒屋塌土死之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是墙倒屋塌、被土压死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died buried under a fallen wall or collapsed house. |

> **打架**:怎样认是哪一路神鬼：黄金策旧注（LY.ILL.HJC.50）按日辰的地支推（子是北斗或北阴，亥是溺死鬼），王洪绪按生合世爻或用爻的那一爻带的六神认；觉子这里按鬼爻自己的五行和旺衰认（旺相是神，休囚是鬼），又说鬼休囚或空破墓绝根本不是鬼神作祟。三家的办法各不相同。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How to tell which sort of spirit or ghost it is: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.50) reasons from the branch of the Day (Zi is the Northern Dipper or the North Yin, Hai a drowned ghost), and Wang Hongxu from the six spirits carried by the line that generates or combines with the Self or Subject line; Juezi here tells by the phase and strength of the Pressure line itself (strong is a god, resting or trapped a ghost), and adds that Pressure that is resting or trapped, Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct is not the harm of ghosts and spirits at all. The three ways all differ.

> **按**:「武神」是关公、岳飞一类的神；「文神」是文昌、孔子一类，书里没有列名单，译文按字面。「刑杖悬梁」是被杖打死、吊死。「雷公电母」是司雷电的神，「窖灶」是地窖和灶，「社稷」是土神和谷神。
>
> **Note:** 'Martial gods' are gods such as Guan Yu and Yue Fei, and 'civil gods' are of the kind of Wenchang and Confucius; the book gives no list, and the translation follows the letter. 'The rod or hanging from a beam' is being beaten to death or hanged. The 'Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning' are the gods of thunder and lightning, 'cellar and stove' are the underground store and the hearth, and 'the altars of the land and grain' are the gods of soil and grain.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.24 不必按五行硬指哪路神：金官指为关公岳公等武神不对，武神有封有未封各地不同；须问病人许愿未酬或污秽了哪座庙，把所疑的神占一卦，鬼爻旺或动就是此神，不上卦再占别处；得罪正神用香花纸马祭，招惹邪祟用浆纸钱夜静时送（金鬼向西、水鬼向北）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:古时候按五行分是哪路神，比如金官就说是天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公这些武神，觉子认为不对：武神有朝廷敕封的、有没有封的，各地风俗又不同，书里没记的更多，不可能一座庙一座庙地祭。该问病人是不是在哪座武神庙里许了愿没有还、或者在哪座庙里弄脏糟蹋了；凡是心里怀疑哪位神，就指着那位神占一卦，鬼爻旺或发动就是这位神，不上卦再占别处的神。鬼临木火水土的，都这样办。得罪了正神的，用香花纸马祭；招惹了邪祟的鬼，用浆水和纸钱，夜深人静时送走，金鬼向西送，水鬼向北送。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In ancient times the gods were told apart by the five phases, for example a Metal Pressure was said to be a heavenly general, Guan Gong, a Vajra, Wu Gong, Yue Gong and the other martial gods; Juezi thinks this wrong: martial gods include those enfeoffed by imperial decree and those not, local customs differ, and those not recorded in books are still more, so one cannot sacrifice at temple after temple. One should ask the patient whether he vowed something in the temple of some martial god and has not paid it, or defiled and abused some temple; whatever god he suspects in his heart, point to that god and divine, and if the Pressure line is strong or moving it is that god, and if it is not in the hexagram divine the god of some other place. Pressure on Wood, Fire, Water or Earth is treated in the same way. If he has offended an orthodox god, sacrifice with incense, flowers and paper horses; if he has provoked an evil haunting ghost, use rice-water and paper money and send it off in the stillness of the night, a Metal ghost to the west and a Water ghost to the north.

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13204 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13204*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 按，古以五行而分其神，如金官，谓之天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公，诸武神，余以为非也。 | 按：古时候按五行来分是哪路神，比如金官就说是天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公这些武神，我认为不对。 | Note: in ancient times the gods were told apart by the five phases, for example a Metal Pressure was said to be a heavenly general, Guan Gong, a Vajra, Wu Gong, Yue Gong and the other martial gods; I think this is wrong. |
| 夫武神，有敕封者，有未封者，有各方土俗之不同，书之未载者更多，岂能庙庙而祭之？ | 武神有朝廷敕封的，有没有封的，各地风俗又不一样，书里没有记载的更多，哪里能一座庙一座庙地祭过去？ | Martial gods include those enfeoffed by imperial decree and those not, and local customs differ from place to place, and those not recorded in books are still more; how could one sacrifice at temple after temple? |
| 然则奈何？ | 那么该怎么办呢？ | Then what is to be done? |
| 须问病人，或于某武神庙中许愿未酬乎？ | 要问病人，是不是在某位武神的庙里许过愿、还没有还？ | One must ask the patient whether he vowed something in the temple of some martial god and has not yet paid it. |
| 或于何庙污秽作践乎？ | 或者是不是在哪座庙里弄脏了、糟蹋了？ | Or whether he defiled or abused some temple. |
| 凡心有所疑者，则指其神而占之。 | 凡是心里有怀疑的，就指着那位神来占。 | Whatever god he suspects in his heart, point to that god and divine. |
| 鬼爻或旺或动，是此神也； | 鬼爻旺相或者发动，就是这位神； | If the Pressure line is strong or moving, it is that god; |
| 如不上卦，再占他处之神可耳。 | 如果不上卦，再占别处的神就行。 | if it is not in the hexagram, divine the god of some other place. |
| 其鬼值木火水土者，皆同此法。 | 鬼临木、火、水、土的，都照这个办法。 | Pressure on Wood, Fire, Water or Earth is treated in the same way. |
| 如既得罪于正神者，以香花纸马祭之； | 如果是得罪了正神，用香花纸马来祭； | If he has offended an orthodox god, sacrifice with incense, flowers and paper horses; |
| 勾惹邪祟之鬼者，用浆纸钱，夜静之时，金鬼者向西送之，水鬼者向北送之。 | 如果是招惹了邪祟的鬼，用浆水和纸钱，夜深人静的时候送走：金鬼向西送，水鬼向北送。 | if he has provoked an evil haunting ghost, use rice-water and paper money, and send it off in the stillness of the night: a Metal ghost to the west, a Water ghost to the north. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |

> **按**:觉子上一条（LY.ILL.ZS.23）先按五行分了大类，这一条说不必再把大类落实到某位具体的神：古人把金官指为关公岳飞等，他不同意，主张问病人心里疑的是哪位神，再占一卦验证。「金官」按字面是金爻的官鬼。「浆纸钱」是米浆水和纸钱。「香花纸马」是香、花和纸扎的马，祭正神用。
>
> **Note:** In the previous entry (LY.ILL.ZS.23) Juezi first told the great kinds apart by the five phases; this entry says the great kinds need not be pinned down to a particular god: he disagrees with the ancients pointing a Metal Pressure to Guan Gong, Yue Fei and the like, and holds that one should ask which god the patient suspects in his heart and then divine to test it. A 'Metal official' is in the letter a Metal Pressure line. 'Rice-water and paper money' is rice-water and paper money. 'Incense, flowers and paper horses' are incense, flowers and paper-made horses, used to sacrifice to orthodox gods.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.25 祭送不是万灵药：时灾瘟癖照法祭送屡验，疯痨气蛊是自己失调、与鬼神无关；野鹤见滇黔蜀粤病不服药专信鬼神、杀牲救命反害命；李我平驳《易冒》《易林补遗》指定关岳、说不祭降灾是邪神，并说此书疾病章言鬼神是从俗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:祭送鬼神，对时行灾病、瘟疫、癖积一类的病有用（野鹤多次验过）；疯、痨、气、蛊是自己没有调养好、酒色伤了身，和鬼神没有关系，祭送没有益处，该做的是节制饮食、远离色欲、平息怒气、养护精神。有病不吃药、专靠杀牲祭神的，反而害了性命。李我平批评有的书指定哪位神（关帝、岳王、关圣），又说祭了降福、不祭降灾，那是作威作福的邪神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Sacrificing and sending off the ghosts and spirits is of use for seasonal calamities, epidemics and hard lumps (the Old Man of Yehe verified it many times); madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly come from failing to look after oneself and from wine and lust damaging the body, have nothing to do with ghosts and spirits, and gain nothing from sacrifice, what is to be done being to moderate food, keep away from lust, calm anger and nourish the spirit. Those who take no medicine when ill and rely wholly on slaughtering animals to the gods only harm life. Li Woping criticises books that name a particular god (Emperor Guan, King Yue, the Sage Guan) and say that those who sacrifice are blessed and those who do not are sent calamity, which he calls evil gods who lord it and dispense favour.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13205 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13205*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：凡得时灾瘟癖，照此法而祭送者，余屡验之。 | 野鹤说：凡是得的是时行灾病、瘟疫、癖积一类的，照这个办法祭送，我多次验过。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: for illnesses that are seasonal calamities, epidemics or hard lumps, sacrifices and sending-off by this method have proved true many times. |
| 倘若是疯痨气蛊，既失调于前，酒色伤身，又失慎重于后，与鬼神何干？ | 如果是疯病、痨病、气病、蛊胀，先前没有调养好，酒色伤了身，后来又不谨慎，和鬼神有什么关系？ | But if it is madness, consumption, qi disorder or drum-belly, when he first failed to look after himself and wine and lust damaged his body, and then was again not careful, what has this to do with ghosts and spirits? |
| 祭之送之皆无益矣。 | 祭他、送他都没有用。 | Sacrificing to them and sending them off is of no use. |
| 惟节饱食、远色欲、息气养神，其病自减。 | 只有节制饮食、远离色欲、平息怒气、养护精神，病自然会减轻。 | Only by moderating food, keeping away from lust, calming anger and nourishing the spirit does the illness ease of itself. |
| 余游遍江湖，曾至滇黔蜀粤，见彼病不服药，专信鬼神。 | 我走遍江湖，到过云南、贵州、四川、广东，看到那里的人生病不吃药，专门相信鬼神。 | I have travelled all over the rivers and lakes, to Yunnan, Guizhou, Sichuan and Guangdong, and seen how there the sick take no medicine and put their whole trust in ghosts and spirits. |
| 先用三牲五牲，后至杀牛宰马，一次不愈，甚至二三十次。 | 先用三牲、五牲，后来杀牛宰马，一次不好，甚至做二三十次。 | First they use three or five kinds of sacrificial animals, then they slaughter oxen and horses, and if once does not cure, they go on even twenty or thirty times. |
| 因病致穷者多矣。 | 因为生病弄穷的人很多。 | Many are made poor by illness. |
| 殊不知害彼命而救命，其罪愈彰。 | 殊不知伤害别的生命来救自己的命，罪过更加明显。 | They do not realise that to harm other lives in order to save a life makes the sin all the plainer. |
| 所以屡见害性命之多者，终不能救而死，可不戒与？ | 所以屡次看到伤害的性命越多的人，最后还是救不了而死，难道不该引以为戒吗？ | So I have often seen that those who harmed the most lives could still not be saved and died in the end; should one not take warning? |
| 今下路读书人亦信此邪神者，余实不解。 | 现在下游一带的读书人也信这种邪神，我实在不明白。 | Now even scholars of the lower reaches believe in such evil gods, which I truly do not understand. |
| 神农尝百草，遗救生灵。 | 神农尝百草，是留给后世来救活生灵的。 | Shennong tasted the hundred herbs and left them to save living beings. |
| 未闻有何圣贤，教人宰牲命以救命也。 | 没听说有哪位圣贤，教人杀牲畜的命来救人的命。 | I have never heard of any sage who taught men to slaughter animals' lives in order to save a life. |

**李我平(楚江李坦,《增删卜易》的鉴定者)** — 《增删卜易》第 13207 行 · *Li Woping (Li Tan of Chujiang, who appraised the Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13207*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 李我平曰：《易冒》以鬼临金者为关帝、岳王。 | 李我平说：《易冒》把鬼临金的当作关帝、岳王。 | Li Woping says: the Yi Mao takes Pressure on Metal as Emperor Guan and King Yue. |
| 《易林补遗》又以青龙为关圣，却不知关岳以前，金官与青龙是何神也？ | 《易林补遗》又把青龙当作关圣，却不知道在关羽、岳飞以前，金官和青龙是什么神？ | The Yilin Buyi in turn takes the Azure Dragon as the Sage Guan, but does not ask what gods the Metal Pressure and the Azure Dragon were before Guan and Yue. |
| 又曰：祭者降之以福，不祭者降之以灾。 | 又说：祭的就降福给他，不祭的就降灾给他。 | It also says: to those who sacrifice they send blessing, to those who do not they send calamity. |
| 若此，乃作威福之邪神也，而谓正直之神，何得有此？ | 如果是这样，那是作威作福的邪神，而说是正直的神，哪里会有这种事？ | If so, they are evil gods who lord it and dispense favour, and how could a god called upright do this? |
| 悖甚。 | 荒谬极了。 | Utterly absurd. |
| 此书通前彻后，无一不合于理，惟疾病章中，亦言鬼神之事，何也？ | 这部书从头到尾，没有一处不合道理，只有疾病章里也讲鬼神的事，为什么呢？ | This book, from beginning to end, is in accord with reason everywhere, and only its chapter on illness also speaks of ghosts and spirits; why? |
| 疑因今人问病，开口先问鬼神，不得不从俗耳，知者察之。 | 我怀疑是因为现在的人问病，开口先问鬼神，不得不随俗罢了，明白的人自己去体察。 | I suspect it is because people now, in asking about illness, first ask about ghosts and spirits as soon as they open their mouths, so that the book has had to follow custom; let those who understand judge. |

> **打架**:要不要指定是哪位神：《易冒》以鬼临金为关帝、岳王，《易林补遗》以青龙为关圣（李我平点了这两部书）；李我平说这些说法不通，还说「祭者降福、不祭者降灾」是作威作福的邪神。野鹤对祭送的态度是：时灾瘟癖照法祭送灵验，疯痨气蛊与鬼神无关。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to name a particular god: the Yi Mao takes Pressure on Metal as Emperor Guan and King Yue, and the Yilin Buyi takes the Azure Dragon as the Sage Guan (Li Woping names the two books); Li Woping says this does not hold, and that 'blessing to those who sacrifice, calamity to those who do not' would make evil gods who lord it and dispense favour. The Old Man's attitude to sacrifice is that for seasonal calamities and epidemics sacrifice by the method proves true, and that madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly have nothing to do with ghosts and spirits.

> **按**:「时灾瘟癖」是当令的灾病、瘟疫和癖积（腹内结块的病）；「疯痨气蛊」是疯病、痨病（肺结核一类）、气病和蛊胀。「三牲」是牛、羊、猪，「五牲」是再加鸡、犬之类。「下路」在这里指长江下游一带。「乾按」是今人加的话，本库不引。
>
> **Note:** 'Seasonal calamity, epidemic and hard lump' are the calamities in season, pestilence and lumps forming in the belly; 'madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly' are madness, consumption (an illness of the lungs) and disorders of qi and of belly distension. 'Three sacrificial animals' are the ox, sheep and pig and 'five' add the fowl, dog and the like. 'Lower reaches' here means the lower Yangtze. The 'Qian note' is a modern addition and is not quoted here.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

---
